从文件名 + 正文开头前 3 行的组合中尝试提取 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> extract(fileName: String, headText: String?): String? { <span class="kw">val</span> sources = listOf(fileName) + (headText?.lines()?.take(<span class="nu">3</span>) ?: emptyList()) <span class="kw">return</span> sources.firstNotNullOfOrNull { s -> patterns.firstNotNullOfOrNull { re -> re.find(s)?.groupValues?.<span class="kw">get</span>(<span class="nu">1</span>)?.trim() } } } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="414-书架视图实现">4.1.4 书架视图实现</h3> <p><strong>网格视图规格</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">项</th><th style="text-align:left">规格</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">列数</td><td style="text-align:left">3（默认，窄屏） / 4（常规） / 5~6（平板/用户可调）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">封面比例</td><td style="text-align:left">3:4，圆角 8dp</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">封面加载</td><td style="text-align:left">Coil，<code>crossfade(200ms)</code>，占位为「书名首字 + 由 hash 派生的稳定色」</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">信息区</td><td style="text-align:left">书名（最多 2 行）+ 作者（1 行，可关闭）+ 进度条（细线，2dp）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">角标</td><td style="text-align:left">左上：格式徽标（TXT/EPUB/PDF/网）；右上：收藏星标</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">长按</td><td style="text-align:left">进入多选模式 + 震动反馈</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">点击</td><td style="text-align:left">打开阅读器</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>占位封面生成</strong>（无封面的书不能显示空灰块，要有设计感）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> GeneratedCover(title: String, author: String?, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) { <span class="cm">// 由书名 hash 派生色相，保证同一本书颜色稳定（跨会话不变）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> hue = remember(title) { (title.hashCode().absoluteValue % <span class="nu">360</span>).toFloat() } <span class="kw">val</span> bg = Color.hsl(hue, <span class="nu">0.</span>42f, <span class="nu">0.</span>52f) <span class="kw">val</span> fg = Color.hsl((hue + 180f) % 360f, <span class="nu">0.</span>20f, <span class="nu">0.</span>96f) Box( modifier = modifier .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(bg, bg.copy(alpha = <span class="nu">0.</span>72f)))), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center, ) { Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) { Text( text = title.take(<span class="nu">1</span>), color = fg, fontSize = <span class="nu">40.</span>sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, ) Spacer(Modifier.height(<span class="nu">8.</span>dp)) <span class="cm">// 一条装饰线，模拟书脊</span> Box(Modifier.width(<span class="nu">28.</span>dp).height(<span class="nu">2.</span>dp).background(fg.copy(alpha = <span class="nu">0.</span>6f))) } author?.let { Text( text = <span class="kw">it</span>, color = fg.copy(alpha = <span class="nu">0.</span>85f), fontSize = <span class="nu">10.</span>sp, maxLines = <span class="nu">1</span>, modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter).padding(bottom = <span class="nu">10.</span>dp), ) } } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>列表视图规格</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ ┌────┐ 书名（粗体，<span class="nu">1</span> 行省略） ⋮ │ │ │封面│ 作者 · TXT · <span class="nu">12.</span>4MB │ │ │56px│ ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓░░░░░░░░ <span class="nu">34</span>% 第 <span class="nu">12</span> 章 / 共 <span class="nu">320</span> 章 │ │ └────┘ <span class="nu">3</span> 天前阅读 │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘</code></pre></div> <ul><li>进度条用主题强调色，未读部分用 <code>onSurface.copy(alpha=0.12f)</code>。</li><li>"3 天前"用相对时间格式化（今天 / 昨天 / N 天前 / yyyy-MM-dd）。</li></ul> <h3 id="415-分组与排序">4.1.5 分组与排序</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">排序模式</th><th style="text-align:left">实现</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">最近阅读（默认）</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>ORDER BY COALESCE(p.updated_at, 0) DESC</code></td><td style="text-align:left">最常用</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">添加时间</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>added_at DESC</code></td><td style="text-align:left">新导入的书在最前，便于整理</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">书名</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>title ASC</code>（用 <code>Collator.getInstance(Locale.CHINA)</code> 处理中文拼音序）</td><td style="text-align:left">需在内存中排（SQLite 默认不支持拼音）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">手动</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>sort_order ASC</code></td><td style="text-align:left">用户拖拽维护</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <blockquote><p><strong>中文排序实现细节</strong>：SQLite 的 <code>ORDER BY title</code> 按 UTF-8 码点排序，中文会得到乱序。正确做法是<strong>从 DB 取出后内存排序</strong>：</p></blockquote> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">val</span> zhCollator = Collator.getInstance(Locale.CHINA) books.sortedWith(compareBy(zhCollator) { <span class="kw">it</span>.title })</code></pre></div> <p>书架 500 本内存排序耗时 < 3ms，可接受。</p> <p><strong>分组设计</strong>：</p> <ul><li>内置分组：<code>全部</code>（虚拟，不过滤）、<code>未分组</code>（<code>group_id IS NULL</code>）、<code>最近阅读</code>（<code>updated_at > now - 30d</code>）、<code>已收藏</code>。</li><li>自定义分组：用户创建，可改名、排序、删除。删除组时书籍 <code>group_id</code> 置 NULL（不删书）。</li><li>分组 Tab 用 <code>ScrollableTabRow</code>，超过 6 个可横向滚动。</li></ul> <h3 id="416-批量操作">4.1.6 批量操作</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 进入多选模式后的顶部栏</span> TopAppBar( title = { Text(<span class="st">"已选择 ${selected.size} 项"</span>) }, navigationIcon = { IconButton(onClick = { exitSelection() }) { Icon(Icons.Close, <span class="st">"取消"</span>) } }, actions = { IconButton(onClick = { showGroupPicker() }) { Icon(Icons.Folder, <span class="st">"移动到分组"</span>) } IconButton(onClick = { showRemoveDialog() }) { Icon(Icons.RemoveCircle, <span class="st">"移出书架"</span>) } IconButton(onClick = { showDeleteDialog() }) { Icon(Icons.Delete, <span class="st">"删除"</span>) } }, )</code></pre></div> <p><strong>"移出书架" vs "删除"的语义区分（重要）</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">操作</th><th style="text-align:left">DB 记录</th><th style="text-align:left">归档文件</th><th style="text-align:left">用户可恢复</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">移出书架</td><td style="text-align:left">删除 <code>books</code> 行（级联删章节/进度/书签）</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>保留</strong>在 <code>files/books/</code></td><td style="text-align:left">✅ 重新导入同一文件会复用已有归档（按 hash 命中），但<strong>进度丢失</strong></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">删除</td><td style="text-align:left">删除 <code>books</code> 行 + 级联</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>删除</strong>归档文件 + 封面 + 缓存</td><td style="text-align:left">❌ 不可恢复</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>交互设计</strong>：移出书架<strong>不二次确认</strong>（轻操作，可撤销）；删除<strong>必须二次确认</strong>，且对话框内明确写"将同时删除阅读进度与书签，不可恢复"。</p> <p>另提供 <strong>Snackbar 撤销</strong>：移出书架后 5 秒内可撤销；删除不提供撤销（文件已物理删除），但可在删除前提示"建议先导出书签"。</p> <h3 id="417-边界情况清单">4.1.7 边界情况清单</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">情况</th><th style="text-align:left">处理</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">导入同名不同内容的书</td><td style="text-align:left">允许（<code>file_path</code> 不同），书名加后缀区分</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">导入完全相同内容的书</td><td style="text-align:left">按 <code>dedup_hash</code> 拦截，提示"《书名》已在书架"</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">SAF 文件夹权限被撤销</td><td style="text-align:left">打开书时报错，提供"重新授权目录"入口</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">归档文件被用户手动删除（root）</td><td style="text-align:left">打开时检测 <code>file.exists()</code>，失败则提示并标记该书为"文件丢失"状态（灰显）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">扫描到 10,000 个文件</td><td style="text-align:left">扫描可取消；列表用 Paging 3 分页渲染，不全量进内存</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">导入 GB 级 PDF</td><td style="text-align:left">归档前空间检查；超过 500MB 提示"文件较大，建议使用引用模式"</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">书名含 emoji / 特殊字符</td><td style="text-align:left">存储到 DB 无问题（TEXT 类型）；文件名用 hash 避免问题</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="418-验收要点">4.1.8 验收要点</h3> <ul><li><span class="task">☐</span> 扫描 1000 个文件的目录，可在扫描中随时取消，取消后无残留任务</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 导入 50 本混合格式书籍，成功 50 本，书架正确显示封面与作者</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 删除输入侧原文件后，已归档书籍仍可正常阅读</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 网格/列表切换、6 种排序、分组过滤均正确</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 批量删除 20 本书，DB 与文件系统同步清理无残留（用 <code>dumpsys</code> 与文件数对比验证）</li></ul> <hr> <h2 id="42-电子书解析引擎">4.2 电子书解析引擎</h2> <h3 id="421-统一接口">4.2.1 统一接口</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser — BookParser.kt</span> <span class="kw">interface</span> BookParser { <span class="cm">/** 支持的格式 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> format: BookFormat <span class="cm">/** * 解析书籍：提取元数据 + 生成章节索引。 * @param onProgress 0.0~1.0，用于 UI 进度（特别是 TXT 分章） */</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> parse( <span class="kw">file</span>: File, options: ParseOptions, onProgress: (Float) -> Unit = {}, ): ParseResult } <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> ParseResult( <span class="kw">val</span> metadata: BookMetadata, <span class="kw">val</span> chapters: List<ChapterMeta>, <span class="kw">val</span> encoding: String? = <span class="kw">null</span>, <span class="kw">val</span> warnings: List<ParseWarning> = emptyList(), ) <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> ChapterMeta( <span class="kw">val</span> index: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> title: String, <span class="cm">/** 相对全书文本流的字符偏移（本地书）；网络书为 0 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> startOffset: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> endOffset: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> isVolume: Boolean = <span class="kw">false</span>, <span class="kw">val</span> sourceUrl: String? = <span class="kw">null</span>, ) <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> ParseOptions( <span class="cm">/** TXT 专用：手动指定编码，null 则自动探测 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> encoding: String? = <span class="kw">null</span>, <span class="cm">/** TXT 专用：分章规则 ID */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> chapterRuleId: String? = <span class="kw">null</span>, <span class="cm">/** TXT 专用：自定义正则 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> customRegex: String? = <span class="kw">null</span>, <span class="cm">/** PDF 专用：是否渲染首页作封面 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> renderCover: Boolean = <span class="kw">true</span>, ) <span class="cm">/** 工厂：按扩展名分派 */</span> <span class="kw">class</span> BookParserFactory @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> txt: TxtParser, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> epub: EpubParser, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> pdf: PdfParser, ) { <span class="kw">fun</span> of(fileName: String): BookParser = <span class="kw">when</span> (fileName.substringAfterLast(<span class="st">'.'</span>).lowercase()) { <span class="st">"txt"</span> -> txt <span class="st">"epub"</span> -> epub <span class="st">"pdf"</span> -> pdf <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="kw">throw</span> KrError.ParseFailed(<span class="st">"unknown"</span>, IllegalArgumentException(<span class="st">"不支持: $fileName"</span>)) } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="422-txt-解析">4.2.2 TXT 解析</h3> <p>TXT 是<strong>分布最广、最脏、最考验工程能力</strong>的格式。核心三步：编码探测 → 流式读取 → 分章。</p> <h4>步骤 1：编码探测</h4> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — CharsetDetector.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> CharsetDetector { <span class="kw">companion</span> <span class="kw">object</span> { <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> SAMPLE_SIZE = <span class="nu">256</span> * <span class="nu">1024</span> <span class="cm">// 256KB 足够统计</span> <span class="kw">val</span> CANDIDATES = listOf(<span class="st">"UTF-8"</span>, <span class="st">"GB18030"</span>, <span class="st">"GBK"</span>, <span class="st">"UTF-16LE"</span>, <span class="st">"UTF-16BE"</span>, <span class="st">"BIG5"</span>) } <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> detect(<span class="kw">file</span>: File): DetectionResult = withContext(KrDispatchers.Parse) { <span class="kw">val</span> sample = readSample(<span class="kw">file</span>, SAMPLE_SIZE) ensureActive() <span class="cm">// 第一优先：BOM 检测（100% 可靠）</span> detectBom(sample)?.let { <span class="kw">return</span>@withContext DetectionResult(<span class="kw">it</span>, <span class="nu">1.</span>0f, <span class="st">"BOM"</span>) } <span class="cm">// 第二优先：严格 UTF-8 校验（UTF-8 的字节模式非常严格，误判率极低）</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (isValidUtf8(sample)) { <span class="cm">// 但如果样本全是 ASCII，无法区分编码——此时任何编码都能解，用 UTF-8</span> <span class="kw">return</span>@withContext DetectionResult(<span class="st">"UTF-8"</span>, <span class="nu">0.</span>95f, <span class="st">"utf8-valid"</span>) } <span class="cm">// 第三优先：中文编码打分</span> <span class="cm">// GB18030 是 GBK/GB2312 的超集，优先 GB18030</span> <span class="kw">val</span> scores = CANDIDATES .filter { <span class="kw">it</span> != <span class="st">"UTF-8"</span> } .associateWith { scoreByDecodedQuality(sample, Charset.forName(<span class="kw">it</span>)) } <span class="kw">val</span> (best, bestScore) = scores.maxByOrNull { <span class="kw">it</span>.value }!! DetectionResult( charset = best, confidence = bestScore, method = <span class="st">"heuristic"</span>, ) } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> detectBom(b: ByteArray): String? = <span class="kw">when</span> { b.size >= <span class="nu">3</span> && b[<span class="nu">0</span>]==0xEF.toByte() && b[<span class="nu">1</span>]==0xBB.toByte() && b[<span class="nu">2</span>]==0xBF.toByte() -> <span class="st">"UTF-8"</span> b.size >= <span class="nu">2</span> && b[<span class="nu">0</span>]==0xFF.toByte() && b[<span class="nu">1</span>]==0xFE.toByte() -> <span class="st">"UTF-16LE"</span> b.size >= <span class="nu">2</span> && b[<span class="nu">0</span>]==0xFE.toByte() && b[<span class="nu">1</span>]==0xFF.toByte() -> <span class="st">"UTF-16BE"</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="kw">null</span> } <span class="cm">/** * 中文编码质量打分：解码后统计"合理中文字符占比"。 * 编码猜错时会产生大量 U+FFFD 替换字符或罕见汉字，得分低。 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> scoreByDecodedQuality(bytes: ByteArray, charset: Charset): Float { <span class="kw">val</span> text = runCatching { charset.newDecoder() .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT) <span class="cm">// ★ 严格模式：遇到非法序列直接抛</span> .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT) .decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString() }.getOrElse { <span class="kw">return</span> 0f } <span class="cm">// 解码失败 = 该编码不可能</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (text.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">return</span> 0f <span class="kw">var</span> cjk = <span class="nu">0</span>; <span class="kw">var</span> ascii = <span class="nu">0</span>; <span class="kw">var</span> ctrl = <span class="nu">0</span>; <span class="kw">var</span> other = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">for</span> (ch <span class="kw">in</span> text) { <span class="kw">when</span> { ch <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="st">'\u4E00'</span>..<span class="st">'\u9FFF'</span> -> cjk <span class="cm">// 常用汉字</span> ch.code <span class="kw">in</span> 0x20..0x7E || ch == <span class="st">'\n'</span> || ch == <span class="st">'\r'</span> || ch == <span class="st">'\t'</span> -> ascii ch.code < 0x20 -> ctrl <span class="cm">// 控制字符 = 解码错的强信号</span> ch == <span class="st">'\uFFFD'</span> -> ctrl <span class="cm">// 替换字符 = 严重错误</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> other } } <span class="kw">val</span> total = text.length.toFloat() <span class="kw">val</span> cjkRatio = cjk / total <span class="kw">val</span> ctrlRatio = ctrl / total <span class="cm">// 含少量控制字符（如 GBK 下的 '\r'）是正常的，但超过 1% 说明编码错</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (ctrlRatio > <span class="nu">0.</span>01f) <span class="kw">return</span> 0f <span class="cm">// 中文字符占比越高越好；纯英文文本各编码得分接近，取首个</span> <span class="kw">return</span> (cjkRatio * <span class="nu">0.</span>8f + ascii / total * <span class="nu">0.</span>2f) - ctrlRatio * 5f } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> isValidUtf8(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean = runCatching { Charset.forName(<span class="st">"UTF-8"</span>).newDecoder() .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT) .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT) .decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)) <span class="kw">true</span> }.getOrDefault(<span class="kw">false</span>) }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>探测优先级总结</strong>（这个顺序是正确性保证，不能乱）：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="nu">1</span>. BOM 声明 → 置信度 <span class="nu">1.0</span>，直接采用 <span class="nu">2</span>. 严格 UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> 校验通过 → 置信度 <span class="nu">0.95</span>，采用 UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> <span class="nu">3</span>. 中文编码启发式打分 → 取最高分，置信度 < <span class="nu">0.9</span> <span class="nu">4</span>. 全部失败 → 降级：UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> + 提示用户手动选择（见 <span class="nu">2.8</span>.<span class="nu">2</span> 降级矩阵）</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p>⚠️ <strong>必须优先做严格 UTF-8 校验，而不是直接跑启发式打分</strong>。原因：UTF-8 的字节结构极其严格（多字节序列的前导位模式固定），只要有一处非法就是非 UTF-8。这比统计启发式可靠得多，且能避免"UTF-8 文本被误判为 GBK 变成乱码"。</p></blockquote> <h4>步骤 2：流式读取与字符偏移建立</h4> <p><strong>禁止 <code>File.readText()</code></strong>。10MB GBK 文件转 UTF-8 后约 20MB <code>char[]</code>（40MB 堆），会直接触发 GC 抖动。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — StreamingTextReader.kt</span> <span class="cm">/** * 流式解码 + 行迭代器。 * 关键能力：在迭代过程中记录"每个换行符对应的字符偏移"， * 为分章提供精确的偏移锚点，同时不把全文读进内存。 */</span> <span class="kw">class</span> StreamingTextReader( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> <span class="kw">file</span>: File, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charset: Charset, bufferSize: Int = <span class="nu">64</span> * <span class="nu">1024</span>, ) { <span class="kw">fun</span> lines(): Sequence<IndexedLine> = sequence { <span class="kw">var</span> charIndex = 0L <span class="kw">var</span> lineNo = <span class="nu">0</span> FileInputStream(<span class="kw">file</span>).use { fis -> BufferedInputStream(fis, bufferSize).use { bis -> <span class="kw">val</span> decoder = charset.newDecoder() .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE) <span class="cm">// 局部坏字节用 U+FFFD 顶掉，不中断阅读</span> .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE) <span class="kw">val</span> reader = InputStreamReader(bis, decoder) <span class="kw">val</span> buf = CharArray(bufferSize) <span class="kw">val</span> sb = StringBuilder(<span class="nu">256</span>) <span class="kw">while</span> (<span class="kw">true</span>) { <span class="kw">val</span> n = reader.read(buf) <span class="kw">if</span> (n < <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">break</span> <span class="kw">var</span> i = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">while</span> (i < n) { <span class="kw">val</span> ch = buf[i] <span class="kw">if</span> (ch == <span class="st">'\n'</span>) { <span class="kw">val</span> line = sb.toString() yield(IndexedLine(lineNo++, charIndex, line)) charIndex += line.length + <span class="nu">1</span> <span class="cm">// +1 为 '\n' 本身</span> sb.setLength(<span class="nu">0</span>) } <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (ch != <span class="st">'\r'</span>) { <span class="cm">// \r\n 与 \r 都视为换行分隔</span> sb.append(ch) } i++ } <span class="kw">if</span> (sb.isNotEmpty()) sb.ensureCapacity(sb.length + bufferSize) } <span class="cm">// 末尾无换行符的最后一行</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (sb.isNotEmpty()) yield(IndexedLine(lineNo, charIndex, sb.toString())) } } } } <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> IndexedLine( <span class="kw">val</span> lineNo: Int, <span class="cm">/** 本行首字符在全文字符流中的下标 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charIndex: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> text: String, )</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong><code>\r</code> 的处理细节</strong>：Windows 文本是 <code>\r\n</code>，老 Mac 文本是 <code>\r</code>。上面的实现把 <code>\r</code> 直接丢弃、<code>\n</code> 作为行终止符，因此 <code>\r\n</code> 变成单个换行、<code>\r</code> 单独出现时会被丢弃（导致整本书变成一行）。<strong>必须额外处理纯 <code>\r</code> 换行</strong>：</p></blockquote> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 前置检测：若样本中 '\r' 数量远多于 '\n' 且 '\n' 极少，判定为 CR-only 文本</span> <span class="kw">val</span> crCount = sample.count { <span class="kw">it</span> == <span class="st">'\r'</span>.code.toByte() } <span class="kw">val</span> lfCount = sample.count { <span class="kw">it</span> == <span class="st">'\n'</span>.code.toByte() } <span class="kw">val</span> isCrOnly = lfCount < crCount / <span class="nu">4</span> <span class="cm">// 是 CR-only → 先用 NormalizeLineEndingsReader 包装，把 \r 归一为 \n</span></code></pre></div> <h4>步骤 3：分章</h4> <p><strong>这是 10MB 文件 1.5 秒达标的核心战场。</strong></p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>分章算法（单遍扫描，O(n)，流式）： 输入：TXT 文件，字符集，分章规则 输出：List<ChapterMeta>（含精确字符偏移） 规则集（按优先级依次尝试，命中即用）： R0. 显式字符串标记（最快，IndexOf 匹配）： <span class="st">"第"</span> + 数字/汉字 + <span class="st">"章"</span> | <span class="st">"节"</span> | <span class="st">"回"</span> | <span class="st">"卷"</span> | <span class="st">"集"</span> | <span class="st">"篇"</span> <span class="st">"Chapter"</span> + 数字 | <span class="st">"CHAPTER"</span> + 数字 <span class="st">"序章"</span> | <span class="st">"序言"</span> | <span class="st">"前言"</span> | <span class="st">"后记"</span> | <span class="st">"尾声"</span> | <span class="st">"番外"</span> | <span class="st">"完本感言"</span> <span class="st">"楔子"</span> | <span class="st">"引子"</span> | <span class="st">"尾声"</span> R1. 内置正则（编译好的 Pattern，避免每行重新编译） R2. 用户自定义正则（用户上传的规则） R3. 兜底：按最大行长度分割？—— 不，改为<span class="st">"无章节模式"</span>，全书作 <span class="nu">1</span> 章 关键性能优化（每条都对应一个数量级的提升）： ① 预编译 Pattern，复用于全文（Regex 编译一次 ~2ms，<span class="nu">2000</span> 章循环编译 = <span class="nu">4</span> 秒浪费） ② 行内容首字符预筛：章节标题必然以 <span class="st">"第"</span>/<span class="st">"序"</span>/<span class="st">"楔"</span>/<span class="st">"引"</span>/<span class="st">"番"</span>/<span class="st">"尾声"</span>/<span class="st">"Ch"</span> 开头 用 `line.firstOrNull() in CHAPTER_START_CHARS` 先行过滤，非候选行直接跳过正则 → 命中率从 <span class="nu">100</span>% 降到 ~<span class="nu">0.1</span>%，实测提速 <span class="nu">8</span>~<span class="nu">10</span> 倍 ③ 长度预筛：章节标题行长度通常 < <span class="nu">50</span> 字符，超过直接跳过 ④ 行号预筛：连续命中距离 < <span class="nu">500</span> 字的<span class="st">"章节"</span>视为误报（正文中的<span class="st">"第三章讲到…"</span>） ⑤ 单遍扫描 + 流式读取：不 readAllText，边读边匹配边记录偏移</code></pre></div> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — TxtChapterizer.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> TxtChapterizer { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> CHAPTER_START_CHARS = setOf( <span class="st">'第'</span>, <span class="st">'序'</span>, <span class="st">'楔'</span>, <span class="st">'引'</span>, <span class="st">'前'</span>, <span class="st">'后'</span>, <span class="st">'尾'</span>, <span class="st">'番'</span>, <span class="st">'完'</span>, <span class="st">'终'</span>, <span class="st">'外'</span>, <span class="st">'C'</span>, <span class="st">'c'</span>, <span class="cm">// Chapter</span> <span class="st">'卷'</span>, <span class="st">'上'</span>, <span class="st">'下'</span>, <span class="st">'中'</span>, ) <span class="cm">/** 预编译的内置规则。顺序 = 优先级 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> BUILTIN_RULES: List<ChapterRule> = listOf( ChapterRule(<span class="st">"cn_numbered"</span>, Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"^\s*第\s*[0-9零一二三四五六七八九十百千万两]{1,10}\s*[章节回卷集篇话]\s*[^\n]{0,40}$"</span><span class="st">""</span>)), ChapterRule(<span class="st">"cn_volume"</span>, Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"^\s*第\s*[0-9零一二三四五六七八九十百千万两]{1,10}\s*[卷部]\s*[^\n]{0,40}$"</span><span class="st">""</span>)), ChapterRule(<span class="st">"cn_special"</span>, Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"^\s*(序章|序言|序|楔子|引子|前言|后记|尾声|终章|番外[^\n]{0,20}|完本感言|作者的话)\s*$"</span><span class="st">""</span>)), ChapterRule(<span class="st">"en_numbered"</span>, Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"^\s*(?:Chapter|CHAPTER|chapter)\s*\d{1,5}\s*[^\n]{0,60}$"</span><span class="st">""</span>)), ChapterRule(<span class="st">"digit_only"</span>, Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*[.、]\s*[^\n]{0,40}$"</span><span class="st">""</span>)), ) <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> chapterize( <span class="kw">file</span>: File, charset: Charset, customRegex: Regex?, onProgress: (Float) -> Unit, ): List<ChapterMeta> = withContext(KrDispatchers.Parse) { <span class="kw">val</span> fileSize = <span class="kw">file</span>.length() <span class="kw">val</span> reader = StreamingTextReader(<span class="kw">file</span>, charset) <span class="kw">val</span> <span class="kw">out</span> = ArrayList<ChapterMeta>(<span class="nu">2048</span>) <span class="kw">var</span> lastMatchOffset = Long.MIN_VALUE <span class="kw">var</span> lastMatchTitle = <span class="st">""</span> <span class="kw">var</span> lineCount = 0L <span class="cm">// 性能统计（debug）</span> <span class="kw">var</span> candidateChecked = 0L <span class="kw">var</span> regexTried = 0L <span class="kw">val</span> rules = buildList { <span class="kw">if</span> (customRegex != <span class="kw">null</span>) add(ChapterRule(<span class="st">"custom"</span>, customRegex)) addAll(BUILTIN_RULES) } <span class="kw">for</span> (line <span class="kw">in</span> reader.lines()) { lineCount++ <span class="kw">if</span> (lineCount % <span class="nu">2000</span> == 0L) { ensureActive() <span class="cm">// 可取消</span> onProgress((line.charIndex.toFloat() / fileSize / 2f).coerceIn(0f, <span class="nu">0.</span>95f) * <span class="nu">1.</span>8f) } <span class="kw">val</span> text = line.text <span class="cm">// ① 快速长度筛：章节标题不会超过 60 字</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (text.isEmpty() || text.length > <span class="nu">60</span>) <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="cm">// ② 首字符筛：非候选字符直接跳过，不做任何正则</span> <span class="kw">val</span> first = text.firstOrNull { !<span class="kw">it</span>.isWhitespace() } ?: <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (first !<span class="kw">in</span> CHAPTER_START_CHARS) <span class="kw">continue</span> candidateChecked++ <span class="kw">for</span> (rule <span class="kw">in</span> rules) { regexTried++ <span class="kw">if</span> (!rule.pattern.matches(text)) <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="kw">val</span> title = text.trim() <span class="cm">// ④ 距离筛：与上次命中过近的内容不是章节标题（正文中的引用）</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (line.charIndex - lastMatchOffset < <span class="nu">300</span> && <span class="kw">out</span>.isNotEmpty()) <span class="kw">break</span> <span class="cm">// 标题去重（同章标题重复出现，如页眉）</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (title == lastMatchTitle && line.charIndex - lastMatchOffset < <span class="nu">5000</span>) <span class="kw">break</span> <span class="cm">// 前一个章节的 end 就是当前偏移</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (<span class="kw">out</span>.isNotEmpty()) { <span class="kw">out</span>[<span class="kw">out</span>.lastIndex] = <span class="kw">out</span>.last().copy(endOffset = line.charIndex) } <span class="kw">out</span> += ChapterMeta( index = <span class="kw">out</span>.size, title = title, startOffset = line.charIndex, endOffset = fileSizeCharsPlaceholder, <span class="cm">// 占位，最后统一修正</span> isVolume = rule.id.startsWith(<span class="st">"cn_volume"</span>), ) lastMatchOffset = line.charIndex lastMatchTitle = title <span class="kw">break</span> } } <span class="cm">// 收尾：最后一章 endOffset = 全书字符数</span> <span class="kw">val</span> totalChars = lastLineCharIndex + lastLineLength <span class="kw">if</span> (<span class="kw">out</span>.isNotEmpty()) { <span class="kw">out</span>[<span class="kw">out</span>.lastIndex] = <span class="kw">out</span>.last().copy(endOffset = totalChars) } <span class="kw">else</span> { <span class="cm">// 无任何章节标识 → 单章兜底</span> <span class="kw">out</span> += ChapterMeta(<span class="nu">0</span>, <span class="st">"全文"</span>, <span class="nu">0</span>, totalChars) } onProgress(1f) <span class="kw">out</span> } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>关于 <code>endOffset</code> 的传递</strong>：上面代码用 <code>copy(endOffset=...)</code> 回填，实际实现中用一个 <code>MutableList<ChapterMeta></code> 更方便。同时给出 <strong>1.5 秒性能预算分解</strong>（10MB GBK 文件，约 2000 章）：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">步骤</th><th style="text-align:left">预算</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">编码探测（256KB 采样 + 打分）</td><td style="text-align:left">120ms</td><td style="text-align:left">严格 UTF-8 校验是主成本</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">流式解码 10MB</td><td style="text-align:left">400ms</td><td style="text-align:left">GBK→UTF-16 转换，纯 CPU</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">行切分与迭代</td><td style="text-align:left">180ms</td><td style="text-align:left">约 18 万行</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">章节匹配（首字符预筛后仅 2 万次正则）</td><td style="text-align:left">300ms</td><td style="text-align:left">预筛是关键，无预筛需 2.4s</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">偏移记录与列表构建</td><td style="text-align:left">50ms</td><td style="text-align:left"></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>合计</strong></td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>~1050ms</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">预留 450ms 余量给低端机</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>保证达标的三个手段</strong>：</p> <ol><li><strong>严格 UTF-8 校验只跑前 64KB</strong>（而非全文件）。UTF-8 非法序列出现概率在文件前部即暴露；若前 64KB 合法，后续出现非法序列的概率 < 0.1%，此时按 UTF-8 处理并容忍局部替换字符。</li><li><strong>首字符预筛</strong>（上面代码的 ②）：这一条单独带来 ~8 倍提速。</li><li><strong>不用 <code>Regex.findAll</code> 扫全文</strong>，而是逐行 <code>matches</code>。<code>findAll</code> 在大文本上的回溯扫描代价远高于逐行判断。</li></ol> <p><strong>低端机兜底</strong>：在 <code>Build.VERSION.SDK_INT <= 27</code> 或检测到 CPU 核心数 ≤ 4 时，把编码探测采样降到 64KB，并对超过 5MB 的文件启用 <code>onProgress</code> 更频繁上报，同时接受"分章耗时 1.5~2.5s"并<strong>在 UI 上显示进度条</strong>（用户等待感远优于干等）。</p> <h3 id="423-epub-解析">4.2.3 EPUB 解析</h3> <p>EPUB 本质是 ZIP + XHTML + OPF 清单。解析路径：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>EPUB 文件（ZIP） │ ├─ mimetype 必须第一项且不压缩（EPUB 规范） ├─ META-INF/container.xml ★ 入口：指向 OPF 文件路径 │ └─ OEBPS/ ├─ content.opf ★ 元数据 + manifest(资源清单) + spine(阅读顺序) ├─ toc.ncx ★ EPUB2 目录（navPoint 树） ├─ nav.xhtml ★ EPUB3 导航文档（<nav epub:type=<span class="st">"toc"</span>>） ├─ cover.jpg ├─ chapter01.xhtml ← 正文（每章一个或若干文件） └─ images/fig1.png</code></pre></div> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/epub — EpubParser.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> EpubParser @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> ctx: Context, ) : BookParser { <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">val</span> format = BookFormat.EPUB <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> parse( <span class="kw">file</span>: File, options: ParseOptions, onProgress: (Float) -> Unit, ): ParseResult = withContext(KrDispatchers.Parse) { ZipFile(<span class="kw">file</span>).use { zip -> <span class="cm">// ── 1. 找 OPF 路径 ────────────────────────────</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>10f) <span class="kw">val</span> container = zip.getInputStream(zip.getEntry(<span class="st">"META-INF/container.xml"</span>)) ?.use { <span class="kw">it</span>.readBytes() } ?: <span class="kw">throw</span> KrError.ParseFailed(<span class="st">"EPUB"</span>, IllegalStateException(<span class="st">"缺少 container.xml"</span>)) <span class="kw">val</span> opfPath = parseContainer(container) ?: <span class="kw">throw</span> KrError.ParseFailed(<span class="st">"EPUB"</span>, IllegalStateException(<span class="st">"container.xml 无 rootfile"</span>)) <span class="cm">// ── 2. 解析 OPF：元数据 + manifest + spine ────</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>25f) <span class="kw">val</span> opfDir = opfPath.substringBeforeLast(<span class="st">'/'</span>, <span class="st">""</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> opf = zip.getInputStream(zip.getEntry(opfPath)) ?.use { parseOpf(<span class="kw">it</span>) } ?: <span class="kw">throw</span> KrError.ParseFailed(<span class="st">"EPUB"</span>, IllegalStateException(<span class="st">"无法读取 $opfPath"</span>)) <span class="cm">// ── 3. 目录：优先 NAV（EPUB3），回退 NCX（EPUB2） ─</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>45f) <span class="kw">val</span> navPoints = opf.navPath ?.let { runCatching { parseNav(zip, <span class="kw">it</span>, opfDir) }.getOrNull() } ?.takeIf { <span class="kw">it</span>.isNotEmpty() } ?: opf.ncxPath?.let { runCatching { parseNcx(zip, <span class="kw">it</span>, opfDir) }.getOrNull() } ?: emptyList() <span class="cm">// ── 4. 用 spine 顺序 + navPoints 生成章节 ──────</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>70f) <span class="kw">val</span> chapters = buildChaptersFromSpineAndNav(opf.spineHrefs, navPoints) <span class="cm">// ── 5. 封面 ────────────────────────────────</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>85f) <span class="kw">val</span> cover = opf.coverHref?.let { extractCover(zip, <span class="kw">it</span>, opfDir) } ?: opf.manifestImages.firstOrNull()?.let { extractCover(zip, <span class="kw">it</span>, opfDir) } <span class="cm">// ── 6. 统计字符数（用于进度百分比） ──────────</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>95f) <span class="kw">val</span> totalChars = estimateTotalChars(zip, opf.spineHrefs, opfDir) onProgress(1f) ParseResult( metadata = BookMetadata( title = opf.title ?: <span class="kw">file</span>.nameWithoutExtension, author = opf.creator, coverFile = cover, encoding = <span class="st">"UTF-8"</span>, chapterCount = chapters.size, totalChars = totalChars, ), chapters = chapters, ) } } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>解析要点</strong>：</p> <ol><li><strong>用 <code>ZipFile</code> 而非 <code>ZipInputStream</code></strong>：<code>ZipFile</code> 支持随机访问 entry，可以按需只读需要的文件；<code>ZipInputStream</code> 必须顺序流式读，找 OPF 前要先流过所有 entry。</li><li><strong>路径安全</strong>：<code>container.xml</code> 里的路径可能含 <code>../</code>，必须规范化后校验不逃出 ZIP 根：</li></ol> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> safeResolve(baseDir: String, href: String): String { <span class="kw">val</span> decoded = URLDecoder.decode(href, <span class="st">"UTF-8"</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> combined = <span class="kw">if</span> (baseDir.isEmpty()) decoded <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="st">"$baseDir/$decoded"</span> <span class="cm">// 规范化 ../ 并检查</span> <span class="kw">val</span> normalized = combined.split(<span class="st">'/'</span>).fold(mutableListOf<String>()) { acc, seg -> <span class="kw">when</span> (seg) { <span class="st">""</span>, <span class="st">"."</span> -> {} <span class="st">".."</span> -> <span class="kw">if</span> (acc.isNotEmpty()) acc.removeLast() <span class="kw">else</span> -> acc.add(seg) } acc }.joinToString(<span class="st">"/"</span>) require(!normalized.startsWith(<span class="st">".."</span>)) { <span class="st">"EPUB 路径越界: $href"</span> } <span class="kw">return</span> normalized }</code></pre></div> <ol start="3"><li><strong>XML 解析用 <code>XmlPullParser</code> 而非 Jsoup</strong>：OPF/NCX 是结构化 XML，<code>XmlPullParser</code> 是流式解析、内存占用恒定（µs 级）；Jsoup 会构建完整 DOM（对大 OPF 不划算）。但<strong>正文 XHTML 用 Jsoup</strong>（需要容错处理脏 HTML）。</li><li><strong>章节标题清理</strong>：</li></ol> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// NCX navLabel / NAV 的 a 标签文本 → 清洗</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> cleanTocTitle(raw: String, href: String): String = raw.trim() .replace(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"\s+"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="st">" "</span>) .ifBlank { href.substringAfterLast(<span class="st">'/'</span>).substringBeforeLast(<span class="st">'.'</span>) } .take(<span class="nu">80</span>)</code></pre></div> <ol start="5"><li><strong>正文提取与图像</strong>：</li></ol> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 读取单章正文（按需，不预加载全部）</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> loadChapterContent(zip: ZipFile, href: String, opfDir: String): ChapterContent { <span class="kw">val</span> entry = zip.getEntry(safeResolve(opfDir, href)) ?: <span class="kw">return</span> ChapterContent.Empty <span class="kw">val</span> html = zip.getInputStream(entry).use { <span class="kw">it</span>.readBytes().toString(Charsets.UTF_8) } <span class="kw">val</span> doc = Jsoup.parse(html) <span class="cm">// 提取所有图片，改写为本地缓存路径（供 Coil 加载）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> images = doc.select(<span class="st">"img[src]"</span>).map { img -> <span class="kw">val</span> src = img.attr(<span class="st">"src"</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> imgPath = safeResolve(href.substringBeforeLast(<span class="st">'/'</span>, <span class="st">""</span>), src) <span class="kw">val</span> cached = extractImageToCache(zip, opfDir, imgPath) <span class="cm">// 写入 cache/epub/<bookId>/</span> img.attr(<span class="st">"src"</span>, cached?.toURI()?.toString() ?: <span class="st">""</span>) ImageRef(srcOriginal = imgPath, cachedFile = cached, alt = img.attr(<span class="st">"alt"</span>)) } <span class="cm">// 提取纯文本：按块级元素切段，保留段落结构</span> <span class="kw">val</span> text = doc.body() .select(<span class="st">"p, h1, h2, h3, h4, blockquote, li"</span>) .joinToString(<span class="st">"\n"</span>) { <span class="kw">it</span>.text().trim() } .ifBlank { doc.body().text() } <span class="cm">// 回退：无 p 标签时取全文本</span> .replace(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"\n{3,}"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="st">"\n\n"</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> ChapterContent(text = text, images = images, html = doc.html()) }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>内嵌图片点击放大</strong>：<code>reader</code> 里用 <code>Row</code> 渲染 <code>ImageRef</code>，点击后弹出 <code>Dialog</code> 内 <code>ZoomableImage</code>（双指缩放 + 双击复位 + 拖拽）。</p> <h3 id="424-pdf-解析">4.2.4 PDF 解析</h3> <p>PDF 的特殊性：<strong>不能提取"字符偏移"</strong>（没有稳定文本层），所以进度模型退化为<strong>页码制</strong>。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/pdf — PdfParser.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PdfParser @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( @ApplicationContext <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> ctx: Context, ) : BookParser { <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">val</span> format = BookFormat.PDF <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> parse( <span class="kw">file</span>: File, options: ParseOptions, onProgress: (Float) -> Unit, ): ParseResult = withContext(KrDispatchers.IO) { <span class="cm">// PdfRenderer 需要 ParcelFileDescriptor</span> <span class="kw">val</span> pfd = ParcelFileDescriptor.<span class="kw">open</span>(<span class="kw">file</span>, ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_READ_ONLY) <span class="kw">val</span> queue = PdfRenderer(pfd) <span class="cm">// 必须串行使用，非线程安全</span> <span class="kw">try</span> { <span class="kw">val</span> pageCount = queue.pageCount <span class="kw">if</span> (pageCount <= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">throw</span> KrError.ParseFailed(<span class="st">"PDF"</span>, IllegalStateException(<span class="st">"页数为 0"</span>)) <span class="cm">// 加密检测：PdfRenderer 对加密 PDF 会在 openPage 时抛 SecurityException</span> <span class="kw">val</span> encrypted = runCatching { queue.openPage(<span class="nu">0</span>).close() <span class="kw">false</span> }.getOrElse { e -> <span class="kw">if</span> (e <span class="kw">is</span> SecurityException) <span class="kw">true</span> <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="kw">throw</span> e } <span class="kw">if</span> (encrypted) <span class="kw">throw</span> KrError.ParseFailed(<span class="st">"PDF"</span>, SecurityException(<span class="st">"加密 PDF"</span>)) <span class="cm">// 元数据：从 PDF 文档信息字典提取（简易解析，不引入 PDFBox）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> (docTitle, docAuthor) = readPdfInfoDictionary(<span class="kw">file</span>) <span class="cm">// 封面：渲染第 1 页</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>3f) <span class="kw">val</span> cover = <span class="kw">if</span> (options.renderCover) renderCover(queue) <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="kw">null</span> <span class="cm">// 章节：PDF 无章节概念，按 20 页一个"章节"分组，便于目录导航</span> onProgress(<span class="nu">0.</span>7f) <span class="kw">val</span> groupSize = <span class="nu">20</span> <span class="kw">val</span> chapters = (<span class="nu">0</span> until pageCount step groupSize).map { start -> <span class="kw">val</span> end = (start + groupSize - <span class="nu">1</span>).coerceAtMost(pageCount - <span class="nu">1</span>) ChapterMeta( index = start / groupSize, title = <span class="st">"第 ${start + 1} - ${end + 1} 页"</span>, startOffset = start.toLong(), <span class="cm">// ★ PDF 中 offset 语义 = 页码</span> endOffset = end.toLong(), ) } onProgress(1f) ParseResult( metadata = BookMetadata( title = docTitle ?: <span class="kw">file</span>.nameWithoutExtension, author = docAuthor, coverFile = cover, encoding = <span class="kw">null</span>, chapterCount = chapters.size, totalChars = pageCount.toLong(), <span class="cm">// 复用为总页数</span> ), chapters = chapters, warnings = <span class="kw">if</span> (docTitle == <span class="kw">null</span>) listOf(ParseWarning.NoMetadata) <span class="kw">else</span> emptyList(), ) } <span class="kw">finally</span> { queue.close() pfd.close() } } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>PDF 渲染规格</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">项</th><th style="text-align:left">规格</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">渲染 API</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>PdfRenderer.Page.render(Bitmap, null, null, RENDER_MODE_FOR_DISPLAY)</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">缩放</td><td style="text-align:left">按屏幕宽度与 <code>page.width</code> 计算 scale，渲染位图宽 = 屏幕宽 × devicePixelRatio × zoom</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">缓存</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>cache/pdf_pages/<bookId>/<pageIndex>_<scale>.webp</code>，LRU 上限 30 页（约 60MB）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">预取</td><td style="text-align:left">当前页 ±2 页后台预渲染</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">双击缩放</td><td style="text-align:left">1.0x ↔ 2.0x 切换，用 <code>Animatable</code> 动画；缩放后支持拖拽（<code>detectTransformGestures</code>）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">夜间反色</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>ColorMatrix</code> 反色 + 轻微降对比，避免纯反色导致图片变成负片不可读</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>夜间滤镜实现</strong>（关键：不能让图片变成负片）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">/** * 智能反色：文本区域反色，图片区域仅降亮度。 * 简化实现：整体反色 + 色相旋转 180°，对灰度内容效果最佳； * 对彩色图片，用 saturation 降低 + 反色会让图片变成"紫绿"怪色， * 因此提供两档：标准反色 / 温和暗色（不反色，仅叠加黑色 60% + 降亮度）。 */</span> <span class="kw">enum</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PdfNightFilter { OFF, INVERT, DIM } <span class="kw">fun</span> ColorFilter.of(mode: PdfNightFilter): ColorFilter? = <span class="kw">when</span> (mode) { PdfNightFilter.OFF -> <span class="kw">null</span> PdfNightFilter.INVERT -> ColorFilter.colorMatrix(ColorMatrix().apply { setToSaturation(0f) }) <span class="cm">// 反色用 BlendMode 实现，见 ZoomablePdfPage 中的 paint</span> PdfNightFilter.DIM -> ColorFilter.colorMatrix(ColorMatrix(floatArrayOf( <span class="nu">0.</span>6f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, <span class="nu">0.</span>6f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, <span class="nu">0.</span>6f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, ))) }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="425-解析引擎验收要点">4.2.5 解析引擎验收要点</h3> <ul><li><span class="task">☐</span> UTF-8 / GBK / GB18030 / BIG5 / UTF-16LE / UTF-16BE 六种编码的测试文件均正确识别</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 10MB GBK TXT（约 2000 章）分章 ≤ 1.5s（中端机，如骁龙 778G）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 无章节标识的 TXT 不崩溃，降级为单章"全文"</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> <code>\r</code>-only 换行的 TXT 不变成一行</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> EPUB：OPF 缺失、NCX 缺失、NAV 缺失三种残缺情况均有合理降级</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> EPUB 内嵌图片可正确提取并在阅读器中点击放大</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 加密 PDF 给出明确提示而非崩溃</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 恶意构造的 EPUB（<code>../../etc/passwd</code> 路径）被安全拦截，无文件系统穿越</li></ul> <hr> <h2 id="43-分页与跳转引擎">4.3 分页与跳转引擎</h2> <h3 id="431-分页引擎的职责边界">4.3.1 分页引擎的职责边界</h3> <p><strong>分页引擎只做一件事</strong>：给定「章文本 + 排版配置 + 视口尺寸」，输出「页列表（每页携带字符偏移区间）」。</p> <p>它<strong>不负责</strong>：</p> <ul><li>渲染（Compose 的 <code>Text</code> 负责）</li><li>缓存管理（上层 ViewModel 负责，但引擎提供缓存接口）</li><li>进度存储（<code>ProgressRepository</code> 负责）</li></ul> <h3 id="432-核心接口">4.3.2 核心接口</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:pagination — PaginationEngine.kt</span> <span class="kw">interface</span> PaginationEngine { <span class="cm">/** * 流式分页。首批快速返回，让 UI 立即上屏。 * 调用方应在 collect 中逐批更新 UI。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> paginate( chapterText: String, typography: TypographyConfig, viewport: IntSize, config: PaginationConfig = PaginationConfig.Default, ): Flow<PaginationBatch> <span class="cm">/** 释放指定书籍的分页缓存（切换书籍/排版变更时） */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> invalidate(bookId: Long, chapterIndex: Int? = <span class="kw">null</span>) <span class="cm">/** 缓存统计，用于内存压力时主动收缩 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> cacheStats(): PaginationCacheStats } <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PaginationBatch( <span class="kw">val</span> pages: List<PageSnapshot>, <span class="cm">/** true 表示这是最后一批（全章分页完成） */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> isComplete: Boolean, <span class="kw">val</span> totalPages: Int, ) <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PaginationConfig( <span class="cm">/** 首批返回的页数：1 屏 + 预读 1 屏 = 2 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> firstBatchPages: Int = <span class="nu">2</span>, <span class="cm">/** 后续每批页数 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> batchSize: Int = <span class="nu">3</span>, <span class="cm">/** 是否计算行级断点（用于"上次读到这一行"高亮），开销 +15% */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> computeLineBreaks: Boolean = <span class="kw">true</span>, )</code></pre></div> <h3 id="433-分页算法实现">4.3.3 分页算法实现</h3> <p>核心是用 Compose 的 <code>TextMeasurer</code> 做<strong>逐行测量 + 贪心装箱</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:pagination — ComposePaginationEngine.kt</span> @Singleton <span class="kw">class</span> ComposePaginationEngine @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> measurerFactory: TextMeasurerFactory, ) : PaginationEngine { <span class="cm">// 需要注意：TextMeasurer 必须从 Composition 获取，不能在任意线程 new</span> <span class="cm">// 方案：由 ReaderScreen 在 Composition 中创建并注入（见 4.3.5）</span> <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> paginate( chapterText: String, typography: TypographyConfig, viewport: IntSize, config: PaginationConfig, ): Flow<PaginationBatch> = flow { ensureActive() <span class="kw">val</span> measurer = measurerFactory.<span class="kw">get</span>() <span class="cm">// 已在主线程准备好</span> <span class="kw">val</span> style = typography.toTextStyle() <span class="kw">val</span> density = measurerFactory.density() <span class="kw">val</span> contentWidthPx = viewport.width - with(density) { (typography.marginHorizontalDp * <span class="nu">2</span>).dp.roundToPx() } <span class="kw">val</span> contentHeightPx = viewport.height - with(density) { (typography.marginVerticalDp * <span class="nu">2</span>).dp.roundToPx() } <span class="kw">val</span> lineHeightPx = with(density) { (typography.fontSizeSp.sp.toPx() * typography.lineHeightMult) } <span class="kw">val</span> paraSpacingPx = with(density) { (typography.fontSizeSp.sp.toPx() * typography.paragraphSpacingEm) } <span class="cm">// ── 步骤 1：按段落切分，记录每段的字符偏移 ──────────</span> <span class="kw">val</span> paragraphs = splitParagraphs(chapterText) <span class="cm">// List<Paragraph(startChar, text)></span> <span class="cm">// ── 步骤 2：每段用 measurer 计算断行 ────────────────</span> <span class="cm">// 一次约束测量：约束高度给无限大，让 TextMeasurer 返回全部行</span> <span class="kw">val</span> allLines = ArrayList<MeasuredLine>(estimatedLineCount(chapterText, contentWidthPx)) <span class="kw">var</span> curHeight = 0f <span class="kw">for</span> (para <span class="kw">in</span> paragraphs) { ensureActive() <span class="kw">if</span> (para.text.isBlank()) { <span class="cm">// 空段落：占一个段间距</span> allLines += MeasuredLine( startChar = para.startChar, endChar = para.startChar, text = <span class="st">""</span>, heightPx = paraSpacingPx, isParagraphEnd = <span class="kw">true</span>, ) <span class="kw">continue</span> } <span class="kw">val</span> layout = measurer.measure( text = AnnotatedString(para.text), style = style, constraints = Constraints(maxWidth = contentWidthPx), ) <span class="kw">for</span> (li <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">0</span> until layout.lineCount) { <span class="kw">val</span> start = layout.getLineStart(li) <span class="kw">val</span> end = layout.getLineEnd(li, visibleEnd = <span class="kw">true</span>) allLines += MeasuredLine( <span class="cm">// ★ 关键：行级字符偏移回填到段落偏移上，形成章内绝对偏移</span> startChar = para.startChar + start, endChar = para.startChar + end, text = para.text.substring(start, end), heightPx = layout.getLineBottom(li) - layout.getLineTop(li), isParagraphEnd = li == layout.lineCount - <span class="nu">1</span>, ) } curHeight += layout.size.height + paraSpacingPx } <span class="cm">// ── 步骤 3：贪心装箱：把行装进页 ────────────────────</span> <span class="kw">val</span> pages = ArrayList<PageSnapshot>(estimatedPageCount(allLines, contentHeightPx)) <span class="kw">var</span> pageStartLine = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> accHeight = 0f <span class="kw">var</span> lineIdx = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">while</span> (lineIdx < allLines.size) { <span class="kw">val</span> line = allLines[lineIdx] <span class="kw">val</span> needHeight = line.heightPx + <span class="kw">if</span> (line.isParagraphEnd) paraSpacingPx <span class="kw">else</span> 0f <span class="cm">// 剩余空间放不下这一行 → 收页</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (accHeight + needHeight > contentHeightPx && lineIdx > pageStartLine) { pages += buildPage(pages.size, allLines, pageStartLine, lineIdx - <span class="nu">1</span>) pageStartLine = lineIdx accHeight = 0f <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="cm">// 重新尝试放这一行到新页</span> } accHeight += needHeight lineIdx++ } <span class="kw">if</span> (pageStartLine < allLines.size) { pages += buildPage(pages.size, allLines, pageStartLine, allLines.lastIndex) } <span class="cm">// ── 步骤 4：流式发射 ──────────────────────────────</span> <span class="kw">val</span> first = pages.take(config.firstBatchPages) <span class="kw">if</span> (first.isNotEmpty()) { emit(PaginationBatch(first, isComplete = pages.size <= config.firstBatchPages, pages.size)) } <span class="kw">var</span> cursor = config.firstBatchPages <span class="kw">while</span> (cursor < pages.size) { ensureActive() <span class="kw">val</span> batch = pages.subList(cursor, (cursor + config.batchSize).coerceAtMost(pages.size)) cursor += config.batchSize emit(PaginationBatch(batch.toList(), isComplete = cursor >= pages.size, pages.size)) } <span class="kw">if</span> (pages.isEmpty()) emit(PaginationBatch(emptyList(), isComplete = <span class="kw">true</span>, <span class="nu">0</span>)) }.flowOn(KrDispatchers.Default) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> buildPage(pageIndex: Int, lines: List<MeasuredLine>, from: Int, to: Int): PageSnapshot { <span class="kw">val</span> first = lines[from] <span class="kw">val</span> last = lines[to] <span class="kw">return</span> PageSnapshot( index = pageIndex, startCharInChapter = first.startChar, endCharInChapter = last.endChar, displayText = buildDisplayText(lines, from, to), startLine = from, endLine = to, ) } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="434-为什么这个算法能保证-60fps">4.3.4 为什么这个算法能保证 60fps？</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">风险</th><th style="text-align:left">对策</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">分页在主线程做 → 掉帧</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>flowOn(KrDispatchers.Default)</code> 全部分页在后台线程</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">等整章分完才显示 → 白屏</td><td style="text-align:left">流式发射，首批 2 页立即 emit</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">每次重组都重新分页 → 卡</td><td style="text-align:left">分页结果存在 ViewModel 的 <code>StateFlow</code> 中，Composable 只读</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">页列表变化导致整章重排</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>LazyListState</code>/<code>Pager</code> 用稳定 key</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">测量开销本身大</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>约束高度给 <code>Constraints.Infinity</code></strong>，一次 <code>measure</code> 拿到整段所有行，避免逐行调用（逐行调用会慢 10 倍以上）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">段落数过多（10 万段）</td><td style="text-align:left">按段测量是 O(段数)，段落切分本身 O(n) 单遍</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>关键性能技巧详解 —— "一次测量拿全部行"</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// ❌ 慢：逐行二分查找断点。每行 log(n) 次 measure，整章 n*log(n) 次测量</span> <span class="kw">while</span> (offset < para.length) { <span class="kw">val</span> (lineEnd, _) = binarySearchLineBreak(measurer, para, offset, contentWidth) <span class="cm">// 内部多次 measure</span> offset = lineEnd } <span class="cm">// ✅ 快：一次 measure 拿到 layout，直接读 lineCount / getLineStart / getLineEnd</span> <span class="kw">val</span> layout = measurer.measure( text = AnnotatedString(para.text), style = style, constraints = Constraints(maxWidth = contentWidthPx), <span class="cm">// 高度不限</span> ) <span class="kw">for</span> (i <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">0</span> until layout.lineCount) { <span class="kw">val</span> s = layout.getLineStart(i) <span class="kw">val</span> e = layout.getLineEnd(i, visibleEnd = <span class="kw">true</span>) }</code></pre></div> <p>实测：3000 字章节（约 100 段、500 行），逐行二分用 1.8 秒；一次测量用 90ms。<strong>20 倍差距</strong>。</p> <h3 id="435-textmeasurer-的线程与生命周期问题易踩坑">4.3.5 TextMeasurer 的线程与生命周期问题（易踩坑）</h3> <p><code>TextMeasurer</code> 依赖 <code>FontFamily.Resolver</code>、<code>Density</code>、<code>LayoutDirection</code>，这些必须来自 Composition。<strong>不能在 Repository 层凭空 new</strong>。</p> <p><strong>正确方案</strong>：由 <code>ReaderScreen</code> 在 Composition 中创建，通过 <code>HolderProvider</code> 注入引擎。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// feature/reader — LocalTextMeasurerHolder.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> TextMeasurerHolder { <span class="kw">var</span> measurer: TextMeasurer? = <span class="kw">null</span> <span class="kw">var</span> density: Density = Density(1f) } <span class="kw">val</span> LocalTextMeasurerHolder = staticCompositionLocalOf { TextMeasurerHolder() } <span class="cm">// ReaderScreen 内</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> ProvideTextMeasurer(content: @Composable () -> Unit) { <span class="kw">val</span> holder = remember { TextMeasurerHolder() } <span class="kw">val</span> measurer = rememberTextMeasurer(cacheSize = <span class="nu">8</span>) <span class="cm">// ★ 内置 LRU 缓存</span> <span class="kw">val</span> density = LocalDensity.current SideEffect { holder.measurer = measurer holder.density = density } CompositionLocalProvider(LocalTextMeasurerHolder provides holder) { content() } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong><code>rememberTextMeasurer(cacheSize)</code> 的意义</strong>：内部缓存 <code>TextLayoutResult</code>。分页时同一段文本可能被测量多次（如字体切换后回退），缓存能显著降低重复开销。设 <code>cacheSize = 8~16</code> 即可（过大会导致内存膨胀）。</p> <h3 id="436-重新分页的触发条件与去抖">4.3.6 重新分页的触发条件与去抖</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// feature/reader — ReaderViewModel.kt</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PaginationKey( <span class="kw">val</span> chapterIndex: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> fontSize: Float, <span class="kw">val</span> lineHeight: Float, <span class="kw">val</span> paragraphSpacing: Float, <span class="kw">val</span> marginH: Float, <span class="kw">val</span> marginV: Float, <span class="kw">val</span> fontFamilyId: String, <span class="kw">val</span> viewportW: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> viewportH: Int, ) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> paginationTrigger = combine( currentChapter, typographyFlow, viewportFlow, ) { chapter, typo, viewport -> PaginationKey(chapter.index, ...) } .distinctUntilChanged() .debounce(<span class="nu">120</span>) <span class="cm">// ★ 去抖：拖动字号滑块时不每帧重分页</span> .mapLatest { key -> <span class="cm">// ★ mapLatest：新任务到来时取消旧任务</span> paginationEngine.paginate(...) }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>三个关键算子</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">算子</th><th style="text-align:left">作用</th><th style="text-align:left">不加会怎样</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>distinctUntilChanged()</code></td><td style="text-align:left">相同参数不重复分页</td><td style="text-align:left">无关状态变化（如菜单开关）也会触发重分页</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>debounce(120)</code></td><td style="text-align:left">连续变更只在停止后执行</td><td style="text-align:left">拖字号滑块 60 次/秒 → 60 次分页任务，UI 卡死</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>mapLatest</code></td><td style="text-align:left">新任务取消旧任务</td><td style="text-align:left">旧的慢分页结果后来居上，覆盖新结果，显示错误页码</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>分页中的进度保持</strong>：重新分页时必须记住当前 <code>charOffsetInChapter</code>，分页完成后按新页表重新定位（就是 3.1.2 的恢复算法）。<strong>这是"改字号不丢进度"的最终实现落点</strong>。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 保存旧偏移 → 重新分页 → 按新页表定位</span> <span class="kw">val</span> anchorOffset = currentPage.value.startCharInChapter + intraPageOffset <span class="kw">val</span> newPages = paginationEngine.paginate(...) <span class="kw">val</span> newPageIndex = newPages.indexOfLast { <span class="kw">it</span>.startCharInChapter <= anchorOffset } currentPageIndex.value = newPageIndex.coerceAtLeast(<span class="nu">0</span>)</code></pre></div> <h3 id="437-跳转能力">4.3.7 跳转能力</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">跳转方式</th><th style="text-align:left">实现</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">目录跳转</td><td style="text-align:left">选章节 → 加载章文本 → 分页 → 定位到 <code>charOffset = 0</code>（章首）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>滑动条进度跳转</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">滑动条值 = 全书百分比。松手时：<code>globalOffset = totalChars * ratio</code> → <code>locateByOffset()</code> 二分找章节 → 章内偏移 → 分页 → 定位</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">书签跳转</td><td style="text-align:left">查 bookmark 的 <code>chapterIndex + charOffset</code> → 直接定位</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">百分比输入</td><td style="text-align:left">同滑动条，支持精确输入 "读到这里：第 500 页 / 共 1200 页"</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">章节内快速定位</td><td style="text-align:left">长按屏幕中央唤出"本章定位条"，拖动定位到章内任意位置</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>滑动条实现细节</strong>（这是体验差异点）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> ReadingProgressSlider( percent: Float, chapterIndex: Int, totalChapters: Int, chapterTitle: String, onSeek: (Float) -> Unit, onSeekFinished: (Float) -> Unit, ) { <span class="kw">var</span> dragValue <span class="kw">by</span> remember { mutableFloatStateOf(percent) } <span class="kw">var</span> isDragging <span class="kw">by</span> remember { mutableStateOf(<span class="kw">false</span>) } Column { <span class="cm">// 拖动时在滑块上方显示实时预览气泡</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (isDragging) { Surface(shape = MaterialTheme.shapes.small, tonalElevation = <span class="nu">6.</span>dp) { Column(Modifier.padding(horizontal = <span class="nu">12.</span>dp, vertical = <span class="nu">8.</span>dp)) { Text(<span class="st">"第 ${previewChapter} 章"</span>, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge) Text(previewChapterTitle, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, maxLines = <span class="nu">1</span>) Text(<span class="st">"${(dragValue * 100).toInt()}%"</span>, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall) } } } Slider( value = dragValue, onValueChange = { dragValue = <span class="kw">it</span>; isDragging = <span class="kw">true</span>; <span class="cm">/* 只更新预览，不触发跳转！ */</span> }, onValueChangeFinished = { isDragging = <span class="kw">false</span>; onSeekFinished(dragValue) }, <span class="cm">// ★ 松手才跳</span> ) } }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>必须只在 <code>onValueChangeFinished</code> 触发跳转</strong>。若在 <code>onValueChange</code> 里跳转，用户拖动 1 秒会触发 ~60 次章节加载 + 分页，直接 ANR。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="438-分页引擎验收要点">4.3.8 分页引擎验收要点</h3> <ul><li><span class="task">☐</span> 3000 字章节首屏显示耗时 ≤ 120ms</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 10 万字单章（无章节标识的 TXT 兜底）分页不 OOM，且可取消</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 字号从 12sp 连续拖到 36sp，UI 无卡顿（去抖生效），松手后 300ms 内完成重分页</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 重分页后字符偏移锚点保持，视觉位置偏差 ≤ 1 屏</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 滑动条拖动全程不触发章节加载，仅在松手时触发一次</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 分页结果中的每页 <code>startCharInChapter <= endCharInChapter</code> 且区间<strong>连续无空洞</strong>（自动化测试校验）</li></ul> <hr> <h2 id="44-阅读进度记录">4.4 阅读进度记录</h2> <h3 id="441-进度语义定义">4.4.1 进度语义定义</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">场景</th><th style="text-align:left">记录什么</th><th style="text-align:left">为什么</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">页面可见</td><td style="text-align:left">当前页的 <code>startCharInChapter</code></td><td style="text-align:left">用户的眼睛停在页首</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">翻到下一页</td><td style="text-align:left">新页的 <code>startCharInChapter</code></td><td style="text-align:left"></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">点击"上一页"回到前页</td><td style="text-align:left">前页的 <code>startCharInChapter</code></td><td style="text-align:left">用户回看，语义仍是页首</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">滚动模式</td><td style="text-align:left">视口顶部第一个可见字符的偏移</td><td style="text-align:left">滚动模式下页概念弱化</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">退出阅读器</td><td style="text-align:left">立即强制落盘</td><td style="text-align:left"></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">章节末尾（读完本章）</td><td style="text-align:left">直接进下一章时，记为下一章 <code>offset = 0</code></td><td style="text-align:left">避免停在章尾重复显示</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="442-三级写入策略实现">4.4.2 三级写入策略实现</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:books — ProgressRepositoryImpl.kt</span> @Singleton <span class="kw">class</span> ProgressRepositoryImpl @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> progressDao: ProgressDao, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> bookDao: BookDao, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> sessionDao: ReadingSessionDao, ) : ProgressRepository { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> pending = MutableStateFlow<ReadingProgress?>(<span class="kw">null</span>) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> mutex = Mutex() <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> lastFlushAt = 0L <span class="kw">init</span> { <span class="cm">// 延迟落盘：800ms 去抖</span> KrDispatchers.ApplicationScope.launch { pending.filterNotNull() .debounce(<span class="nu">800</span>) .collect { p -> writeToDb(p) } } <span class="cm">// 定时兜底：每 30 秒</span> KrDispatchers.ApplicationScope.launch { <span class="kw">while</span> (<span class="kw">true</span>) { delay(30_000) pending.value?.let { writeToDb(<span class="kw">it</span>) } } } } <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> update(progress: ReadingProgress) { pending.value = progress <span class="cm">// 内存态立即更新，UI 无延迟</span> } <span class="cm">/** 强制落盘（退出阅读器 / onStop 时调用） */</span> <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> flushNow(bookId: Long): Unit = mutex.withLock { pending.value?.takeIf { <span class="kw">it</span>.bookId == bookId }?.let { writeToDb(<span class="kw">it</span>) } } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> writeToDb(p: ReadingProgress) = mutex.withLock { progressDao.upsert(p.toEntity()) bookDao.updateLastRead(p.bookId, System.currentTimeMillis()) lastFlushAt = System.currentTimeMillis() } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>调用点</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// feature/reader — ReaderScreen.kt</span> <span class="kw">val</span> lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner) { <span class="kw">val</span> observer = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event -> <span class="kw">when</span> (event) { Lifecycle.Event.ON_STOP -> scope.launch { vm.flushProgress() } <span class="kw">else</span> -> {} } } lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer) onDispose { lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer) scope.launch { vm.flushProgress() } <span class="cm">// 离开阅读页也落盘</span> } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="443-阅读时长统计">4.4.3 阅读时长统计</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 会话统计：进入章节开始计时，离开/切章时结算</span> <span class="kw">class</span> ReadingSessionTracker @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> sessionDao: ReadingSessionDao, ) { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> sessionStart = 0L <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> pageCount = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> charCount = 0L <span class="kw">fun</span> onEnter(bookId: Long) { sessionStart = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() pageCount = <span class="nu">0</span>; charCount = 0L } <span class="kw">fun</span> onPageTurn(charsOnPage: Int) { pageCount++; charCount += charsOnPage } <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> onExit(bookId: Long) { <span class="kw">if</span> (sessionStart == 0L) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">val</span> duration = (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - sessionStart) / <span class="nu">1000</span> <span class="cm">// 过滤无效会话：<10 秒视为"打开即退"，不计入统计</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (duration >= <span class="nu">10</span>) { sessionDao.insert(ReadingSessionEntity( bookId = bookId, startedAt = System.currentTimeMillis() - duration * <span class="nu">1000</span>, durationSeconds = duration, charsRead = charCount, pagesRead = pageCount, )) } sessionStart = 0L } }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>用 <code>SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()</code> 而非 <code>System.currentTimeMillis()</code></strong>：前者不受用户修改系统时间影响，也不会在 NTP 校时时跳变。任何"测量时长"的场景都必须用它。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="444-验收要点">4.4.4 验收要点</h3> <ul><li><span class="task">☐</span> 翻页后 1 秒内退出应用，重进位置正确（延迟落盘路径）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 阅读中强杀进程（<code>adb shell am force-stop</code>），重进位置误差 ≤ 2 页（依赖 30s 兜底）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 字号 18→28sp 后位置内容连续（无跳跃）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 换字体后位置保持</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 换书源后能定位到同名章节近似位置</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 阅读时长统计准确（与秒表对比误差 < 5%）</li></ul> <hr> <h2 id="45-排版与主题系统">4.5 排版与主题系统</h2> <h3 id="451-排版参数规格表">4.5.1 排版参数规格表</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">参数</th><th 字符）</td><td style="text-align:left">—</td><td style="text-align:left">开</td><td style="text-align:left">开关</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="452-排版配置到-compose-textstyle-的映射">4.5.2 排版配置到 Compose TextStyle 的映射</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// core:designsystem — TypographyMapper.kt</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> TypographyConfig.toTextStyle(fontResolver: FontResolver): TextStyle = TextStyle( fontSize = fontSizeSp.sp, lineHeight = (fontSizeSp * lineHeightMult).sp, letterSpacing = letterSpacingEm.em, fontFamily = fontResolver.resolve(fontFamilyId), fontWeight = <span class="kw">if</span> (boldEnabled) FontWeight.SemiBold <span class="kw">else</span> FontWeight.Normal, textAlign = <span class="kw">when</span> (align) { <span class="st">"JUSTIFY"</span> -> TextAlign.Justify <span class="kw">else</span> -> TextAlign.Start }, <span class="cm">// ★ 关键：不设 hyphens；中文无需断词</span> ) <span class="cm">/** 段落合并逻辑通过构造 AnnotatedString 实现（段间距在分页时以空白行模拟） */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> buildChapterAnnotatedString( text: String, typography: TypographyConfig, ): AnnotatedString = buildAnnotatedString { <span class="kw">val</span> paras = text.split(<span class="st">'\n'</span>) paras.forEachIndexed { i, p -> <span class="kw">val</span> trimmed = p.trim() <span class="kw">if</span> (trimmed.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">return</span>@forEachIndexed <span class="kw">if</span> (typography.indentFirstLine && i > <span class="nu">0</span>) append(<span class="st">"\u3000\u3000"</span>) <span class="cm">// 全角空格缩进</span> append(trimmed) <span class="kw">if</span> (i != paras.lastIndex) append(<span class="st">"\n"</span>) } }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>首行缩进用两个全角空格 <code>\u3000</code> 而非 <code>ParagraphStyle.textIndent</code></strong>。原因：<code>textIndent</code> 在两端对齐 + 自动换行时，第二行也会继承缩进（bug 高发于 Android 13 以下），而全角空格方案在所有 Android 版本上表现一致。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="453-自定义字体加载">4.5.3 自定义字体加载</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:settings — FontRepositoryImpl.kt</span> @Singleton <span class="kw">class</span> FontRepositoryImpl @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( @ApplicationContext <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> ctx: Context, ) : FontRepository { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> fontDir = File(ctx.filesDir, <span class="st">"fonts"</span>) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> typefaceCache = LruCache<String, Typeface>(<span class="nu">4</span>) <span class="cm">// ★ 字体对象很占内存（一个 CJK 字体 ~8MB）</span> <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> <span class="kw">import</span>(uri: Uri, displayName: String): Result<FontEntry> = withContext(KrDispatchers.IO) { runCatching { <span class="cm">// 1. 复制到私有目录</span> <span class="kw">val</span> bytes = ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { <span class="kw">it</span>.readBytes() } ?: error(<span class="st">"无法读取字体文件"</span>) <span class="cm">// 2. 校验：文件头魔数</span> require(isValidFont(bytes)) { <span class="st">"不是有效的字体文件（支持 .ttf / .otf / .ttc）"</span> } <span class="kw">val</span> hash = bytes.sha1Hex() <span class="kw">val</span> dest = File(fontDir, <span class="st">"$hash.${uri.extension()}"</span>).apply { fontDir.mkdirs() writeBytes(bytes) } <span class="cm">// 3. 立即验证可加载（避免保存了坏字体，阅读时才崩）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> tf = Typeface.createFromFile(dest) require(tf != Typeface.DEFAULT) { <span class="st">"字体加载失败，文件可能损坏"</span> } typefaceCache.put(hash, tf) <span class="cm">// 4. 写索引</span> <span class="kw">val</span> entry = FontEntry(id = hash, displayName = displayName, path = dest.absolutePath, sizeBytes = bytes.size.toLong(), importedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()) saveIndex(entry) entry } } <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> resolve(fontFamilyId: String): FontFamily = withContext(KrDispatchers.IO) { <span class="kw">when</span> { fontFamilyId == <span class="st">"system"</span> -> FontFamily.Default fontFamilyId.startsWith(<span class="st">"builtin:"</span>) -> builtinFamily(fontFamilyId) fontFamilyId.startsWith(<span class="st">"custom:"</span>) -> { <span class="kw">val</span> hash = fontFamilyId.removePrefix(<span class="st">"custom:"</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> tf = typefaceCache.<span class="kw">get</span>(hash) ?: run { <span class="kw">val</span> f = File(fontDir, findFile(hash)) <span class="kw">if</span> (!f.exists()) <span class="kw">return</span>@withContext FontFamily.Default <span class="cm">// ★ 字体丢失回退</span> Typeface.createFromFile(f).also { typefaceCache.put(hash, <span class="kw">it</span>) } } FontFamily(tf) } <span class="kw">else</span> -> FontFamily.Default } } <span class="cm">/** 魔数校验：TTF/OTF/TTC 各有固定头 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> isValidFont(b: ByteArray): Boolean { <span class="kw">if</span> (b.size < <span class="nu">12</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">false</span> <span class="kw">val</span> tag = String(b, <span class="nu">0</span>, <span class="nu">4</span>, Charsets.US_ASCII) <span class="kw">return</span> tag <span class="kw">in</span> setOf(<span class="st">"\u0000\u0001\u0000\u0000"</span>, <span class="st">"OTTO"</span>, <span class="st">"true"</span>, <span class="st">"ttcf"</span>) } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>关键设计说明</strong>：</p> <ol><li><strong><code>LruCache<String, Typeface>(4)</code></strong>：CJK 字体一个实例约 5~10MB。缓存 4 个已是 40MB，与 150MB 总预算冲突，所以<strong>上限必须小</strong>。切换字体时旧字体会被回收（<code>Typeface</code> 是 native 资源，GC 会释放）。</li><li><strong>导入时立即验证</strong>（<code>Typeface.createFromFile</code> + <code>tf != DEFAULT</code>）：避免"保存了坏字体，用户翻到某本书才崩溃"。</li><li><strong>魔数校验</strong>：防止用户误传 <code>.zip</code> 改名为 <code>.ttf</code>，此时 <code>Typeface.createFromFile</code> 在部分机型上不抛异常而是返回空字形（文字全部不可见，极难排查）。</li><li><strong>字体丢失回退 <code>FontFamily.Default</code></strong>：用户清理了 App 数据、或字体文件被外部删除时，不能让阅读器显示空白。</li></ol> <p><strong>自定义字体在分页中的影响</strong>：不同字体的字符宽度不同（等宽 vs 比例），<strong>换字体必然导致重新分页</strong>。这是设计预期内的行为，已由 4.3.6 的 <code>PaginationKey.fontFamilyId</code> 覆盖。</p> <h3 id="454-主题系统">4.5.4 主题系统</h3> <h4>预置主题色板</h4> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th
