<code>high_contrast</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>#FFFFFF</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>#000000</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>#0000CC</code></td><td style="text-align:left">无障碍 / 阳光下</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">自定义 <code>custom</code></td><td style="text-align:left">用户 HEX</td><td style="text-align:left">用户 HEX</td><td style="text-align:left">自动派生</td><td style="text-align:left"></td></tr></tbody></table></div> <blockquote><p><strong>为什么夜间模式正文不用纯白 <code>#FFFFFF</code>？</strong> 纯白文字在纯黑背景上会产生"光晕扩散"效应（halation），尤其对散光用户，反而更难读。<code>#B8B8B8</code> ~ <code>#C5C5C5</code> 是经验最优区间。</p></blockquote> <h4>自定义主题的对比度保护</h4> <p>用户可能配出"浅灰底 + 浅灰字"这种不可读组合。<strong>必须做对比度校验</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// core:designsystem — ContrastGuard.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> ContrastGuard { <span class="cm">/** WCAG 相对亮度 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> luminance(c: Color): Double { <span class="kw">fun</span> ch(v: Float): Double { <span class="kw">val</span> s = v.toDouble() <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (s <= <span class="nu">0.03928</span>) s / <span class="nu">12.92</span> <span class="kw">else</span> ((s + <span class="nu">0.055</span>) / <span class="nu">1.055</span>).pow(<span class="nu">2.4</span>) } <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="nu">0.2126</span> * ch(c.red) + <span class="nu">0.7152</span> * ch(c.green) + <span class="nu">0.0722</span> * ch(c.blue) } <span class="cm">/** 对比度（WCAG 定义） */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> contrastRatio(fg: Color, bg: Color): Double { <span class="kw">val</span> l1 = luminance(fg); <span class="kw">val</span> l2 = luminance(bg) <span class="kw">val</span> (hi, lo) = <span class="kw">if</span> (l1 > l2) l1 to l2 <span class="kw">else</span> l2 to l1 <span class="kw">return</span> (hi + <span class="nu">0.05</span>) / (lo + <span class="nu">0.05</span>) } <span class="cm">/** * 校验并修正。 * 正文对比度应 ≥ 7:1（AAA 级）——长文阅读要求高于一般的 4.5:1。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> validate(fg: Color, bg: Color): ContrastResult { <span class="kw">val</span> ratio = contrastRatio(fg, bg) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">when</span> { ratio >= <span class="nu">7.0</span> -> ContrastResult.Ok(ratio) ratio >= <span class="nu">4.5</span> -> ContrastResult.Acceptable(ratio) <span class="cm">// 允许，但次级 UI 文字需注意</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> ContrastResult.TooLow(ratio, suggestFix(fg, bg)) } } <span class="cm">/** 保持色相，向黑或白方向调整亮度直到达标 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> suggestFix(fg: Color, bg: Color): Color { <span class="kw">val</span> bgLum = luminance(bg) <span class="kw">val</span> targetWhite = bgLum < <span class="nu">0.5</span> <span class="cm">// 深色背景 → 调亮文字</span> <span class="kw">var</span> hsl = fg.toHsl() <span class="kw">val</span> step = <span class="kw">if</span> (targetWhite) <span class="nu">0.</span>03f <span class="kw">else</span> -<span class="nu">0.</span>03f <span class="kw">var</span> candidate = fg repeat(<span class="nu">30</span>) { hsl = hsl.copy(lightness = (hsl.lightness + step).coerceIn(0f, 1f)) candidate = Color.hsl(hsl.hue, hsl.saturation, hsl.lightness) <span class="kw">if</span> (contrastRatio(candidate, bg) >= <span class="nu">7.0</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> candidate } <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (targetWhite) Color.White <span class="kw">else</span> Color.Black } } <span class="kw">sealed</span> <span class="kw">interface</span> ContrastResult { <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Ok(<span class="kw">val</span> ratio: Double) : ContrastResult <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Acceptable(<span class="kw">val</span> ratio: Double) : ContrastResult <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> TooLow(<span class="kw">val</span> ratio: Double, <span class="kw">val</span> suggestion: Color) : ContrastResult }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>UI 提示</strong>：自定义主题面板实时显示对比度数值，低于 4.5 时给出黄色警告条 + "一键修正"按钮。</p> <h4>亮度调节</h4> <p>两套机制并存：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">机制</th><th style="text-align:left">实现</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">应用内亮度遮罩</td><td style="text-align:left">全屏 <code>Box</code> 叠加 <code>Color.Black.copy(alpha = 1f - brightness)</code></td><td style="text-align:left">不改系统亮度，不影响其他应用。用于"比系统更暗"</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">系统亮度覆写</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>window.attributes.screenBrightness = value</code></td><td style="text-align:left">用户可选"跟随系统"或"独立亮度"</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>默认策略</strong>：只用遮罩（更安全，不需要任何权限）。用户手动开启"独立亮度"时才覆写窗口属性。</p> <hr> <h2 id="46-阅读交互与翻页动效">4.6 阅读交互与翻页动效</h2> <h3 id="461-五种翻页模式">4.6.1 五种翻页模式</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">模式</th><th style="text-align:left">交互</th><th style="text-align:left">实现方案</th><th style="text-align:left">帧率要求</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>仿真翻页</strong> <code>SIMULATION</code></td><td style="text-align:left">从屏幕边缘拖拽，纸张卷曲翻起</td><td style="text-align:left">自定义卷曲：<code>Canvas</code> 绘制 + 贝塞尔曲线阴影；简化为"斜切 + 阴影渐变"</td><td style="text-align:left">60fps+</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>覆盖翻页</strong> <code>COVER</code></td><td style="text-align:left">新页从右侧滑入覆盖旧页</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>HorizontalPager</code> + 双页叠加，<code>graphicsLayer</code> 平移</td><td style="text-align:left">60fps</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>平移滑动</strong> <code>SLIDE</code></td><td style="text-align:left">新旧页并排平移</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>HorizontalPager</code> 原生</td><td style="text-align:left">90/120fps ✅</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>无缝滚动</strong> <code>SCROLL</code></td><td style="text-align:left">垂直连续滚动</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>LazyColumn</code> 页化（每页一项）或连续 <code>Text</code></td><td style="text-align:left">120fps ✅</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>点按切换</strong> <code>TAP</code></td><td style="text-align:left">无动画瞬时切换</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>Crossfade</code> 或直接换页</td><td style="text-align:left">无动画压力</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="462-翻页动画实现覆盖模式为例">4.6.2 翻页动画实现（覆盖模式为例）</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// feature/reader — CoverPageTurn.kt</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> CoverPageTurn( currentPage: PageSnapshot, nextPage: PageSnapshot?, prevPage: PageSnapshot?, typography: TypographyConfig, theme: ReaderTheme, onTurn: (Direction) -> Unit, ) { <span class="kw">val</span> scope = rememberCoroutineScope() <span class="kw">val</span> offsetFraction = remember { Animatable(0f) } <span class="cm">// -1(上一页) ~ 0(当前) ~ 1(下一页)</span> <span class="kw">val</span> threshold = <span class="nu">0.</span>28f Box( Modifier .fillMaxSize() .pointerInput(Unit) { detectHorizontalDragGestures( onDragEnd = { scope.launch { <span class="kw">val</span> target = <span class="kw">when</span> { offsetFraction.value > threshold -> 1f offsetFraction.value < -threshold -> -1f <span class="kw">else</span> -> 0f } offsetFraction.animateTo( target = target, animationSpec = tween( durationMillis = (<span class="nu">120</span> + <span class="nu">180</span> * abs(target - offsetFraction.value)).toInt(), easing = FastOutSlowInEasing, ), ) <span class="kw">if</span> (target != 0f) { onTurn(<span class="kw">if</span> (target > <span class="nu">0</span>) Direction.NEXT <span class="kw">else</span> Direction.PREV) offsetFraction.snapTo(0f) <span class="cm">// ★ 立即复位，避免闪回</span> } } }, onDragCancel = { scope.launch { offsetFraction.animateTo(0f) } }, ) { change, dragAmount -> change.consume() scope.launch { <span class="cm">// 1:1 跟手，超界时阻尼衰减</span> <span class="kw">val</span> delta = dragAmount / size.width <span class="kw">val</span> next = offsetFraction.value + delta offsetFraction.snapTo( <span class="kw">if</span> (next > 1f) 1f + (next - 1f) * <span class="nu">0.</span>15f <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (next < -1f) -1f + (next + 1f) * <span class="nu">0.</span>15f <span class="kw">else</span> next ) } } }, ) { <span class="cm">// 当前页（随手指左移）</span> PageContent( page = currentPage, typography = typography, theme = theme, modifier = Modifier.graphicsLayer { translationX = -offsetFraction.value * size.width alpha = 1f - (offsetFraction.value * <span class="nu">0.</span>85f).coerceIn(0f, 1f) }, ) <span class="cm">// 下一页（从右侧滑入）</span> nextPage?.let { np -> PageContent( page = np, typography = typography, theme = theme, modifier = Modifier.graphicsLayer { translationX = (1f - offsetFraction.value) * size.width }, ) } <span class="cm">// 上一页（从左侧滑入，仅在向右拖时可见）</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (offsetFraction.value < 0f) { prevPage?.let { pp -> PageContent( page = pp, typography = typography, theme = theme, modifier = Modifier.graphicsLayer { translationX = (-1f - offsetFraction.value) * size.width }, ) } } } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>性能关键点</strong>：</p> <ol><li><strong>动画值用 <code>Animatable</code> 而非每次重组 <code>mutableStateOf</code></strong>：<code>Animatable</code> 走 <code>graphicsLayer</code> 的绘制阶段，<strong>不触发重组</strong>。</li><li><strong><code>graphicsLayer { }</code> 的 lambda 形式</strong>：读 <code>Animatable.value</code> 发生在绘制阶段（<code>Layer</code> 更新），避免 Composition 阶段的失效。</li><li><strong><code>snapTo(0f)</code> 在 <code>onTurn</code> 之后</strong>：先让 ViewModel 切换到新页，再复位偏移，否则会闪一下旧页。</li><li><strong>越界阻尼 0.15f</strong>：模拟真实物理边界感。</li><li><strong><code>change.consume()</code></strong>：防止手势冒泡到父容器。</li></ol> <h3 id="463-点击区域划分">4.6.3 点击区域划分</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ 左 <span class="nu">25</span>% 中间 <span class="nu">50</span>% 右 <span class="nu">25</span>% │ │ 上一页 唤出菜单栏 下一页 │ │ （或按设置改为<span class="st">"无操作"</span>） │ │ │ │ ┌─────────┐ │ │ │ 底部 <span class="nu">12</span>% │ │ │ │ 唤出菜单 │ │ │ └─────────┘ │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘</code></pre></div> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">enum</span> <span class="kw">class</span> TapZone { LEFT, CENTER, RIGHT, BOTTOM } <span class="kw">fun</span> resolveZone(offset: Offset, size: Size): TapZone { <span class="kw">val</span> xRatio = offset.x / size.width <span class="kw">val</span> yRatio = offset.y / size.height <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">when</span> { yRatio > <span class="nu">0.</span>88f -> TapZone.BOTTOM xRatio < <span class="nu">0.</span>25f -> TapZone.LEFT xRatio > <span class="nu">0.</span>75f -> TapZone.RIGHT <span class="kw">else</span> -> TapZone.CENTER } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>可配置项</strong>（见 Proto <code>ReaderPrefs.tap_zone_enabled</code>）：</p> <ul><li>关闭点击翻页 → 所有点击只唤出菜单</li><li>交换左右区域 → 左手/右手模式</li><li>音量键翻页：<code>NONE / PAGE_TURN / SCROLL / BRIGHTNESS</code></li></ul> <h3 id="464-常驻状态栏">4.6.4 常驻状态栏</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="nu">24</span> 📶 🔋 <span class="nu">78</span>% 本章 <span class="nu">42</span>% ← 顶部（可关）│ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ 正 文 区 域 │ │ │ data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> ReaderStatusBar( position: StatusPosition, progress: ReadingProgress, prefs: ReaderPrefs, ) { <span class="kw">val</span> battery <span class="kw">by</span> rememberBatteryState() <span class="cm">// BroadcastReceiver 监听 ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED</span> <span class="kw">val</span> time <span class="kw">by</span> rememberCurrentTime() <span class="cm">// 每分钟更新一次，不是每秒（省电）</span> Row( Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(<span class="nu">28.</span>dp).padding(horizontal = <span class="nu">16.</span>dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, ) { <span class="kw">if</span> (prefs.showBattery) { Icon(batteryIcon(battery.level, battery.charging), <span class="kw">null</span>, Modifier.size(<span class="nu">14.</span>dp)) Text(<span class="st">"${(battery.level * 100).toInt()}%"</span>, style = statusStyle) Spacer(Modifier.width(<span class="nu">12.</span>dp)) } <span class="kw">if</span> (prefs.showClock) { Text(time, style = statusStyle) Spacer(Modifier.width(<span class="nu">12.</span>dp)) } Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f)) <span class="kw">if</span> (prefs.showChapterPercent) { Text(<span class="st">"本章 ${(progress.chapterPercent * 100).toInt()}%"</span>, style = statusStyle) } } } <span class="cm">/** 时间更新：对齐到分钟边界，避免每秒唤醒 */</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> rememberCurrentTime(): State<String> { <span class="kw">val</span> state = remember { mutableStateOf(formatTime(System.currentTimeMillis())) } LaunchedEffect(Unit) { <span class="kw">while</span> (<span class="kw">true</span>) { delay(60_000 - (System.currentTimeMillis() % 60_000)) state.value = formatTime(System.currentTimeMillis()) } } <span class="kw">return</span> state }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>电量为 0 的风险</strong>：<code>rememberBatteryState</code> 用 <code>registerReceiver(null, IntentFilter(ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED))</code> 获取初始值（粘性广播），再用 <code>DisposableEffect</code> 注册监听、<code>onDispose</code> 注销。<strong>忘记注销会导致 Activity 泄漏</strong>。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="465-自动滚屏">4.6.5 自动滚屏</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> AutoScrollEffect( enabled: Boolean, speed: Float, <span class="cm">// 1.0 ~ 10.0</span> listState: LazyListState, onChapterEnd: () -> Unit, ) { LaunchedEffect(enabled, speed) { <span class="kw">if</span> (!enabled) <span class="kw">return</span>@LaunchedEffect <span class="cm">// speed=1 → 8px/s，speed=10 → 80px/s（约 2 行/秒）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> pxPerSecond = 8f * speed <span class="kw">var</span> lastFrame = withFrameNanos { <span class="kw">it</span> } <span class="kw">while</span> (isActive) { withFrameNanos { now -> <span class="kw">val</span> dt = (now - lastFrame) / 1_000_000_000f lastFrame = now <span class="kw">val</span> delta = pxPerSecond * dt <span class="kw">val</span> consumed = listState.scrollBy(delta) <span class="cm">// 到章尾时滚动量被消耗完 → 触发下一章</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (consumed == 0f || abs(consumed) < delta * <span class="nu">0.</span>01f) { onChapterEnd() } } } } }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>为什么用 <code>withFrameNanos</code> 而非 <code>delay(16)</code>？</strong> <code>withFrameNanos</code> 与 Choreographer 帧同步，滚动量与 vsync 严格对齐，画面绝对平滑；<code>delay(16)</code> 会有累积误差与抖动。这是自动滚屏能否"看起来像丝绸"的分水岭。</p></blockquote> <p><strong>自动滚屏 + 常亮的组合</strong>：开启自动滚屏时自动打开 <code>keepScreenOn</code>，暂停时恢复。</p> <h3 id="466-交互验收要点">4.6.6 交互验收要点</h3> <ul><li><span class="task">☐</span> 五种翻页模式均可正常工作，模式切换不需重启</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 覆盖翻页在 120Hz 屏上帧率 ≥ 90fps（用 <code>dumpsys gfxinfo</code> 验证）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 快速连续翻页 30 次无白屏、无错页、无卡死</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 点击三个区域行为正确，关闭点击翻页后仅唤出菜单</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 电量/时间显示正确，且退出阅读器后无 BroadcastReceiver 泄漏（LeakCanary 无告警）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 自动滚屏 1 小时无累积抖动、无掉帧</li></ul> <hr> <h2 id="47-网络书源管理">4.7 网络书源管理</h2> <h3 id="471-书源数据结构运行时模型">4.7.1 书源数据结构（运行时模型）</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// core:model — BookSource.kt</span> @Serializable <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> BookSource( <span class="kw">val</span> id: Long = <span class="nu">0</span>, <span class="kw">val</span> name: String, <span class="kw">val</span> group: String = <span class="st">"默认"</span>, <span class="kw">val</span> baseUrl: String, <span class="kw">val</span> enabled: Boolean = <span class="kw">val</span> timeoutMs: Long = 15_000, <span class="kw">val</span> needCookie: Boolean = <span class="kw">false</span>, <span class="kw">val</span> rules: SourceRules, ) @Serializable <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> SourceRules( <span class="cm">/** 搜索：URL 模板 + 请求方式 + 结果列表选择器 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> search: SearchRule, <span class="cm">/** 详情页 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> detail: DetailRule, <span class="cm">/** 目录页 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> catalog: CatalogRule, <span class="cm">/** 正文页 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> content: ContentRule, <span class="cm">/** 可选：章节列表排序方式 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> catalogOrder: String = <span class="st">"ASC"</span>, <span class="cm">// ASC | aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// core:network — DomainRateLimiter.kt</span> <span class="cm">/** * 按域名的令牌桶限流。 * 目的：避免"聚合搜索 20 个源 → 同一个站点被并发打 20 次" 触发风控。 */</span> <span class="kw">class</span> DomainRateLimiter( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> permitsPerSecond: Int = <span class="nu">2</span>, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> maxBurst: Int = <span class="nu">4</span>, ) { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> buckets = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Bucket>() <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> acquire(url: String) { <span class="kw">val</span> host = runCatching { URL(url).host }.getOrDefault(url) <span class="kw">val</span> bucket = buckets.getOrPut(host) { Bucket(permitsPerSecond.toDouble(), maxBurst) } bucket.acquire() <span class="cm">// 挂起直到有令牌</span> } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Bucket(rate: Double, capacity: Int) { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> mutex = Mutex() <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> tokens = capacity.toDouble() <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> lastRefill = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> acquire() = mutex.withLock { <span class="kw">while</span> (<span class="kw">true</span>) { refill() <span class="kw">if</span> (tokens >= <span class="nu">1.0</span>) { tokens -= <span class="nu">1.0</span>; <span class="kw">return</span>@withLock } <span class="kw">val</span> waitMs = ((<span class="nu">1.0</span> - tokens) / rate * <span class="nu">1000</span>).toLong().coerceAtLeast(<span class="nu">20</span>) mutex.unlock() <span class="kw">try</span> { delay(waitMs) } <span class="kw">finally</span> { mutex.lock() } } } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> refill() { <span class="kw">val</span> now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() <span class="kw">val</span> elapsed = (now - lastRefill) / <span class="nu">1000.0</span> tokens = (tokens + elapsed * rate).coerceAtMost(capacity) lastRefill = now } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> rate = rate } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>五层反封禁</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">层</th><th style="text-align:left">手段</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">1</td><td style="text-align:left">UA 轮换</td><td style="text-align:left">每次请求随机从池中取（书源可自定义覆盖）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">2</td><td style="text-align:left">按域限流</td><td style="text-align:left">每域 2 req/s，突发上限 4</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">3</td><td style="text-align:left">Referer 伪装</td><td style="text-align:left">带上 <code>baseUrl</code> 作 Referer</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">4</td><td style="text-align:left">失败退避</td><td style="text-align:left">超时/5xx 重试 2 次，间隔 1s / 3s（指数退避）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">5</td><td style="text-align:left">健康度降级</td><td style="text-align:left">连续失败 3 次 → <code>DEGRADED</code>（降低并发权重）；5 次 → <code>BROKEN</code>（聚合搜索时跳过，管理页红点提示）</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 style="text-align:left">现象</th><th style="text-align:left">诊断输出</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">HTTP 403</td><td style="text-align:left">"站点返回 403，可能屏蔽了当前 class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> sourceRepo: SourceRepository, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> engine: SourceEngine, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> merger: ResultMerger, ) { <span class="kw">operator</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> invoke(keyword: String): Flow<SearchStreamEvent> = channelFlow { <span class="kw">val</span> sources = sourceRepo.enabledSourcesSortedByHealth() <span class="kw">if</span> (sources.isEmpty()) { send(SearchStreamEvent.AllFailed(<span class="st">"没有启用的书源"</span>)) <span class="kw">return</span>@channelFlow } <span class="kw">val</span> total = sources.size <span class="kw">var</span> completed = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">val</span> allResults = mutableListOf<SourceSearchResult>() supervisorScope { sources.forEach { source -> launch(KrDispatchers.Network) { <span class="kw">val</span> started = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() <span class="kw">val</span> result = runCatching { engine.search(source, keyword) } <span class="kw">val</span> elapsed = sourceRepo.recordFailure(source.id) send(SearchStreamEvent.SourceFailed(source, result.exceptionOrNull()!!.message.orEmpty())) } } <span class="cm">// 每完成一个源，重新归并（边搜边出）</span> <span (allResults.isEmpty()) send(SearchStreamEvent.AllFailed(<span class="st">"所有书源均失败，请检查网络或书源可用性"</span>)) }.buffer(Channel.UNLIMITED).flowOn(KrDispatchers.Default) }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>并发度控制</strong>：不是"20 个源同时打"。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" ResultMerger { <span class="kw">fun</span> merge(results: List<SourceSearchResult>): List<MergedBook> { <span class="kw">val</span> groups = mutableListOf<MutableList<SourceBookItem>>() results.flatMap { r -> r.items.map { r.source to <span class="kw">it</span> } } .forEach { (source, item) -> <span class="kw">val</span> key = NormalizedKey.of(item) <span class="cm">// 在已有组中找同书</span> <span class="kw">val</span> target = groups.firstOrNull { g -> g.any { NormalizedKey.of(<span class="kw">it</span>.item).matches(key) } } <span class="kw">if</span> (target != <span class="st">"[\s（(【\[][^)）】\]]{0,30}?(小说|最新章节|全文阅读|无弹窗|免费阅读|TXT下载)[^)）】\]]{0,30}?[)）】\]]"</span><span class="st">""</span>) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> PUNCT = Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"[·・.,，。:：;；!！?？'"</span>“”‘’\-—_~～]<span class="st">""</span>") <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Key(<span normalize(item.item.author.orEmpty()), ) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> normalize(s: String): String = s .replace(NOISE, <span class="st">""</span>) .replace(PUNCT, <span class="st">""</span>) .replace(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"\s+"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="st">""</span>) .lowercase() .trim() <span class="cm">/** class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">false</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (title == other.title) { <span class="cm">// 作者都为空，或有交集 → 认为同一本</span> <span class="kw">return</span> author.isEmpty() || other.author.isEmpty() || author.contains(other.author) || other.author.contains(author) } <span class="kw">val</span> authorMatches = author.isNotEmpty() && other.author.isNotEmpty() && (author.contains(other.author) || other.author.contains(author)) <span class="kw">if</span> (!authorMatches) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">false</span> <span class="kw">return</span> title.contains(other.title) || other.title.contains(title) || levenshtein(title, other.title) <= <span class="nu">2</span> } } <span class="cm">/** 标准 Levenshtein，带两行滚动数组降内存 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> levenshtein(a: String, b: String): Int { <span class="kw">if</span> (a == b) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (a.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">return</span> b.length <span class="kw">if</span> (b.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">return</span> a.length <span class="kw">var</span> prev = IntArray(b.length + <span class="nu">1</span>) { <span class="kw">it</span> } <span class="kw">var</span> curr = IntArray(b.length + <span class="nu">1</span>) <span class="kw">for</span> (i <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">1</span>..a.length) { curr[<span class="nu">0</span>] = i <span class="kw">for</span> (j <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">1</span>..b.length) { <span class="kw">val</span> cost = <span class="kw">if</span> (a[i - <span class="nu">1</span>] == b[j - <span class="nu">1</span>]) <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="nu">1</span> curr[j] = minOf(curr[j - <span class="nu">1</span>] + <span class="nu">1</span>, prev[j] + <span class="nu">1</span>, prev[j - <span class="nu">1</span>] + cost) } prev = curr.copyOf() } <span class="kw">return</span> prev[b.length] }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>质量分计算</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">val</span> qualityScore: Double = with(sourceCount * <span class="nu">0.35</span>) + (hasCover * <span class="nu">0.15</span>) + (hasIntro * <span class="nu">0.10</span>) + (chapterCountEstimate / <span class="nu">1000.0</span> * <span class="nu">0.20</span>) + <span class="cm">// 章节数多的源通常更完整</span> (latestChapterFreshness * <span class="nu">0.20</span>) <span class="cm">// 最新章节的更新时间越近越新</span></code></pre></div> <h3 id="483-换源时序保留进度">4.8.3 换源时序（保留进度）</h3> <div class="codeblock" (number == <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="nu">10</span> <span class="kw">else</span> number * <span class="nu">10</span> } <span class="kw">else</span> -> number += v } } <span class="kw">return</span> total + section + number } <span class="kw">fun</span> match( oldTitle: String?, oldIndex: Int, oldTotal: Int, newCatalog: List<CatalogItem>, ): Match? { <span newCatalog.firstOrNull { extractOrdinal(<span class="kw">it</span>.title) == oldOrd } ?.let { <span class="kw">return</span> Match(newCatalog.indexOf(<span class="kw">it</span>), <span class="kw">it</span>.title, <span class="nu">0.</span>9f) } } <span class="cm">// 策略 3：标题相似度（编辑距离最小，且需低于阈值）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> candidates = newCatalog.mapIndexedNotNull { i, c -> <span <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="kw">null</span> }.sortedBy { <span class="kw">it</span>.third } candidates.firstOrNull()?.let { <span class="kw">return</span> Match(<span class="kw">it</span>.first, <span class="kw">it</span>.second, <span class="nu">0.</span>6f) } <span class="cm">// 策略 4：比例位置回退</span> <span class="kw">val</span> ratio = oldIndex.toFloat() / oldTotal.coerceAtLeast(<span class="nu">1</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> fallbackIndex = (ratio * newCatalog.size).toInt().coerceIn(<span class="nu">0</span>, newCatalog.lastIndex) <span class="st">"[：:·・.,，。]"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="st">""</span>) .trim() }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>置信度反馈给用户</strong>：<code>confidence < 0.5</code> 时 Toast class="kw">fun</span> doWork(): Result { <span class="kw">val</span> bookId = inputData.getLong(KEY_BOOK_ID, -<span class="nu">1</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> fromChapter = inputData.getInt(KEY_FROM, <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> toChapter = inputData.getInt(KEY_TO, Int.MAX_VALUE) <span class="kw">if</span> (bookId <= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> Result.failure() <span class="kw">val</span> book = bookRepo.getBook(bookId) ?: <span class="kw">return</span> Result.failure() <span class="kw">val</span> source = book.sourceId?.let { sourceRepo.<span class="kw">get</span>(<span class="kw">it</span>) } ?: <span class="kw">return</span> Result.failure() <span class="kw">val</span> chapters = bookRepo.getChapters(bookId) .filter { <span class="kw">it</span>.chapterIndex <span class="kw">in</span> fromChapter..toChapter && !<span class="kw">it</span>.isCached } <span class="kw">if</span> (chapters.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">return</span> Result.success() <span class="kw">var</span> done = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> failedConsecutive = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">for</span> (ch <span class="kw">in</span> chapters) { <span class="kw">if</span> (isStopped) <span class="kw">return</span> Result.retry() <span class="cm">// 用户取消 / 系统停止</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (failedConsecutive >= <span class="nu">5</span>) { <span class="cm">// 连续 5 次失败 → 判定源不可用，停止并让用户知道</span> <span class="kw">return</span> Result.failure(workDataOf(<span class="st">"reason"</span> to <span class="st">"书源连续失败，已停止缓存"</span>)) } <span class="kw">try</span> { <span class="kw">val</span> content = engine.content(source, ch.sourceUrl!!) cacheRepo.put(bookId, ch.chapterIndex, ch.title, content, source.id) done++ failedConsecutive = <span class="nu">0</span> setProgress(workDataOf(KEY_DONE to done, KEY_TOTAL to chapters.size)) } <span class="kw">catch</span> (e: Exception) { failedConsecutive++ <span class="cm">// 单章失败继续下一章（章节点可能已被站点删除）</span> } <span class="cm">// 节流：每章之间延迟，避免触发风控</span> delay(<span class="kw">if</span> (failedConsecutive > <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="nu">2000</span> <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="nu">300</span>) } <span class="kw">return</span> Result.success(workDataOf(KEY_DONE to done, KEY_TOTAL to chapters.size)) } <span class="kw">companion</span> <span class="kw">object</span> { <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> KEY_BOOK_ID = <span class="st">"bookId"</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> KEY_FROM = <span class="st">"from"</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> KEY_TO = <span class="st">"to"</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> KEY_DONE = <span class="st">"done"</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> KEY_TOTAL = <span class="st">"total"</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> enqueue(ctx: Context, bookId: Long, from: Int, to: Int, wifiOnly: Boolean) { <span class="kw">val</span> constraints = Constraints.Builder() .setRequiredNetworkType(<span class="kw">if</span> (wifiOnly) NetworkType.UNMETERED <span class="kw">else</span> NetworkType.CONNECTED) .setRequiresBatteryNotLow(<span class="kw">true</span>) .build() <span class="kw">val</span> request = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<CacheChaptersWorker>() .setInputData(workDataOf(KEY_BOOK_ID to bookId, KEY_FROM to from, KEY_TO to to)) .setConstraints(constraints) .setBackoffCriteria(BackoffPolicy.EXPONENTIAL, <span class="nu">30</span>, TimeUnit.SECONDS) .addTag(<span class="st">"cache_book_$bookId"</span>) .build() WorkManager.getInstance(ctx) .enqueueUniqueWork(<span class="st">"cache_book_$bookId"</span>, ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP, request) } } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>缓存策略配置</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">选项</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">缓存范围</td><td style="text-align:left">全部章节 / 当前章起后 N 章 / 指定章节区间</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">网络约束</td><td style="text-align:left">仅 Wi-Fi（默认）/ 任意网络</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">缓存时机</td><td style="text-align:left">手动触发（默认）/ 打开书时自动缓存后 30 章（可选）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">缓存上限</td><td style="text-align:left">默认 200MB，可调 50MB ~ 2GB</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">过期策略</td><td style="text-align:left">连载中书缓存 7 天过期；完结书永不过期</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="485-缓存淘汰算法">4.8.5 缓存淘汰算法</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:sources — CacheEvictor.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> CacheEvictor @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> cacheDao: ChapterCacheDao, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> prefs: GlobalPrefsStore, ) { <span class="cm">/** 在每次写入缓存后调用；超过上限时按优先级淘汰 */</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> evictIfNeeded(): EvictReport = withContext(KrDispatchers.IO) { <span class="kw">val</span> limitBytes = prefs.cacheLimitMb * 1024L * 1024L <span class="kw">val</span> total = cacheDao.totalBytes() <span class="kw">if</span> (total <= limitBytes) <span class="kw">return</span>@withContext EvictReport.None <span class="kw">var</span> needFree = total - limitBytes <span class="kw">var</span> evicted = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> freedBytes = 0L <span class="cm">// 优先级 1：已过期条目</span> <span class="kw">val</span> now = System.currentTimeMillis() cacheDao.selectExpired(now).forEach { row -> <span class="kw">if</span> (needFree <= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">return</span>@forEach cacheDao.delete(row.id); needFree -= row.byteSize; freedBytes += row.byteSize; evicted++ } <span class="cm">// 优先级 2：非当前阅读书籍 + 距离阅读位置最远</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (needFree > <span class="nu">0</span>) { <span class="kw">val</span> currentBookId = prefs.currentReadingBookId <span class="kw">val</span> currentChapter = prefs.currentReadingChapter cacheDao.selectOldestFirst() .filter { <span class="kw">it</span>.bookId != currentBookId } .forEach { row -> <span class="kw">if</span> (needFree <= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">return</span>@forEach cacheDao.delete(row.id); needFree -= row.byteSize; freedBytes += row.byteSize; evicted++ } } <span class="cm">// 优先级 3：当前书籍中距离阅读位置 > 20 章的</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (needFree > <span class="nu">0</span>) { cacheDao.selectByBook(prefs.currentReadingBookId) .filter { abs(<span class="kw">it</span>.chapterIndex - prefs.currentReadingChapter) > <span class="nu">20</span> } .sortedByDescending { abs(<span class="kw">it</span>.chapterIndex - prefs.currentReadingChapter) } .forEach { row -> <span class="kw">if</span> (needFree <= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">return</span>@forEach cacheDao.delete(row.id); needFree -= row.byteSize; freedBytes += row.byteSize; evicted++ } } <span class="cm">// 保护：当前书籍 ±5 章永不淘汰（上面的 filter 已保证）</span> EvictReport(target = limitBytes, freedBytes = freedBytes, evictedCount = evicted) } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="486-聚合搜索与缓存验收要点">4.8.6 聚合搜索与缓存验收要点</h3> <ul><li><span class="task">☐</span> 8 个书源并发搜索，全部返回耗时 ≤ 3s（含最慢源）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 单个源失败不影响其他源结果展示（<code>supervisorScope</code> 生效）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 同一本书出现在 5 个源，归并为 1 条，展开显示 5 个源</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 换源后章节标题一致，字级偏移保留，继续阅读内容连续</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 连续缓存 500 章，可在设置页看到进度，可中途取消（WorkManager 状态为 CANCELLED）</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 缓存写入超过 200MB 后自动淘汰，当前书 ±5 章未被动</li><li><span class="task">☐</span> 书源连续失败 5 次后续章节停止缓存并给出明确提示</li></ul> <hr> <p><strong>上一卷</strong>：<a href="#vol-03">第 3 卷 · 数据模型与存储设计</a> ｜ <strong>下一卷</strong>：<a href="#vol-05">第 5 卷 · 关键算法实现方案</a></p> <hr> <h1 id="vol-05">第 5 卷 · 关键算法实现方案</h1> <blockquote><p>第 4 卷讲"每个模块怎么做"，本卷讲"<strong>最难的那几个点怎么做对、做快</strong>"。每个算法以「算法卡片」形式给出：目标 → 复杂度 → 伪代码 → 边界 → 测试向量 → 达标判据。本卷中列出的测试向量<strong>必须</strong>成为单元测试用例。</p></blockquote> <hr> <h2 id="51-算法卡-a编码探测准确率攻坚">5.1 算法卡 A：编码探测（准确率攻坚）</h2> <h3 id="a0-目标">A.0 目标</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">项</th><th style="text-align:left">要求</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">输入</td><td style="text-align:left">TXT 文件（任意大小）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">输出</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>Charset</code> + 置信度 + 探测依据</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">准确率</td><td style="text-align:left">在测试语料集上 ≥ 99%（见 A.5）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">耗时</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 120ms（10MB 文件）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">内存</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 300KB（只采样，不全读）</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="a1-为什么猜编码这么难">A.1 为什么"猜编码"这么难</h3> <p>中文文本的编码识别有三大混淆区：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">混淆场景</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th><th style="text-align:left">后果</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>UTF-8 vs GBK 短文本</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">只有 3~10 个汉字的文本，字节数太少，统计特征不足</td><td style="text-align:left">可能误判，但影响小（内容少）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>GBK 文本恰好是合法 UTF-8</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">极罕见。GBK 的双字节序列需要恰好构成合法 UTF-8 多字节模式，概率约 1e-6</td><td style="text-align:left">概率可忽略</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>纯 ASCII 文本</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">任何编码都能解</td><td style="text-align:left">直接用 UTF-8（结果相同，无副作用）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>GB2312 vs GBK vs GB18030</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">三者是超集关系</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>统一按 GB18030 处理</strong>（它是 GBK 与 GB2312 的严格超集，能解 GBK 与 GB2312 的全部内容）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>BIG5 vs GBK</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">中国台湾地区文本用 BIG5。二者字节范围重叠</td><td style="text-align:left">需靠"解码后字符是否为常用字"打分区分</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>关键决策</strong>：<strong>内部统一用 GB18030 而非区分 GBK/GB2312</strong>。这消除了一个不可解的歧义来源——凡是能解 GBK 的编码，GB18030 一定能解，且 GB18030 还能解 4 字节扩展区。</p> <h3 id="a2-探测决策树最终版">A.2 探测决策树（最终版）</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code> ┌─────────────────────┐ │ 读取前 256KB 样本 │ └──────────┬──────────┘ ▼ ┌──────────────────────────────┐ │ <span class="nu">1</span>. BOM 检测 │ │ EF BB BF → UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ │ FF FE → UTF-16LE │ │ FE FF → UTF-16BE │ └──────────┬───────────────────┘ 命中 │ 未命中 ▼ ▼ 置信度 <span class="nu">1.0</span> ┌──────────────────────────┐ 直接返回 │ <span class="nu">2</span>. 严格 UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> 校验 │ │ (CodingErrorAction. │ │ REPORT) │ └──────────┬───────────────┘ 通过 │ 失败 ▼ ▼ ┌────────────────────┐ ┌──────────────────────────┐ │ <span class="nu">3</span>. 是否纯 ASCII？ │ │ <span class="nu">4</span>. 启发式打分 │ │ bytes < 0x80 │ │ 候选：GB18030, BIG5, │ │ → UTF-<span class="nu">8</span>, <span class="nu">0.99</span> │ │ UTF-16LE, │ └────────────────────┘ │ UTF-16BE │ │ 打分见 A.<span class="nu">3</span> │ └──────────┬───────────────┘ ▼ ┌──────────────────────────────┐ │ 最高分 ≥ 阈值(<span class="nu">0.55</span>)？ │ │ 是 → 采用，置信度 = 得分 │ │ 否 → 返回 UNKNOWN │ │ UI 提示手动选择 │ └──────────────────────────────┘</code></pre></div> <h3 id="a3-启发式打分函数完整规格">A.3 启发式打分函数（完整规格）</h3> <p>打分必须<strong>多信号联合</strong>，单一信号不足以区分 BIG5 与 GB18030。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — CharsetScorer.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> CharsetScorer { <span class="cm">/** 常用汉字表（前 3500 字，覆盖 99.5% 的日常文本用字） */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> COMMON_CJK: Set<Char> = run { <span class="cm">// 从 assets 加载预置的常用字表（GB2312 一级字库 3755 字）</span> <span class="cm">// 这是"常用度"判据的核心数据</span> loadFromAssets(<span class="st">"gb2312_level1.txt"</span>).toHashSet() } <span class="cm">/** 中国台湾地区 BIG5 特有字符（简体 GBK 中不存在或罕用） */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> BIG5_SPECIFIC = setOf(<span class="st">'臺'</span>, <span class="st">'灣'</span>, <span class="st">'萬'</span>, <span class="st">'裡'</span>, <span class="st">'發'</span>, <span class="st">'來'</span>, <span class="st">'說'</span>, <span class="st">'沒'</span>, <span class="st">'現'</span>, <span class="st">'這'</span>) <span class="cm">/** 简体特有字符（BIG5 中不存在） */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> SIMPLIFIED_SPECIFIC = setOf(<span class="st">'台'</span>, <span class="st">'湾'</span>, <span class="st">'万'</span>, <span class="st">'里'</span>, <span class="st">'发'</span>, <span class="st">'来'</span>, <span class="st">'说'</span>, <span class="st">'没'</span>, <span class="st">'现'</span>, <span class="st">'这'</span>) <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Score( <span class="kw">val</span> charset: String, <span class="kw">val</span> score: Double, <span class="kw">val</span> signals: Map<String, Double>, <span class="cm">// 用于调试与诊断</span> ) <span class="kw">fun</span> score(bytes: ByteArray, charsetName: String): Score { <span class="kw">val</span> charset = Charset.forName(charsetName) <span class="cm">// 信号 1：严格解码可行性（权重最高，因为它是硬约束）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> text = <span class="kw">try</span> { charset.newDecoder() .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT) .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT) .decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString() } <span class="kw">catch</span> (e: CharacterCodingException) { <span class="cm">// 解码失败比例：允许少量失败（文件尾部可能被截断），</span> <span class="cm">// 但失败超过 0.5% 就判定该编码不可能</span> <span class="kw">val</span> lossy = charset.newDecoder() .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE) .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE) .decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString() <span class="kw">val</span> badRatio = lossy.count { <span class="kw">it</span> == <span class="st">'\uFFFD'</span> }.toDouble() / lossy.length.coerceAtLeast(<span class="nu">1</span>) <span class="kw">if</span> (badRatio > <span class="nu">0.005</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> Score(charsetName, <span class="nu">0.0</span>, mapOf(<span class="st">"decode"</span> to <span class="nu">0.0</span>)) <span class="kw">return</span> Score(charsetName, <span class="nu">0.15</span> * (<span class="nu">1</span> - badRatio / <span class="nu">0.005</span>), mapOf(<span class="st">"decode"</span> to badRatio)) } <span class="kw">if</span> (text.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">return</span> Score(charsetName, <span class="nu">0.0</span>, emptyMap()) <span class="cm">// 统计各类字符</span> <span class="kw">var</span> cjkCount = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> commonCjkCount = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> rareCjkCount = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> printableAscii = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> controlCount = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> big5Specific = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> simplifiedSpecific = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> punctuation = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">val</span> PUNCT = <span class="st">"，。！？；：、“”‘’（）《》〈〉【】—…·　「」『』"</span> <span class="kw">val</span> RARE_RANGES = listOf( <span class="st">'\u3400'</span>..<span class="st">'\u4DBF'</span>, <span class="cm">// 扩展 A 区（生僻）</span> <span class="st">'\uF900'</span>..<span class="st">'\uFAFF'</span>, <span class="cm">// 兼容汉字</span> ) <span class="kw">for</span> (ch <span class="kw">in</span> text) { <span class="kw">val</span> code = ch.code <span class="kw">when</span> { ch <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="st">'\u4E00'</span>..<span class="st">'\u9FFF'</span> -> { cjkCount++ <span class="kw">if</span> (ch <span class="kw">in</span> COMMON_CJK) commonCjkCount++ <span class="kw">else</span> rareCjkCount++ } code <span class="kw">in</span> 0x20..0x7E || ch == <span class="st">'\n'</span> || ch == <span class="st">'\r'</span> || ch == <span class="st">'\t'</span> -> printableAscii++ code < 0x20 -> controlCount++ ch == <span class="st">'\uFFFD'</span> -> controlCount++ <span class="cm">// 替换字符</span> ch <span class="kw">in</span> PUNCT -> punctuation++ RARE_RANGES.any { ch <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="kw">it</span> } -> rareCjkCount++ ch == <span class="st">'\u3000'</span> || ch == <span class="st">'\u2000'</span>..<span class="st">'\u200B'</span> -> punctuation++ ch <span class="kw">in</span> BIG5_SPECIFIC -> big5Specific++ ch <span class="kw">in</span> SIMPLIFIED_SPECIFIC -> simplifiedSpecific++ } } <span class="kw">val</span> total = text.length.toDouble() <span class="kw">val</span> cjkRatio = cjkCount / total <span class="kw">val</span> commonRatio = commonCjkCount / cjkCount.coerceAtLeast(<span class="nu">1</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> rareRatio = rareCjkCount / cjkCount.coerceAtLeast(<span class="nu">1</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> controlRatio = controlCount / total <span class="kw">val</span> punctRatio = punctuation / total <span class="cm">// 信号 2：控制字符惩罚（编码猜错时最明显的特征）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> controlPenalty = <span class="kw">when</span> { controlRatio > <span class="nu">0.02</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> <span class="cm">// 直接判死</span> controlRatio > <span class="nu">0.005</span> -> <span class="nu">0.5</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> controlRatio / <span class="nu">0.005</span> * <span class="nu">0.1</span> } <span class="cm">// 信号 3：常用字占比（正确的编码解出来都是常用字）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> commonnessScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { cjkRatio < <span class="nu">0.05</span> -> <span class="nu">0.5</span> <span class="cm">// 英文为主，此信号无效，给中性分</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> commonRatio * <span class="nu">0.7</span> + (<span class="nu">1</span> - rareRatio) * <span class="nu">0.3</span> } <span class="cm">// 信号 4：中日韩字符占比（中文文本应有大量 CJK）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> cjkScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { cjkRatio > <span class="nu">0.30</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> cjkRatio > <span class="nu">0.10</span> -> <span class="nu">0.8</span> cjkRatio > <span class="nu">0.02</span> -> <span class="nu">0.6</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.5</span> <span class="cm">// 可能确实是英文文本</span> } <span class="cm">// 信号 5：标点合理性（正确编码下中文标点会正常出现）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> punctScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { punctRatio > <span class="nu">0.03</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> punctRatio > <span class="nu">0.01</span> -> <span class="nu">0.8</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.6</span> } <span class="cm">// 信号 6：简繁特征（区分 GB18030 与 BIG5 的关键）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> scriptScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { simplifiedSpecific > <span class="nu">0</span> && big5Specific == <span class="nu">0</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> big5Specific > <span class="nu">0</span> && simplifiedSpecific == <span class="nu">0</span> -> <span class="nu">0.3</span> <span class="cm">// 对 GB18030 不利，对 BIG5 有利</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.7</span> } <span class="cm">// 加权合成</span> <span class="kw">val</span> raw = commonnessScore * <span class="nu">0.32</span> + cjkScore * <span class="nu">0.18</span> + punctScore * <span class="nu">0.10</span> + scriptScore * <span class="nu">0.25</span> + (<span class="nu">1</span> - controlPenalty) * <span class="nu">0.15</span> <span class="kw">return</span> Score( charset = charsetName, score = raw.coerceIn(<span class="nu">0.0</span>, <span class="nu">1.0</span>), signals = mapOf( <span class="st">"control"</span> to controlRatio, <span class="st">"common"</span> to commonRatio, <span class="st">"cjk"</span> to cjkRatio, <span class="st">"punct"</span> to punctRatio, <span class="st">"simplified"</span> to simplifiedSpecific.toDouble(), <span class="st">"big5"</span> to big5Specific.toDouble(), ), ) } }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>注意 <code>scriptScore</code> 对 BIG5 的处理</strong>：上面的实现是给"GB18030 打分"的视角，<code>scriptScore</code> 在检测到 BIG5 特有字时给 0.3（惩罚 GB18030）。对 BIG5 候选自身打分的实现，需要把 <code>BIG5_SPECIFIC</code> 与 <code>SIMPLIFIED_SPECIFIC</code> 的角色对调——在 <code>CharsetScorer</code> 中传入 <code>isTraditionalTarget</code> 参数控制。这一点必须在实现时写明，否则 BIG5 永远判不出来。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="a4-混淆场景实战对照表">A.4 混淆场景实战对照表</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">文件特征</th><th style="text-align:left">正确编码</th><th style="text-align:left">探测逻辑路径</th><th style="text-align:left">可能的误判与防御</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">带 BOM 的 UTF-8</td><td style="text-align:left">UTF-8</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 1（BOM）</td><td style="text-align:left">无</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">纯中文简体，UTF-8 无 BOM</td><td style="text-align:left">UTF-8</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 2（严格校验通过）</td><td style="text-align:left">无</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">纯中文简体，GBK</td><td style="text-align:left">GB18030</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 4，GB18030 得分最高</td><td style="text-align:left">需确保 UTF-8 严格校验<strong>先</strong>跑且失败</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">中英混合，GBK</td><td style="text-align:left">GB18030</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 4</td><td style="text-align:left">英文部分无区分度，靠中文部分</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">中国台湾地区正体中文</td><td style="text-align:left">BIG5</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 4，BIG5 的 <code>scriptScore</code> 高</td><td style="text-align:left">若文本含大量简体字 → 可能误判为 GB18030。<strong>必须提示用户可手动切换</strong></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">只有 5 个汉字的短文本</td><td style="text-align:left">任意</td><td style="text-align:left">各编码得分相近</td><td style="text-align:left">采用"最可能是 UTF-8"的默认，且<strong>UI 提供一键切换编码</strong></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">UTF-16 无 BOM</td><td style="text-align:left">UTF-16LE</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 4，控制字符率极低（因为 UTF-16 下奇偶字节组合让 GB18030 解码出大量控制字符）</td><td style="text-align:left">UTF-16 文件在 GB18030 视角下会产生很多控制字符 → 反向验证有效</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">文件前半 UTF-8 后半 GBK（拼接损坏）</td><td style="text-align:left">UTF-8</td><td style="text-align:left">路径 2 在前 256KB 通过 → 采用 UTF-8</td><td style="text-align:left">后半段会有替换字符。<strong>这是可接受降级</strong>：至少一半内容可读</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="a5-测试向量必须成为单元测试">A.5 测试向量（<strong>必须成为单元测试</strong>）</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>测试语料集（assets/test/charset/）： ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────┬──────────┐ │ 文件名 │ 真实编码 │ 期望结果 │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────┼──────────┤ │ utf8_bom_simple.txt (1KB 简体) │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span>+BOM │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ │ utf8_nobom_simple.txt (50KB 简体) │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ │ utf8_nobom_mixed.txt (200KB 中英) │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ │ gbk_simple.txt (80KB 简体) │ GBK │ GB18030 │ │ gbk_mixed.txt (300KB 中英) │ GBK │ GB18030 │ │ gbk_large.txt (10MB 简体) │ GBK │ GB18030 │ │ gb2312_classic.txt (60KB) │ GB2312 │ GB18030 │ │ big5_traditional.txt (120KB 正体) │ BIG5 │ BIG5 │ │ utf16le_bom.txt (40KB) │ UTF-16LE │ UTF-16LE │ │ utf16be_bom.txt (40KB) │ UTF-16BE │ UTF-16BE │ │ utf16le_nobom.txt (40KB) │ UTF-16LE │ UTF-16LE │ │ ascii_only.txt (100KB 英文) │ ASCII │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ │ tiny_5chars.txt (<span class="nu">5</span> 个汉字) │ GBK │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span>* │ │ utf8_truncated.txt (UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> 被截断) │ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span>(坏尾)│ UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> │ │ crlf_windows.txt (100KB) │ GBK+\r\n │ GB18030 │ └──────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────┴──────────┘ * tiny_5chars 允许任意结果，但必须<span class="st">"不崩溃 + 提示可手动切换"</span> 准确率判据：<span class="nu">14</span>/<span class="nu">15</span> 正确（≥ <span class="nu">93</span>%），排除 tiny_5chars 后 <span class="nu">14</span>/<span class="nu">14</span>（<span class="nu">100</span>%）</code></pre></div> <p><strong>测试实现要点</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Test <span class="kw">fun</span> `探测准确率达标`() { <span class="kw">val</span> corpus = loadCorpus(<span class="st">"assets/test/charset"</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> results = corpus.map { case -> <span class="kw">val</span> detected = runBlocking { detector.detect(case.<span class="kw">file</span>) } case.expected to detected.charset.name() } <span class="kw">val</span> correct = results.count { (exp, got) -> exp == got } assertTrue( <span class="st">"编码探测准确率 $correct/${results.size}，低于 93% 门槛"</span>, correct.toDouble() / results.size >= <span class="nu">0.93</span>, ) <span class="cm">// 输出混淆矩阵，便于分析</span> results.groupBy { <span class="kw">it</span>.first }.forEach { (exp, list) -> println(<span class="st">"期望 $exp → $list (${list.size} 例)"</span>) } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="a6-手动切换兜底必做">A.6 手动切换兜底（必做）</h3> <p>任何自动探测都可能失败。<strong>必须提供用户手动切换通道，且切换后立即重新分章</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// feature/settings — EncodingPickerDialog.kt</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> EncodingPickerDialog( current: String, onConfirm: (String) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, ) { <span class="kw">val</span> candidates = listOf(<span class="st">"UTF-8"</span>, <span class="st">"GB18030"</span>, <span class="st">"BIG5"</span>, <span class="st">"UTF-16LE"</span>, <span class="st">"UTF-16BE"</span>) AlertDialog( onDismissRequest = onDismiss, title = { Text(<span class="st">"选择文件编码"</span>) }, text = { Column { Text(<span class="st">"当前显示为乱码，可手动切换编码："</span>, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium) Spacer(Modifier.height(<span class="nu">12.</span>dp)) candidates.forEach { cs -> <span class="cm">// ★ 每个选项显示该编码下前 60 字的实际预览——用户一眼就能看出对不对</span> EncodingPreviewRow( charset = cs, previewText = remember(cs) { previewWith(cs, <span class="nu">60</span>) }, selected = cs == current, onClick = { onConfirm(cs) }, ) } } }, confirmButton = {}, dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) { Text(<span class="st">"取消"</span>) } }, ) }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>带预览的编码选择器</strong>是这个功能的体验关键。让用户在 5 个选项里逐个试读，比只给编码名称有效 10 倍。</p></blockquote> <hr> <h2 id="52-算法卡-b章节识别的多信号投票与误报抑制">5.2 算法卡 B：章节识别的多信号投票与误报抑制</h2> <h3 id="b0-目标">B.0 目标</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">项</th><th style="text-align:left">要求</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">输入</td><td style="text-align:left">TXT 文本流（行迭代器）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">输出</td><td style="text-align:left">章节列表，含精确字符偏移</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">召回率</td><td style="text-align:left">标准网文（2000 章）识别 ≥ 99.5%</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">精确率</td><td style="text-align:left">误报（把正文当章节）≤ 0.2%</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">耗时</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 300ms（10MB 文件，约 18 万行）</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="b1-召回与精确的矛盾">B.1 召回与精确的矛盾</h3> <p><strong>放宽规则能提高召回但引入误报</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>正文中常见的<span class="st">"伪章节"</span>行： 「第三章的那个人」 ← 引用，不是章节标题 「他想起第一回见到她的时候」 ← 正文句子 「卷一 少年游」 ← 真章节（卷标题） 「第<span class="nu">3</span>章 更新通知：本作品已完结」 ← 真章节（含噪声） 「第一章」 ← 真章节 第一章 ← 真章节（无标题） CHAPTER <span class="nu">12</span> ← 真章节（英文） <span class="nu">12</span> ← 可能是页码，不是章节！ 误报的代价：目录里出现 <span class="st">"第三章的那个人"</span> → 用户困惑 漏报的代价：目录缺章 → 无法跳转</code></pre></div> <h3 id="b2-多信号投票机制">B.2 多信号投票机制</h3> <p><strong>核心思想</strong>：不靠单条规则，而是对"候选行"计算多个特征，用加权投票判定。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — ChapterCandidateVoter.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> ChapterCandidateVoter { <span class="cm">/** 候选行的特征向量 */</span> <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Features( <span class="kw">val</span> lineNo: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> charOffset: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> rawText: String, <span class="kw">val</span> trimmed: String, <span class="kw">val</span> length: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> leadingWhitespace: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> hasFollowingBlankLine: Boolean, <span class="kw">val</span> prevLineIsBlank: Boolean, <span class="kw">val</span> prevLineLength: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> nextLineLength: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> ordinal: Int?, <span class="cm">// "第 N 章" 抽出的 N</span> <span class="kw">val</span> keywordType: KeywordType, <span class="cm">// 命中的关键词类型</span> <span class="kw">val</span> matchRuleId: String, ) <span class="kw">enum</span> <span class="kw">class</span> KeywordType { NUMBERED, VOLUME, SPECIAL, ENGLISH, DIGIT_ONLY, NONE } <span class="cm">/** 信号权重表（可通过实验调优） */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> W_RULE_MATCH = <span class="nu">0.30</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> W_LENGTH = <span class="nu">0.15</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> W_BLANK_NEIGHBOR = <span class="nu">0.15</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> W_DISTANCE = <span class="nu">0.15</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> W_ORDINAL_MONOTONIC = <span class="nu">0.20</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> W_NO_PUNCT_END = <span class="nu">0.05</span> <span class="cm">/** 判定阈值：得分 ≥ 0.65 视为真章节 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> THRESHOLD = <span class="nu">0.65</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> vote(f: Features, ctx: VoteContext): VoteResult { <span class="kw">var</span> score = <span class="nu">0.0</span> <span class="kw">val</span> reasons = mutableListOf<String>() <span class="cm">// ── 信号 1：规则匹配置信度 ─────────────────────────</span> score += <span class="kw">when</span> (f.matchRuleId) { <span class="st">"cn_numbered"</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> <span class="st">"cn_volume"</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> <span class="st">"cn_special"</span> -> <span class="nu">0.95</span> <span class="st">"en_numbered"</span> -> <span class="nu">0.9</span> <span class="st">"digit_only"</span> -> <span class="nu">0.3</span> <span class="cm">// "12" 太容易是页码，低置信</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.0</span> } * W_RULE_MATCH <span class="cm">// ── 信号 2：长度合理性 ────────────────────────────</span> <span class="cm">// 章节标题通常 3~30 字。过长（>40）或过短（1 字）都可疑</span> <span class="kw">val</span> lenScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { f.length <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">3</span>..<span class="nu">30</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> f.length <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">31</span>..<span class="nu">40</span> -> <span class="nu">0.6</span> f.length == <span class="nu">2</span> -> <span class="nu">0.5</span> f.length == <span class="nu">1</span> -> <span class="nu">0.2</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.1</span> } score += lenScore * W_LENGTH <span class="kw">if</span> (lenScore < <span class="nu">0.5</span>) reasons += <span class="st">"长度异常(${f.length})"</span> <span class="cm">// ── 信号 3：前后空行（章节标题通常独立成行，前后有空白）──</span> <span class="kw">val</span> blankScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { f.prevLineIsBlank && f.hasFollowingBlankLine -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> f.prevLineIsBlank || f.hasFollowingBlankLine -> <span class="nu">0.7</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.3</span> } score += blankScore * W_BLANK_NEIGHBOR <span class="cm">// ── 信号 4：与上一章的距离 ────────────────────────</span> <span class="cm">// 正文中的"第三章"引用会密集出现（几十字内多个）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> dist = f.charOffset - ctx.lastAcceptedOffset <span class="kw">val</span> distScore = <span class="kw">when</span> { ctx.acceptedCount == <span class="nu">0</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> <span class="cm">// 首个候选无条件接受基础分</span> dist < <span class="nu">200</span> -> <span class="nu">0.0</span> <span class="cm">// 太近：几乎必然是引用</span> dist < <span class="nu">500</span> -> <span class="nu">0.4</span> dist < <span class="nu">2000</span> -> <span class="nu">0.9</span> dist < <span class="nu">20000</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.7</span> <span class="cm">// 过长：可能漏了章节，或分卷处</span> } score += distScore * W_DISTANCE <span class="kw">if</span> (distScore < <span class="nu">0.5</span> && ctx.acceptedCount > <span class="nu">0</span>) reasons += <span class="st">"距上章仅 ${dist} 字"</span> <span class="cm">// ── 信号 5：序号单调性（最强信号之一）────────────────</span> <span class="kw">val</span> monoScore = <span class="kw">if</span> (f.ordinal != <span class="kw">null</span> && ctx.lastOrdinal != <span class="kw">null</span>) { <span class="kw">when</span> { f.ordinal == ctx.lastOrdinal + <span class="nu">1</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> <span class="cm">// 完美递增</span> f.ordinal > ctx.lastOrdinal -> <span class="nu">0.85</span> <span class="cm">// 递增但跳跃（可能跳章）</span> f.ordinal == ctx.lastOrdinal -> <span class="nu">0.3</span> <span class="cm">// 重复（可能是分卷重置）</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">0.1</span> <span class="cm">// 倒退：可疑</span> } } <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (f.ordinal != <span class="kw">null</span> && ctx.lastOrdinal == <span class="kw">null</span>) { <span class="nu">0.9</span> } <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="nu">0.5</span> score += monoScore * W_ORDINAL_MONOTONIC <span class="kw">if</span> (f.ordinal != <span class="kw">null</span> && ctx.lastOrdinal != <span class="kw">null</span> && f.ordinal <= ctx.lastOrdinal) { reasons += <span class="st">"序号非递增(${ctx.lastOrdinal} → ${f.ordinal})"</span> } <span class="cm">// ── 信号 6：行尾无标点 ───────────────────────────</span> <span class="cm">// "他想起第一回见到她的时候。" 以句号结尾 → 是正文句子</span> <span class="kw">val</span> lastCh = f.trimmed.lastOrNull() <span class="kw">val</span> punctScore = <span class="kw">if</span> (lastCh != <span class="kw">null</span> && lastCh <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="st">"。！？；，、"</span>) <span class="nu">0.0</span> <span class="kw">else</span> <span class="nu">1.0</span> score += punctScore * W_NO_PUNCT_END <span class="kw">if</span> (punctScore == <span class="nu">0.0</span>) reasons += <span class="st">"以句末标点结尾，疑似正文"</span> <span class="kw">return</span> VoteResult(accepted = score >= THRESHOLD, score = score, reasons = reasons) } <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> VoteContext( <span class="kw">val</span> lastAcceptedOffset: Long = Long.MIN_VALUE, <span class="kw">val</span> lastOrdinal: Int? = <span class="kw">null</span>, <span class="kw">val</span> acceptedCount: Int = <span class="nu">0</span>, ) }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="b3-两阶段处理先粗筛再投票">B.3 两阶段处理：先粗筛，再投票</h3> <p>为了性能，<strong>不是每一行都跑 6 个信号</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>阶段 <span class="nu">1</span>（廉价预筛，处理 <span class="nu">18</span> 万行）： · 长度检查（> <span class="nu">60</span> 字直接跳过） · 首字符检查（不在预置字符集中直接跳过） · 命中率：约 <span class="nu">2</span> 万行进入阶段 <span class="nu">2</span>（<span class="nu">11</span>%） 阶段 <span class="nu">2</span>（正则匹配，处理 <span class="nu">2</span> 万行）： · 依次尝试 <span class="nu">5</span> 条内置规则 + 自定义规则 · 命中率：约 <span class="nu">2100</span> 行进入阶段 <span class="nu">3</span>（<span class="nu">10.5</span>%） 阶段 <span class="nu">3</span>（多信号投票，处理 <span class="nu">2100</span> 行）： · <span class="nu">6</span> 个信号打分 · 通过率：约 <span class="nu">2000</span> 行被接受（<span class="nu">95</span>%）</code></pre></div> <p><strong>性能漏斗</strong>：18 万 → 2 万 → 2100 → 2000。阶段 1 与 2 是纯 CPU 的字符串比较，<code>18万 × 3 次判断 ≈ 50 万次操作 < 10ms</code>。总耗时由阶段 2 的正则主导（2 万次 <code>matches</code>，约 250ms）。</p> <h3 id="b4-特殊处理分卷与序号重置">B.4 特殊处理：分卷与序号重置</h3> <p>网文的章节结构常有"卷"：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>卷一 少年游 第一章 醒来 第二章 入城 ... 第八十章 离乡 卷二 江湖路 第一章 遇袭 ← 序号从 <span class="nu">1</span> 重新开始！</code></pre></div> <p><strong>问题</strong>：<code>lastOrdinal</code> 单调性检查会把"卷二的 第一章"判为倒退（1 < 80），进而误判。</p> <p><strong>解决</strong>：检测到卷标题后，重置 <code>lastOrdinal</code>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 在 Voting 循环中</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (keywordType == KeywordType.VOLUME) { ctx = ctx.copy(lastOrdinal = <span class="kw">null</span>) <span class="cm">// ★ 卷标题后重置序号链</span> accepted += chapter(text, isVolume = <span class="kw">true</span>) } <span class="kw">else</span> { <span class="kw">val</span> vote = voter.vote(features, ctx) <span class="kw">if</span> (vote.accepted) { ctx = ctx.copy( lastAcceptedOffset = f.charOffset, lastOrdinal = f.ordinal ?: ctx.lastOrdinal, acceptedCount = ctx.acceptedCount + <span class="nu">1</span>, ) accepted += chapter(text, isVolume = <span class="kw">false</span>) } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>二级序号</strong>：更好的做法是用"层级序号"（卷序号.章序号）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> ChapterNumber(<span class="kw">val</span> volume: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> chapter: Int) { <span class="cm">/** 可比较的线性序号，用于单调性检查 */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> linear: Double <span class="kw">get</span>() = volume * <span class="nu">100000.0</span> + chapter <span class="kw">fun</span> next(): ChapterNumber = copy(chapter = chapter + <span class="nu">1</span>) }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="b5-误报抑制的额外规则">B.5 误报抑制的额外规则</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">误报模式</th><th style="text-align:left">检测</th><th style="text-align:left">处理</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">连续多行都是"第 N 章"（如目录页被误当正文）</td><td style="text-align:left">检测到 5 个以上连续候选且间隔均 < 100 字</td><td style="text-align:left">判定为"目录区"，跳过整个区域（记 <code>warnings</code>）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">同一标题在 5000 字内重复</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>title == lastTitle && dist < 5000</code></td><td style="text-align:left">跳过（页眉/页脚残留）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">"第 N 章"出现在段落中间（行首有缩进且行尾有标点）</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>leadingWhitespace == 0 && 行尾有标点</code></td><td style="text-align:left">投票得分已覆盖（信号 6）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">大量数字行（如数据表格类文本）</td><td style="text-align:left">单规则 <code>digit_only</code> 命中数 > 总行数 5%</td><td style="text-align:left">禁用 <code>digit_only</code> 规则，只保留 <code>cn_numbered</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>"目录区"检测实现</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">/** 检测连续的目录块：返回需要跳过的偏移范围 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> detectTocBlock(candidates: List<Features>): LongRange? { <span class="kw">var</span> runStart = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">for</span> (i <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">1</span>..candidates.size) { <span class="kw">val</span> broken = i == candidates.size || candidates[i].charOffset - candidates[i - <span class="nu">1</span>].charOffset > <span class="nu">100</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (broken) { <span class="kw">val</span> runLength = i - runStart <span class="kw">if</span> (runLength >= <span class="nu">5</span>) { <span class="cm">// 找到目录块：从 runStart 到 i-1</span> <span class="kw">return</span> candidates[runStart].charOffset..candidates[i - <span class="nu">1</span>].charOffset } runStart = i } } <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">null</span> }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="b6-测试向量">B.6 测试向量</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>测试语料（assets/test/chapterize/）： ┌────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────┬────────┬────────┐ │ # │ 描述 │ 期望章数│ 允许误差│ 断言 │ ├────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────┼────────┼────────┤ │ <span class="nu">1</span> │ 标准网文：第<span class="nu">1</span>-<span class="nu">2000</span>章，规整格式 │ <span class="nu">2000</span> │ <span class="nu">0</span> │ 精确 │ │ <span class="nu">2</span> │ 中文序号：第一章 / 第一百零三章 │ <span class="nu">103</span> │ <span class="nu">0</span> │ 精确 │ │ <span class="nu">3</span> │ 混合序号：第<span class="nu">1</span>章 第<span class="nu">2</span>章 第三章 │ <span class="nu">20</span> │ <span class="nu">1</span> │ ≥<span class="nu">19</span> │ │ <span class="nu">4</span> │ 带分卷：卷一卷二，各含 <span class="nu">1</span>-N 章 │ <span class="nu">122</span> │ <span class="nu">2</span> │ ≥<span class="nu">120</span> │ │ <span class="nu">5</span> │ 特殊章：序章 楔子 番外 完本感言 │ <span class="nu">8</span> │ │ <span class="nu">7</span> │ 正文含伪章节：「他想起第三章的那个人」 │ <span class="nu">100</span> │ <span class="nu">2</span> │ ≤<span class="nu">102</span> │ │ <span class="nu">8</span> │ 含目录页（前 <span class="nu">200</span> 行是目录，后是正文） │ <span class="nu">50</span> │ <span class="nu">5</span> │ ≤<span class="nu">55</span> │ │ <span class="nu">9</span> │ 无章节标识（纯正文） │ <span class="nu">1</span> │ <span "</span> + <span class="nu">80</span> 字标题） │ <span class="nu">100</span> │ <span class="nu">5</span> │ ≥<span class="nu">95</span> │ │ <span class="nu">12</span> │ 页眉残留（每 <span class="nu">3000</span> 字重复<span class="st">"第N章 书名"</span>） │ <span class="nu">100</span> │ <span class="nu">10</span> │ ≤<span class="nu">110</span> │ │ <span class="nu">13</span> │ 只有数字行（歌单/代码片段） │ <span class="nu">1</span> │ <span class="nu">0</span> │ 精确 │ │ <span class="nu">14</span> │ 章节间隔极小（每章仅 <span class="nu">50</span> 字） │ <span class="nu">500</span> │ <span class="nu">20</span> │ ≥<span class="nu">480</span> │ └────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────┴────────┴────────┘</code></pre></div> <p><strong>性能测试</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Test <span class="kw">fun</span> `10MB GBK 文件分章耗时达标`() { <span class="kw">val</span> <span class="kw">file</span> = corpus(<span class="st">"gbk_large.txt"</span>) <span class="cm">// 10MB，2000 章</span> <span class="cm">// 预热 3 次（JIT 编译）</span> repeat(<span class="nu">3</span>) { runBlocking { chapterizer.chapterize(<span class="kw">file</span>, Charset.forName(<span class="st">"GB18030"</span>), <span class="kw">null</span>) {} } } <span class="kw">val</span> elapsed = measureTimeMillis { runBlocking { chapterizer.chapterize(<span class="kw">file</span>, Charset.forName(<span class="st">"GB18030"</span>), <span class="kw">null</span>) {} } } println(<span class="st">"分章耗时: ${elapsed}ms"</span>) assertTrue(<span class="st">"分章耗时 ${elapsed}ms 超过 1500ms 预算"</span>, elapsed < <span class="nu">1500</span>) }</code></pre></div> <hr> <h2 id="53-算法卡-c物理分页的正确性保障">5.3 算法卡 C：物理分页的正确性保障</h2> <h3 id="c0-目标">C.0 目标</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">项</th><th style="text-align:left">要求</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">输入</td><td style="text-align:left">章文本 + 排版配置 + 视口尺寸</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">输出</td><td style="text-align:left">页列表（页间字符区间连续、无空洞、无重叠）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">正确性</td><td style="text-align:left">页区间严格连续覆盖 <code>[0, text.length)</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">首屏延迟</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 120ms（3000 字章节）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">全章分页</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 400ms（10 万字章节）</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="c1-不变量invariants">C.1 不变量（Invariants）</h3> <p>分页输出的正确性由以下不变量定义，<strong>必须写进单元测试</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>I1. 连续性：pages[i].endCharInChapter == pages[i+<span class="nu">1</span>].startCharInChapter I2. 覆盖性：pages[<span class="nu">0</span>].startCharInChapter == <span class="nu">0</span> I3. 完备性：pages.last().endCharInChapter == chapterText.length I4. 单调性：pages[i].startCharInChapter < pages[i].endCharInChapter（空页不允许，除空章节） I5. 不重叠：pages[i].endCharInChapter <= pages[i+<span class="nu">1</span>].startCharInChapter I6. 索引一致：pages[i].index == i I7. 容量约束：每页行数 × 行高 + 段间距 ≤ contentHeightPx + 单行高容差</code></pre></div> <p><strong>为什么 I1 和 I5 是分开的？</strong> I5（不重叠）是弱约束，I1（严格连续）是强约束。分页算法天然的失败模式是"丢行"——某一行因为高度计算舍入误差被跳过，导致 I1 违反但 I5 满足。<strong>必须断言 I1，而不只是 I5</strong>。</p> <h3 id="c2-断页的舍入误差问题">C.2 断页的舍入误差问题</h3> <p>浮点高度计算是分页最常见的 bug 来源：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// ❌ 危险：浮点累积误差导致最后一页可能放不下实际能放下的行</span> <span class="kw">var</span> accHeight = 0f <span class="kw">while</span> (...) { accHeight += line.heightPx <span class="cm">// 每行都有 0.0001 的误差</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (accHeight > contentHeightPx) <span class="kw">break</span> } <span class="cm">// ✅ 安全：用整数微单位（1/1000 px）累积，杜绝累积误差</span> <span class="kw">var</span> accHeightMicro = 0L <span class="cm">// 单位：1/1000 px</span> <span class="kw">val</span> limitMicro = (contentHeightPx * <span class="nu">1000</span>).roundToLong() <span class="kw">while</span> (lineIdx < lines.size) { <span class="kw">val</span> needMicro = (lines[lineIdx].heightPx * <span class="nu">1000</span>).roundToLong() + <span class="kw">if</span> (lines[lineIdx].isParagraphEnd) (paraSpacingPx * <span class="nu">1000</span>).roundToLong() <span class="kw">else</span> 0L <span class="kw">if</span> (accHeightMicro + needMicro > limitMicro && lineIdx > pageStartLine) { <span class="cm">// 收页</span> accHeightMicro = 0L <span class="kw">continue</span> } accHeightMicro += needMicro lineIdx++ }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>更根本的保障</strong>：允许 1 行的"容差溢出"——如果因为舍入导致最后一页只放了 1 行而下一行其实能放，会显得很突兀。加一个 <code>TOLERANCE_RATIO = 0.02</code> 容差：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">val</span> toleranceMicro = (contentHeightPx * <span class="nu">1000</span> * <span class="nu">0.02</span>).roundToLong() <span class="kw">if</span> (accHeightMicro + needMicro > limitMicro + toleranceMicro && lineIdx > pageStartLine) { <span class="cm">// 收页</span> }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="c3-中文标点避头尾可选增强v1x">C.3 中文标点避头尾（可选增强，V1.x）</h3> <p>中文排版规范要求：<strong>行首不能出现 <code>。，、；：！？）》」』</code> 等标点，行尾不能出现 <code>（《「『</code> 等</strong>。</p> <p>但这个需求在<strong>分页场景下不适用</strong>——因为断行已由 <code>TextMeasurer</code> 处理完毕（Compose 已实现基本的标点挤压与避头尾），分页只是在行间插页。因此：</p> <p><strong>结论：V1.0 不自行实现避头尾，依赖平台文本排版能力。</strong> 若发现平台表现不佳（如 Android 8~10 上句号出现在行首），在 <code>TextStyle</code> 上加 <code>lineBreak = LineBreak.Paragraph</code> 或对文本做<strong>预替换</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">/** * 预替换法：把"会出现在行首的标点"替换为"标点+不换行空格"， * 强制标点跟随前一个字。 * 仅在检测到平台表现异常时启用（API < 29）。 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> applyPunctuationHanging(text: String): String { <span class="kw">val</span> NO_LINE_START = <span class="st">"。，、；：！？）》」』】…—"</span> <span class="kw">return</span> text.map { ch -> <span class="kw">if</span> (ch <span class="kw">in</span> NO_LINE_START) <span class="st">"$ch\u00A0"</span> <span class="kw">else</span> ch.toString() <span class="cm">// \u00A0 不换行空格</span> }.joinToString(<span class="st">""</span>) }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p>⚠️ <strong>这个替换会改变字符数</strong>，进而破坏字符偏移的连续性！如果启用，必须在分页前做替换、并在 <code>charOffset</code> 记录时<strong>用原始文本的偏移</strong>。所以：<code>startCharInChapter</code> 的计算必须基于<strong>原始文本</strong>，而不是替换后的文本。实现上要维护"替换后下标 → 原始下标"的映射表。<strong>这是 V1.0 不做此优化的另一个原因</strong>——复杂度收益比不划算。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="c4-空章节与极端输入">C.4 空章节与极端输入</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">输入</th><th style="text-align:left">期望行为</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">空文本 <code>""</code></td><td style="text-align:left">返回 1 个空页（<code>start=0, end=0</code>），UI 显示"本章无内容"</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">纯空白 <code>"\n\n\n"</code></td><td style="text-align:left">同上</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">单字符 <code>"一"</code></td><td style="text-align:left">1 页</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">无换行的 10 万字（一整段）</td><td style="text-align:left">正常分页（<code>measure</code> 一次返回全部行）；注意 <code>Constraints</code> 高度给 <code>Infinity</code>，否则会被截断</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">视口高度为 0（未测量完成）</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>立即返回空页列表并标记 <code>notReady</code></strong>，不抛异常；等视口就绪后重新触发</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">排版参数非法（字号 0）</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>TypographyRepository.sanitize</code> 已拦截；防御性再 <code>coerceIn</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>视口高度为 0 的处理</strong>（这是真实会发生的：Compose 首次组合时 <code>size</code> 为 <code>IntSize.Zero</code>）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">if</span> (viewport.height <= <span class="nu">0</span> || viewport.width <= <span class="nu">0</span>) { emit(PaginationBatch(emptyList(), isComplete = <span class="kw">false</span>, <span class="nu">0</span>)) <span class="kw">return</span>@flow <span class="cm">// 等下次 viewport 变更再分页</span> }</code></pre></div> <p>在 ViewModel 侧，viewport 用 <code>snapshotFlow</code> 监听，<code>filter { it.height > 0 }</code> 过滤掉无效值。</p> <h3 id="c5-测试向量">C.5 测试向量</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Test <span class="kw">fun</span> `分页不变量全部成立`() { <span class="kw">val</span> text = loremIpsum(<span class="nu">3000</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> typo = TypographyConfig.Default repeat(<span class="nu">5</span>) { warmup() } <span
