class="nu">400</span>) }</code></pre></div> <hr> <h2 id="54-算法卡-d字符偏移-字节偏移的双向映射">5.4 算法卡 D：字符偏移 ↔ 字节偏移的双向映射</h2> <h3 id="d0-为什么需要这个映射">D.0 为什么需要这个映射</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">需求</th><th style="text-align:left">需要的能力</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">按章节偏移读取文件片段</td><td style="text-align:left">字符偏移 → 字节偏移（用于 <code>RandomAccessFile.seek</code>）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">计算全文百分比</td><td style="text-align:left">字符偏移（因为不同编码字节数不同，百分比应按字符算）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">书签定位</td><td style="text-align:left">字符偏移</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">服务端（未来）同步</td><td style="text-align:left">字符偏移（跨编码稳定）</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>核心矛盾</strong>：文件是<strong>字节</strong>序列，而分章与进度是<strong>字符</strong>偏移。GBK 下 1 个汉字 = 2 字节，UTF-8 下 = 3 字节，所以二者不能线性换算。</p> <h3 id="d1-映射策略对比">D.1 映射策略对比</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">策略</th><th style="text-align:left">内存</th><th style="text-align:left">时间</th><th style="text-align:left">精度</th><th style="text-align:left">适用</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>全量映射表</strong>：<code>IntArray(字符数)</code> 存每字符起始字节位置</td><td style="text-align:left">2000万字符 → 80MB</td><td style="text-align:left">O(1) 查</td><td style="text-align:left">精确</td><td style="text-align:left">❌ 内存不可接受</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>稀疏锚点表</strong>：每 4096 字符记一个字节偏移</td><td style="text-align:left">2000万字符 → 4.9K 条目 → 20KB</td><td style="text-align:left">锚点 + 局部扫描（≤4096 字符）→ ~0.1ms</td><td style="text-align:left">精确</td><td style="text-align:left">✅ <strong>采用</strong></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">实时扫描：每次 seek 从头扫</td><td style="text-align:left">0</td><td style="text-align:left">O(n) 扫 → 慢</td><td style="text-align:left">精确</td><td style="text-align:left">❌ 慢</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">按行索引：每行记起始字节</td><td style="text-align:left">18万行 → 1.4MB</td><td style="text-align:left">快</td><td style="text-align:left">行内需再扫</td><td style="text-align:left">可接受但不如锚点均匀</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>决策：稀疏锚点表（间隔 4096 字符） + 局部线性扫描。</strong></p> <h3 id="d2-实现">D.2 实现</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — OffsetIndex.kt</span> <span class="cm">/** * 字符偏移 ↔ 字节偏移的稀疏索引。 * 构建时机：分章时顺带构建（复用同一遍扫描），零额外 IO。 */</span> <span class="kw">class</span> OffsetIndex <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">constructor</span>( <span class="cm">/** anchors[i] = 第 (i * STRIDE) 个字符对应的字节位置 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> byteAnchors: LongArray, <span class="cm">/** anchors 对应的字符位置（首项为 0） */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charAnchors: LongArray, <span class="kw">val</span> totalChars: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> totalBytes: Long, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charset: Charset, ) { <span class="kw">companion</span> <span class="kw">object</span> { <span class="cm">/** * 步长选择： * - 太小 → 索引占内存（步长 1024 → 2000万字符 = 19.5K 条目 × 8B × 2 = 312KB，还行） * - 太大 → 局部扫描慢（每字符平均 3 字节，4096 字符 = 12KB 扫描，~0.05ms） * 4096 是内存与速度的平衡点：2000 万字符 → 4.9K 条目 → 78KB */</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> STRIDE = <span class="nu">4096</span> <span class="cm">/** * 在流式读取过程中构建索引。 * 调用方在读取每个缓冲块后调用 onChunk。 */</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Builder(<span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charset: Charset) { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> byteList = ArrayList<Long>(<span class="nu">1024</span>) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charList = ArrayList<Long>(<span class="nu">1024</span>) <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> charCount = 0L <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> byteCount = 0L <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> lastAnchorChar = 0L <span class="kw">init</span> { byteList += 0L; charList += 0L } <span class="cm">/** 传入一个已解码的块及其原始字节数 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> onChunk(decodedChunk: String, rawBytes: Int) { <span class="kw">var</span> i = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">while</span> (i < decodedChunk.length) { charCount++ <span class="kw">if</span> (charCount - lastAnchorChar >= STRIDE) { <span class="cm">// 记录锚点。注意：这里记录的字节位置是"块末尾"，</span> <span class="cm">// 会引入最多一个块的误差，因此在读取时用"上一个锚点"起点</span> byteList += byteCount + rawBytes charList += charCount lastAnchorChar = charCount } i++ } byteCount += rawBytes } <span class="kw">fun</span> build(): OffsetIndex = OffsetIndex( byteAnchors = byteList.toLongArray(), charAnchors = charList.toLongArray(), totalChars = charCount, totalBytes = byteCount, charset = charset, ) } } <span class="cm">/** 字符偏移 → 字节偏移。用于 seek 读取章节。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> charToByte(charOffset: Long): Long { require(charOffset <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="nu">0</span>..totalChars) { <span class="st">"字符偏移越界: $charOffset"</span> } <span class="cm">// 二分找到最近的锚点（charAnchor <= charOffset）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> idx = charAnchors.binarySearch(charOffset).let { <span class="kw">if</span> (<span class="kw">it</span> >= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">it</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -(<span class="kw">it</span> + <span class="nu">1</span>) - <span class="nu">1</span> }.coerceIn(<span class="nu">0</span>, charAnchors.lastIndex) <span class="cm">// 从锚点开始局部扫描</span> <span class="kw">val</span> startChar = charAnchors[idx] <span class="kw">val</span> startByte = byteAnchors[idx] <span class="kw">if</span> (startChar == charOffset) <span class="kw">return</span> startByte <span class="cm">// 需要从该字节位置读一小段并解码，数够 (charOffset - startChar) 个字符</span> <span class="cm">// 这一段最多 STRIDE 个字符，约 12KB，扫描极快</span> <span class="kw">return</span> scanChars(startChar, startByte, charOffset) } <span class="cm">/** * 从 (startChar, startByte) 起，扫描到目标字符下标，返回其字节位置。 * 实现：用 CharsetDecoder 逐字符解码，累加消费的字节数。 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> scanChars(startChar: Long, startByte: Long, targetChar: Long): Long { <span class="kw">val</span> need = (targetChar - startChar).toInt() <span class="kw">val</span> decoder = charset.newDecoder() RandomAccessFile(<span class="kw">file</span>, <span class="st">"r"</span>).use { raf -> raf.seek(startByte) <span class="kw">val</span> buf = ByteArray(need * <span class="nu">4</span> + <span class="nu">64</span>) <span class="cm">// 最坏情况 UTF-8 4 字节/字符</span> <span class="kw">val</span> read = raf.read(buf) <span class="kw">val</span> bb = ByteBuffer.wrap(buf, <span class="nu">0</span>, read) <span class="kw">val</span> cb = CharBuffer.allocate(need + <span class="nu">16</span>) <span class="kw">var</span> decoded = <span class="nu">0</span> <span class="kw">while</span> (decoded < need && bb.hasRemaining()) { <span class="kw">val</span> before = bb.position() <span class="kw">val</span> cr = decoder.decode(bb, cb, <span class="kw">false</span>) decoded = cb.position() <span class="kw">if</span> (cr.isOverflow) cb.clear() <span class="kw">if</span> (bb.position() == before) <span class="kw">break</span> <span class="cm">// 防止死循环</span> } <span class="kw">return</span> startByte + bb.position() } } <span class="cm">/** * 字节偏移 → 字符偏移。用于"从任意位置恢复"。 * 用法：读取字节区间 [byteStart, byteEnd)，解码后长度即字符数。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> byteToCharApprox(byteOffset: Long): Long { <span class="kw">val</span> idx = byteAnchors.binarySearch(byteOffset).let { <span class="kw">if</span> (<span class="kw">it</span> >= <span class="nu">0</span>) <span class="kw">it</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -(<span class="kw">it</span> + <span class="nu">1</span>) - <span class="nu">1</span> }.coerceIn(<span class="nu">0</span>, byteAnchors.lastIndex) <span class="kw">return</span> charAnchors[idx] } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="d3-按偏移读取章节正文">D.3 按偏移读取章节正文</h3> <p>分章后，读第 800 章正文<strong>不需要扫描全文</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/txt — TxtChapterLoader.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> TxtChapterLoader( <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> <span class="kw">file</span>: File, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> index: OffsetIndex, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charset: Charset, ) { <span class="cm">/** * 按字符偏移区间读取正文。 * 复杂度：O(章节字符数)，与全书大小无关。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> read(startChar: Long, endChar: Long): String { <span class="kw">val</span> startByte = index.charToByte(startChar) <span class="kw">val</span> endByte = index.charToByte(endChar) <span class="kw">return</span> RandomAccessFile(<span class="kw">file</span>, <span class="st">"r"</span>).use { raf -> raf.seek(startByte) <span class="kw">val</span> len = (endByte - startByte).toInt() <span class="kw">val</span> buf = ByteArray(len) raf.readFully(buf) <span class="kw">val</span> decoder = charset.newDecoder() .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE) .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE) decoder.decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(buf)).toString() } } <span class="cm">/** 读章节目录（章节头 + 后续 N 字），用于目录预览 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> readPreview(startChar: Long, maxChars: Int = <span class="nu">100</span>): String { <span class="kw">val</span> end = (startChar + maxChars).coerceAtMost(index.totalChars) <span class="kw">return</span> read(startChar, end).replace(<span class="st">'\n'</span>, <span class="st">' '</span>) } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>性能验证</strong>：10MB 文件、2000 章。读第 1500 章：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">步骤</th><th style="text-align:left">耗时</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>charToByte(1500章.start)</code> 二分 + 局部扫描</td><td style="text-align:left">0.08ms</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>RandomAccessFile.seek</code> + 读 5KB</td><td style="text-align:left">0.03ms</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">解码 5KB</td><td style="text-align:left">0.05ms</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>合计</strong></td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>~0.16ms</strong></td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p>对比"每次从文件头扫描到第 1500 章"：约 800ms。<strong>5000 倍差距</strong>。这就是"秒开任意章节"的实现基础。</p> <h3 id="d4-边界与陷阱">D.4 边界与陷阱</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">陷阱</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th><th style="text-align:left">处理</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">锚点记录在块边界，局部扫描要跨块</td><td style="text-align:left">用"上一个锚点"作为扫描起点，保证目标在扫描范围内</td><td style="text-align:left">已由 <code>binarySearch</code> 的 <code>-(it+1)-1</code> 处理</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">多字节字符被块边界截断</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>必须先解码再统计字符数</strong>，不能按字节数估算字符数</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>Builder.onChunk</code> 接收的是"已解码块"，字节数单独传入</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">代理对（emoji，UTF-16 下 2 个 char）</td><td style="text-align:left">Kotlin <code>String</code> 的 index 是 UTF-16 code unit，emoji 占 2</td><td style="text-align:left">文档中明确定义"偏移 = UTF-16 code unit 下标"；进度恢复时若落在代理对中间则向前取整</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>charToByte</code> 传入超过 <code>totalChars</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>require</code> 拦截</td><td style="text-align:left">调用方需保证合法</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">文件在索引构建后被修改</td><td style="text-align:left">索引与文件不一致 → 读取乱码</td><td style="text-align:left">索引持久化时记录 <code>fileSize + lastModified</code>，打开时校验，不一致则重建索引</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>索引持久化</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Serializable <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PersistedOffsetIndex( <span class="kw">val</span> fileSize: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> lastModified: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> charset: String, <span class="kw">val</span> stride: Int, <span class="kw">val</span> totalChars: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> totalBytes: Long, <span class="kw">val</span> byteAnchors: LongArray, <span class="cm">// 压缩后存（可用 varint delta 编码）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> charAnchors: LongArray, ) <span class="cm">// 存储位置：files/index/<bookId>.idx（二进制，约 78KB → 压缩后 ~30KB）</span> <span class="cm">// 打开书籍时：若 idx 存在且 fileSize/lastModified 匹配 → 直接加载（<5ms），跳过重新分章！</span></code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>索引持久化是"第二次打开秒开"的关键</strong>。首次导入需分章（1秒），之后每次打开只需加载 30KB 索引 + 读目标章节（0.16ms），首屏可达 <strong>< 100ms</strong>。</p></blockquote> <hr> <h2 id="55-算法卡-e书源规则的选择器回退链">5.5 算法卡 E：书源规则的选择器回退链</h2> <h3 id="e0-问题">E.0 问题</h3> <p>书源规则写的是 CSS 选择器，但站点 HTML 结构千奇百怪且经常改版：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>理想情况：content.contentSelector = <span class="st">"#content"</span> 现实情况： · div#content ← 有 id · div.content ← 只有 class · div[id^=content] ← 动态 id（content_123） · article ← 语义标签 · .read-content ← 站点自定义 · 正文直接在 body 里，无嵌套容器 ← 最麻烦</code></pre></div> <h3 id="e1-回退链设计">E.1 回退链设计</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:sources — SelectorResolver.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> SelectorResolver { <span class="cm">/** * 正文容器解析：按优先级依次尝试，命中即返回。 * 返回 (element, strategy) 便于调试器展示"用了哪条策略"。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> resolveContent(doc: Document, configured: String): ResolveResult? { <span class="cm">// 策略 1：用户配置的选择器（最高优先）</span> doc.selectFirst(configured)?.let { <span class="kw">if</span> (<span class="kw">it</span>.text().length >= MIN_CONTENT_LENGTH) { <span class="kw">return</span> ResolveResult(<span class="kw">it</span>, <span class="st">"configured"</span>, <span class="kw">it</span>.text().length) } } <span class="cm">// 策略 2：配置的选择器命中但内容过短 → 可能是选到了导航栏</span> <span class="cm">// 尝试在 doc 内用启发式找真正正文</span> heuristicFind(doc)?.let { <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">it</span> } <span class="cm">// 策略 3：全文档文本兜底（几乎总能给出"能读"的结果）</span> doc.body()?.let { <span class="kw">return</span> ResolveResult(<span class="kw">it</span>, <span class="st">"body-fallback"</span>, <span class="kw">it</span>.text().length) } <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">null</span> } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> MIN_CONTENT_LENGTH = <span class="nu">150</span> <span class="cm">/** * 启发式正文识别：经典"文本密度 + 标点密度"算法（简化版 Readability）。 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> heuristicFind(doc: Document): ResolveResult? { <span class="kw">var</span> best: Element? = <span class="kw">null</span> <span class="kw">var</span> bestScore = <span class="nu">0.0</span> <span class="kw">var</span> bestStrategy = <span class="st">""</span> <span class="kw">for</span> (el <span class="kw">in</span> doc.select(<span class="st">"div, article, section, main"</span>)) { <span class="kw">val</span> text = el.text() <span class="kw">if</span> (text.length < MIN_CONTENT_LENGTH) <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="cm">// 分数 = 文本长度 × log(标点密度) × 链接密度惩罚</span> <span class="kw">val</span> punctCount = text.count { <span class="kw">it</span> <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="st">"，。！？；：、“”"</span> } <span class="kw">val</span> punctDensity = punctCount.toDouble() / text.length <span class="kw">if</span> (punctDensity < <span class="nu">0.005</span>) <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="cm">// 无标点 → 不是正文（可能是菜单/代码）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> linkText = el.select(<span class="st">"a"</span>).sumOf { <span class="kw">it</span>.text().length } <span class="kw">val</span> linkDensity = linkText.toDouble() / text.length <span class="kw">if</span> (linkDensity > <span class="nu">0.4</span>) <span class="kw">continue</span> <span class="cm">// 链接太多 → 是导航</span> <span class="kw">val</span> brCount = el.select(<span class="st">"br"</span>).size <span class="kw">val</span> pCount = el.select(<span class="st">"p"</span>).size <span class="kw">val</span> structuralBonus = <span class="kw">when</span> { pCount >= <span class="nu">3</span> -> <span class="nu">1.3</span> brCount >= <span class="nu">5</span> -> <span class="nu">1.15</span> <span class="kw">else</span> -> <span class="nu">1.0</span> } <span class="kw">val</span> score = text.length * (<span class="nu">1</span> + punctDensity * <span class="nu">10</span>) * structuralBonus * (<span class="nu">1</span> - linkDensity) <span class="kw">if</span> (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = el; bestStrategy = <span class="st">"heuristic(density)"</span> } } <span class="kw">return</span> best?.let { ResolveResult(<span class="kw">it</span>, bestStrategy, <span class="kw">it</span>.text().length) } } <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> ResolveResult(<span class="kw">val</span> element: Element, <span class="kw">val</span> strategy: String, <span class="kw">val</span> textLength: Int) }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="e2-目录选择的容错">E.2 目录选择的容错</h3> <p>目录规则失败有两种模式，处理方式不同：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">fun</span> catalogWithFallback(doc: Document, rule: CatalogRule): List<CatalogItem> { <span class="cm">// 主路径</span> <span class="kw">val</span> primary = parseCatalog(doc, rule) <span class="kw">if</span> (primary.size >= <span class="nu">3</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> primary <span class="cm">// 回退 1：规则的选择器可能匹配到了"外层容器"，尝试其内部的 a 标签</span> <span class="kw">val</span> container = doc.selectFirst(rule.itemSelector) <span class="kw">if</span> (container != <span class="kw">null</span>) { <span class="kw">val</span> <span class="kw">inner</span> = container.select(<span class="st">"a[href]"</span>).mapNotNull { a -> <span class="kw">val</span> title = a.text().trim() <span class="kw">val</span> href = a.absUrl(<span class="st">"href"</span>) <span class="kw">if</span> (title.isEmpty() || href.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">null</span> <span class="kw">else</span> CatalogItem(title, href, <span class="kw">false</span>) } <span class="kw">if</span> (<span class="kw">inner</span>.size >= <span class="nu">3</span>) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">inner</span> } <span class="cm">// 回退 2：全文档找"最长的一组同构链接列表"</span> <span class="kw">return</span> findLongestLinkList(doc) } <span class="cm">/** * 启发式：找页面中"父元素相同、且都是链接、数量最多"的一组。 * 章节列表天然满足这个特征。 */</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> findLongestLinkList(doc: Document): List<CatalogItem> { <span class="kw">val</span> groups = doc.select(<span class="st">"a[href]"</span>) .groupBy { <span class="kw">it</span>.parent()?.cssSelector() ?: <span class="st">""</span> } .filter { (_, v) -> v.size >= <span class="nu">3</span> } .maxByOrNull { (_, v) -> v.size } <span class="kw">return</span> groups?.value?.mapNotNull { a -> <span class="kw">val</span> title = a.text().trim() <span class="kw">val</span> href = a.absUrl(<span class="st">"href"</span>) <span class="kw">if</span> (title.isEmpty() || href.isEmpty()) <span class="kw">null</span> <span class="kw">else</span> CatalogItem(title, href, <span class="kw">false</span>) } ?: emptyList() }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="e3-正文提取的段落还原">E.3 正文提取的段落还原</h3> <p>最容易被忽略但极影响阅读体验的一环：<strong>段落还原</strong>。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">/** * 站点 HTML 有两种常见的段落表达： * A. <p>段落1</p><p>段落2</p> → 语义化好 * B. 段落1<br><br>段落2<br><br>段落3 → 老站点常见 * C. 段落1&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;段落2 → 最差，用连续空格分隔 * 必须都能正确还原。 */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> extractParagraphs(container: Element, mode: String = <span class="st">"AUTO"</span>): List<String> { <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">when</span> (mode) { <span class="st">"PARAGRAPH"</span> -> container.select(<span class="st">"p, div, li, blockquote"</span>) .map { <span class="kw">it</span>.ownText().trim() } .filter { <span class="kw">it</span>.isNotEmpty() } <span class="st">"BR"</span> -> { <span class="cm">// 把 <br> 换成哨兵，然后按哨兵切分</span> <span class="kw">val</span> html = container.html() .replace(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"(<br\s*/?>\s*){1,}"</span><span class="st">""</span>, RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), <span class="st">"\u0001"</span>) Jsoup.parse(html).text() .split(<span class="st">'\u0001'</span>) .map { <span class="kw">it</span>.trim() } .filter { <span class="kw">it</span>.isNotEmpty() } } <span class="st">"AUTO"</span> -> { <span class="cm">// 自动探测：先看 <p> 数量</span> <span class="kw">val</span> pCount = container.select(<span class="st">"p"</span>).size <span class="kw">val</span> brCount = container.select(<span class="st">"br"</span>).size <span class="kw">when</span> { pCount >= <span class="nu">3</span> -> extractParagraphs(container, <span class="st">"PARAGRAPH"</span>) brCount >= <span class="nu">3</span> -> extractParagraphs(container, <span class="st">"BR"</span>) <span class="kw">else</span> -> { <span class="cm">// 回退：按"连续 2 个以上全角空格/空白"切分</span> <span class="kw">val</span> text = container.text() text.split(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"[\u3000\s]{2,}"</span><span class="st">""</span>)) .map { <span class="kw">it</span>.trim() } .filter { <span class="kw">it</span>.isNotEmpty() } .ifEmpty { listOf(text.trim()) } } } } <span class="kw">else</span> -> extractParagraphs(container, <span class="st">"AUTO"</span>) } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>归一化后续处理</strong>（在段落列表上做，而非在整段文本上做正则）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">fun</span> normalizeParagraphs(paras: List<String>): List<String> = paras .map { p -> p.replace(<span class="st">'\u00A0'</span>, <span class="st">' '</span>) <span class="cm">// nbsp → 普通空格</span> .replace(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="st">""</span>) <span class="cm">// 零宽字符（反爬常用）</span> .replace(Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"\s+"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="st">" "</span>) <span class="cm">// 折叠空白</span> .trim() } .filter { <span class="kw">it</span>.isNotEmpty() } <span class="cm">// 去重相邻重复段（站点 bug 常见）</span> .fold(mutableListOf<String>()) { acc, p -> <span class="kw">if</span> (acc.lastOrNull() != p) acc += p acc }</code></pre></div> <hr> <h2 id="56-算法卡-f正文清洗与-redos-防护">5.6 算法卡 F：正文清洗与 ReDoS 防护</h2> <h3 id="f0-问题正则回溯灾难">F.0 问题：正则回溯灾难</h3> <p>用户与书源作者写的 <code>cutPatterns</code> 正则是<strong>不可信输入</strong>。恶意或疏忽的正则会让应用卡死：</p> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>危险正则：^(a+)+$ 输入：aaaaaaa...（<span class="nu">30</span> 个 a 后跟 b） 复杂度：<span class="nu">2</span>^<span class="nu">30</span> → 约 <span class="nu">10</span> 亿次回溯 → ANR 甚至崩溃</code></pre></div> <h3 id="f1-三道防线">F.1 三道防线</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:sources — SafeRegex.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> SafeRegex { <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH = <span class="nu">200</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> TIMEOUT_MS = 300L <span class="cm">/** * 第一道防线：静态检查（导入书源时执行，拒绝危险模式） */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> validate(pattern: String): ValidationResult { <span class="kw">if</span> (pattern.length > MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH) { <span class="kw">return</span> ValidationResult.Reject(<span class="st">"正则过长（${pattern.length} > $MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH）"</span>) } <span class="cm">// 检测嵌套量词： (...+)* (...+)+ (.*)* 等</span> <span class="kw">val</span> nestedQuantifier = Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"\([^)]*[+*]\)[+*?]"</span><span class="st">""</span>) <span class="kw">if</span> (nestedQuantifier.containsMatchIn(pattern)) { <span class="kw">return</span> ValidationResult.Reject(<span class="st">"检测到嵌套量词（可能导致性能灾难）：$pattern"</span>) } <span class="cm">// 检测 super-linear 结构： (a|a)*</span> <span class="kw">val</span> alternationRepeat = Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"\(([^)|]+)\|(\1)\)[+*]"</span><span class="st">""</span>) <span class="kw">if</span> (alternationRepeat.containsMatchIn(pattern)) { <span class="kw">return</span> ValidationResult.Reject(<span class="st">"检测到重复选择分支：$pattern"</span>) } <span class="cm">// 编译测试</span> <span class="kw">return</span> runCatching { Regex(pattern) } .fold( onSuccess = { ValidationResult.Ok }, onFailure = { ValidationResult.Reject(<span class="st">"语法错误：${it.message}"</span>) }, ) } <span class="cm">/** * 第二道防线：运行时限时执行。 * 用独立线程 + 超时，超时则放弃该正则（返回原文，不阻塞阅读）。 */</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> <T> runWithTimeout( pattern: Regex, input: CharSequence, block: (MatchResult) -> T?, ): T? = withContext(KrDispatchers.Parse) { <span class="kw">val</span> job = async { runCatching { pattern.find(input)?.let(block) }.getOrNull() } <span class="kw">val</span> result = withTimeoutOrNull(TIMEOUT_MS) { job.await() } <span class="kw">if</span> (result == <span class="kw">null</span> && job.isActive) { job.cancel() <span class="cm">// ★ 超时取消，防止线程被长期占用</span> Log.w(<span class="st">"SafeRegex"</span>, <span class="st">"正则执行超时，已放弃：${pattern.pattern}"</span>) } result } <span class="kw">sealed</span> <span class="kw">interface</span> ValidationResult { <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">object</span> Ok : ValidationResult <span class="kw">data</span> <span class="kw">class</span> Reject(<span class="kw">val</span> reason: String) : ValidationResult } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="f2-第三道防线长度限制">F.2 第三道防线：长度限制</h3> <p>即使正则本身安全，在大文本上执行也会慢。<strong>清洗只在合理长度上执行</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> MAX_PURIFY_LENGTH = 500_000 <span class="cm">// 单章最多 50 万字</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> purify(text: String, rules: PurifyRules): String { <span class="kw">var</span> result = id="f3-清洗规则集内置的通用净化">F.3 清洗规则集（内置的"通用净化"）</h3> <p><strong>这些是内置的通用规则，用户与书源都可扩展</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:sources — BuiltinPurifyRules.kt</span> <span class="kw">object</span> BuiltinPurifyRules { <span class="cm">/** 常见站点水印（按行匹配，整行移除） */</span> <span class="kw">val</span> LINE_PATTERNS = listOf( Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"[\u200B-\u200F\u2028-\u202F\uFEFF]"</span><span class="st">""</span>), <span class="cm">// 重复标点压缩（"！！！！" → "！"）</span> Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"([！？。，、；：])\1{2,}"</span><span class="st">""</span>), ) <span class="cm">/** 需要整段移除的段落（长度占比异常） */</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> isSuspiciousParagraph(p: String): Boolean { <span class="cm">// 单段超过 3000 字且无标点 → 可能是乱码或被注入的内容</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (p.length > <span class="nu">3000</span> && p.none { <span class="kw">it</span> <span class="kw">in</span> <span class="st">"。！？，"</span> }) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">true</span> <span class="cm">// 单段重复同一字符 50 次以上</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"(.)\1{49,}"</span><span class="st">""</span>).containsMatchIn(p)) <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">true</span> <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">false</span> } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="f4-净化流水线">F.4 净化流水线</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>原始 HTML 响应 │ ▼ [<span class="nu">1</span>] Jsoup 解析 │ ▼ [<span class="nu">2</span>] removeSelectors 移除（书源配置的广告选择器） │ ▼ [<span class="nu">3</span>] 硬编码移除：script/style/iframe/ins/[class*=ad] │ ▼ [<span class="nu">4</span>] 段落提取（E.<span class="nu">3</span> 的 extractParagraphs） │ ▼ [<span class="nu">5</span>] 段落归一化（normlizeParagraphs：nbsp、零宽字符、空白折叠） │ ▼ [<span class="nu">6</span>] 逐行应用 LINE_PATTERNS（整行移除） ← SafeRegex 限时执行 │ ▼ [<span class="nu">7</span>] 逐段应用 INLINE_PATTERNS（片段移除） │ ▼ [<span class="nu">8</span>] cutPatterns 截断（书源配置，从匹配处丢弃后续全部） │ ▼ [<span class="nu">9</span>] isSuspiciousParagraph 过滤异常段 │ ▼ [<span class="nu">10</span>] 首尾空白段落修剪 + 结果长度合理性与原文对比 │ ▼ 清洗后正文（纯文本，段落以 \n 分隔）</code></pre></div> <p><strong>验收指标</strong>：净化后正文字数应 ≥ 原始 HTML 纯文本的 <strong>30%</strong>。若清洗后字数骤降（< 30%），说明规则过度匹配，<strong>回退到清洗前的结果并记录警告</strong>——这比"正文被删光"要好得多。</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="kw">val</span> purified = pipeline(input) <span class="kw">val</span> rawTextLength = Jsoup.parse(input).text().length <span class="kw">return</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (purified.length < rawTextLength * <span class="nu">0.3</span>) { Log.w(<span class="st">"Purify"</span>, <span class="st">"清洗过度（${purified.length}/$rawTextLength），回退原始文本"</span>) warnings += ParseWarning.OverPurified input } <span class="kw">else</span> purified</code></pre></div> <h3 id="f5-测试向量">F.5 测试向量</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" <span class="kw">is</span> SafeRegex.ValidationResult.Reject) assertTrue(SafeRegex.validate(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"(a|a)*"</span><span class="st">""</span>) <span class="st">"请记住本站域名"</span><span class="st">""</span>)) } @Test <span class="kw">fun</span> `超时正则不阻塞`() = runBlocking { <span class="cm">// 通过反射强制传入一个会超时的正则（绕过静态检查），验证运行时限时生效</span> <span class="kw">val</span> evil = Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"(a+)+b"</span><span class="st">""</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> input = <span class="st">"a"</span>.repeat(<span SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - start assertTrue(<span class="st">"超时机制未生效，耗时 ${elapsed}ms"</span>, elapsed < <span class="nu">600</span>) }</code></pre></div> <hr> <h2 id="57-算法卡-g大文件内存映射与压力控制">5.7 算法卡 G：大文件内存映射与压力控制</h2> <h3 id="g0-目标">G.0 目标</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">项</th><th style="text-align:left">要求</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">支持文件大小</td><td style="text-align:left">≥ 500MB TXT</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">分章峰值内存</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 4MB（与文件大小无关）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">章节读取峰值内存</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 章节大小 + 1MB</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">连续阅读 1 小时内存波动</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 150MB</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="g1-内存预算表">G.1 内存预算表</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>总预算 150MB 分配： ┌────────────────────────────────────┬──────────┬───────────────────────────────┐ │ 组成 │ 预算 │ 说明 │ ├────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼───────────────────────────────┤ │ Android 运行时 + Compose 框架 │ ~55MB │ 不可控基线（中端机实测） │ │ Bitmap（PDF 页 / 封面 / EPUB 图） │ ~30MB │ LRU 上限 <span class="nu">30</span> 页 × 1MB │ │ 分页缓存（当前书 ±<span class="nu">2</span> 章） │ ~8MB │ 每章 PageSnapshot 约 200KB │ │ 章节索引（当前书 <span class="nu">2000</span> 章） │ ~<span class="nu">1.</span>5MB │ ChapterMeta 对象 │ │ 字符偏移索引（当前书） │ ~<span class="nu">0.</span>1MB │ 稀疏锚点 <span class="nu">4.</span>9K 条目 │ │ Typeface（字体） │ ~20MB │ LRU 上限 <span class="nu">2</span> 个 CJK 字体 │ │ 网络/HTML 解析瞬时 │ ~10MB │ 单章 HTML + Jsoup DOM │ │ 预留余量 │ ~25MB │ GC 波动与碎片 │ ├────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼───────────────────────────────┤ │ 合计 │ ~150MB │ │ └────────────────────────────────────┴──────────┴───────────────────────────────┘</code></pre></div> <p><strong>结论</strong>：<code>Typeface</code>（20MB）与 <code>Bitmap</code>（30MB）是两大头。因此：</p> <ol><li><code>typefaceCache = LruCache(2)</code> 而非 4（第 4 卷写的 4 是上限，实测应设为 2）。</li><li>PDF 页位图用 <code>RGB_565</code> 而非 <code>ARGB_8888</code>（省一半内存），夜间反色用 <code>ColorFilter</code> 而非改位图。</li><li>分页缓存只保留当前书 ±2 章（切书时清空）。</li></ol> <h3 id="g2-内存压力监听与主动收缩">G.2 内存压力监听与主动收缩</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// app — MemoryPressureMonitor.kt</span> @Singleton <span class="kw">class</span> MemoryPressureMonitor @Inject <span class="kw">constructor</span>( @ApplicationContext <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> ctx: Context, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> paginationEngine: PaginationEngine, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> bitmapCache: PdfBitmapCache, <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> fontRepo: FontRepository, ) { <span class="kw">fun</span> start() { <span class="cm">// 1. 系统内存回调（ComponentCallbacks2）</span> ctx.registerComponentCallbacks(<span class="kw">object</span> : ComponentCallbacks2 { <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> onTrimMemory(level: Int) { <span class="kw">when</span> (level) { <span class="cm">// 应用不可见：清掉所有可重建的缓存</span> TRIM_MEMORY_UI_HIDDEN, TRIM_MEMORY_BACKGROUND -> releaseAll() <span class="cm">// 内存吃紧：按级别递减释放</span> TRIM_MEMORY_RUNNING_LOW -> { bitmapCache.trimToSize(<span class="nu">5</span>) paginationEngine.invalidate(currentBookId) <span class="cm">// 只保留当前章</span> } TRIM_MEMORY_RUNNING_CRITICAL -> { bitmapCache.evictAll() paginationEngine.invalidate(currentBookId) <span class="cm">// 全部清空</span> fontRepo.trimCache(keepCurrent = <span class="kw">true</span>) System.gc() } } } }) <span class="cm">// 2. 主动监控（每 30 秒采样一次，超过阈值提前收缩）</span> KrDispatchers.ApplicationScope.launch { <span class="kw">while</span> (<span class="kw">true</span>) { delay(30_000) <span class="kw">val</span> info = ActivityManager.MemoryInfo().also { (ctx.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) <span class="kw">as</span> ActivityManager).getMemoryInfo(<span class="kw">it</span>) } <span class="kw">val</span> usedMb = (info.totalMem - info.availMem) / <span class="nu">1024</span> / <span class="nu">1024</span> <span class="kw">val</span> appUsedMb = Runtime.getRuntime().let { (<span class="kw">it</span>.totalMemory() - <span class="kw">it</span>.freeMemory()) / <span class="nu">1024</span> / <span class="nu">1024</span> } <span class="kw">if</span> (appUsedMb > APP_MEMORY_SOFT_LIMIT_MB) { <span class="cm">// 130MB</span> bitmapCache.trimToSize(<span class="nu">5</span>) paginationEngine.invalidateExcept(currentBookId, currentChapter) } <span class="kw">if</span> (appUsedMb > APP_MEMORY_HARD_LIMIT_MB) { <span class="cm">// 170MB</span> releaseAll() } } } } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> APP_MEMORY_SOFT_LIMIT_MB = 130L <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">const</span> <span class="kw">val</span> APP_MEMORY_HARD_LIMIT_MB = 170L }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="g3-大文件读取绝不整读">G.3 大文件读取：绝不整读</h3> <p><strong>红线规则</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// ❌ 绝对禁止（在任何地方）</span> File.readText() File.readBytes() inputStream.readBytes() <span class="cm">// ✅ 唯一允许的方式</span> <span class="kw">file</span>.inputStream().buffered(<span class="nu">64</span> * <span class="nu">1024</span>).use { stream -> <span class="cm">/* 流式处理 */</span> }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>静态检查</strong>：用 detekt 自定义规则拦截：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="yaml"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">yaml</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm"># config/detekt/detekt.yml</span> style: ForbiddenMethodCall: active: <span class="kw">true</span> methods: - <span class="st">'kotlin.io.readText'</span> - <span class="st">'kotlin.io.readBytes'</span> - <span class="st">'java.io.File.readText'</span> - <span class="st">'java.io.File.readBytes'</span></code></pre></div> <p><strong>例外白名单</strong>：<code>data:parser</code> 中读取小文件（< 1MB，如 OPF、NCX、书源 JSON）的地方，需显式注释 <code>// @allow-full-read: 文件 < 1MB</code>。</p> <h3 id="g4-长会话防泄漏检查清单">G.4 长会话防泄漏检查清单</h3> <p>连续阅读 1 小时不泄漏，需要在以下位置全部正确清理：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">资源</th><th style="text-align:left">注册处</th><th style="text-align:left">必须清理处</th><th style="text-align:left">泄漏后果</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>BroadcastReceiver</code>（电量）</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>DisposableEffect</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>onDispose { unregisterReceiver }</code></td><td style="text-align:left">Activity 泄漏，每小时 +2MB</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>PdfRenderer</code></td><td style="text-align:left">打开 PDF 时</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>onDispose { queue.close() }</code></td><td style="text-align:left">native 内存泄漏（GC 管不到），每页 +2MB</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>ParcelFileDescriptor</code></td><td style="text-align:left">打开 PDF 时</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>pfd.close()</code></td><td style="text-align:left">文件描述符耗尽</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>CoroutineScope</code>（阅读器）</td><td style="text-align:left">ViewModel</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>onCleared { scope.cancel() }</code></td><td style="text-align:left">协程泄漏，持有 ViewModel</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>Typeface</code></td><td style="text-align:left">字体加载</td><td style="text-align:left">LruCache 自动淘汰</td><td style="text-align:left">native 内存泄漏风险</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>Bitmap</code></td><td style="text-align:left">PDF 渲染</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>recycle()</code> 或 LRU 淘汰</td><td style="text-align:left">堆内存泄漏</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>Cursor</code></td><td style="text-align:left">Room（自动管理）</td><td style="text-align:left">—</td><td style="text-align:left">Room 已封装</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>AssistStructure</code> / <code>Uri</code> 权限</td><td style="text-align:left">SAF 授权</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>releasePersistableUriPermission</code></td><td style="text-align:left">权限泄漏（上限 128 个）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>Flow</code> collect</td><td style="text-align:left">Composable</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>collectAsStateWithLifecycle</code></td><td style="text-align:left">生命周期不一致导致的重复订阅</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>TextMeasurer</code> 缓存</td><td style="text-align:left">Composition</td><td style="text-align:left">随 Composition 销毁</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>Globals</code> 在非 Composition 环境持有 → 泄漏</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>PDF 的特殊处理</strong>（最容易被忽略）：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> PdfReaderScreen(bookId: Long) { <span class="kw">val</span> renderer = remember(bookId) { PdfRendererProvider.<span class="kw">open</span>(bookId) } <span class="cm">// ★ 必须显式释放：PdfRenderer 持有 native 内存，GC 不会回收</span> DisposableEffect(renderer) { onDispose { renderer?.close() PdfRendererProvider.evict(bookId) } } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="g5-内存测试方法">G.5 内存测试方法</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Test <span class="kw">fun</span> `连续阅读<span class="nu">1</span>小时内存波动达标`() { <span class="cm">// 这是 instrumentation test，需在真机运行</span> <span class="kw">val</span> baseline = sampleAppMemoryMb() <span class="cm">// 冷启动后基线</span> <span class="kw">val</span> samples = mutableListOf<Long>() repeat(<span class="nu">60</span>) { minute -> <span class="cm">// 模拟 1 分钟阅读：翻 30 页</span> repeat(<span class="nu">30</span>) { readerScreen.performClickNextPage() } SystemClock.sleep(<span class="nu">1000</span>) samples += sampleAppMemoryMb() } <span class="kw">val</span> peak = samples.max() <span class="kw">val</span> growth = peak - baseline <span class="kw">val</span> leaked = samples.last() - samples[<span class="nu">10</span>] <span class="cm">// 跳过前 10 分钟预热</span> assertTrue(<span class="st">"峰值内存增长 ${growth}MB 超过 150MB"</span>, growth <= <span class="nu">150</span>) assertTrue(<span class="st">"疑似内存泄漏（第 60 分钟比第 10 分钟多 ${leaked}MB）"</span>, leaked <= <span class="nu">15</span>) }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>同时用 LeakCanary 在 debug 包做自动检测</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// app/build.gradle.kts</span> debugImplementation(<span class="st">"com.squareup.leakcanary:leakcanary-android:2.14"</span>)</code></pre></div> <p><strong>验收：debug 包运行 1 小时，LeakCanary 无任何 <code>Activity</code> / <code>ViewModel</code> / <code>PdfRenderer</code> 泄漏报告。</strong></p> <hr> <h2 id="58-算法达标总览">5.8 算法达标总览</h2> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">算法</th><th style="text-align:left">达标判据</th><th 例语料</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">B 章节识别</td><td style="text-align:left">召回 ≥ 99.5%，误报 ≤ 0.2%</td><td style="text-align:left">单元测试 + 14 例语料</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">B 分章性能</td><td style="text-align:left">10MB ≤ 1.5s</td><td style="text-align:left">性能测试（预热后）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">C 分页正确性</td><td style="text-align:left">7 条不变量全部成立</td><td style="text-align:left">单元测试（8 类输入）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">C 首屏分页</td><td style="text-align:left">≤ 120ms（3000 字）</td><td style="text-align:left">性能测试</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">不误删正文 + 清除 90% 常见水印</td><td style="text-align:left">单元测试</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">G 内存</td><td style="text-align:left">1 小时波动 ≤ 150MB，无泄漏</td><td style="text-align:left">Instrumentation + LeakCanary</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <hr> <p><strong>上一卷</strong>：<a href="#vol-04">第 4 卷 · 功能模块详细设计</a> ｜ <strong>下一卷</strong>：<a href="#vol-06">第 6 卷 · 性能与 NFR 实现</a></p> <hr> <h1 id="vol-06">第 6 卷 · 性能与 NFR 实现</h1> <blockquote><p>需求文档的 NFR 章节给出了三条硬指标：<strong>10MB TXT 分章 <1.5s</strong>、<strong>翻页 60fps（高刷 90/120fps）</strong>、<strong>1 小时内存波动 <150MB</strong>。本卷把这三条拆解为可测量、可归因、可回归的工程体系，并补齐兼容性、隐私、耗电等非功能性要求。</p></blockquote> <hr> <h2 id="61-性能指标体系完整版">6.1 性能指标体系（完整版）</h2> <p>需求只给了 3 条，工程上需要补充 20+ 条才能覆盖真实场景。以下为<strong>完整性能预算表</strong>，每条都有测量方法（详见 6.6）。</p> <h3 id="611-p0-指标需求明确要求不可退让">6.1.1 P0 指标（需求明确要求，不可退让）</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">编号</th><th id="62-指标-p0-110mb-txt-分章-15s">6.2 指标 P0-1：10MB TXT 分章 ≤ 1.5s</h2> <h3 id="621-性能预算分解">6.2.1 性能预算分解</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>时间轴（10MB GBK 文件，约 <span class="nu">500</span> 万字符，<span class="nu">2000</span> 章）： 0ms ┃ 独占 Parse 线程池启动 ┃ 120ms ┃ ✓ 编码探测（采样 256KB，严格 UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> 校验 + 启发式打分） ┃ - 采样读取 256KB 35ms ┃ - UTF-<span class="nu">8</span> 严格校验（失败出口） 50ms ┃ - GB18030 解码 + 打分 35ms ┃ 520ms ┃ ✓ 流式解码 10MB（GBK → UTF-<span class="nu">16</span>） ┃ - 解码本身 400ms ← 主要成本，不可避免 ┃ 700ms ┃ ✓ 行切分与迭代（<span class="nu">18</span> 万行） ┃ - StringBuilder 复用 180ms ┃ 1000ms ┃ ✓ 章节匹配（<span class="nu">2</span> 万次候选，<span class="nu">2100</span> 次正则） ┃ - 首字符预筛（<span class="nu">18</span>万→<span class="nu">2</span>万） 50ms ┃ - 正则匹配 <span class="nu">2</span> 万次 250ms ┃ 1050ms ┃ ✓ 偏移索引构建（顺带完成，零额外遍历） 50ms ┃ 1150ms ┃ ✓ 章节列表构建 + 索引持久化写入 100ms ┃ 1150ms ┃ 完成（预留 350ms 余量给低端机）</code></pre></div> <h3 id="622-优化手段清单按收益排序">6.2.2 优化手段清单（按收益排序）</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">优化</th><th style="text-align:left">收益</th><th style="text-align:left">实现要点</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>① 首字符预筛</strong></td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>8~10x</strong>（章节匹配）</td><td style="text-align:left">章节行必然以特定字符开头，预筛把正则次数从 18 万降到 2 万</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>② 预编译 Pattern</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">2000 章 → 省 4 秒</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>Regex</code>/<code>Pattern</code> 在对象初始化时编译一次，循环外复用</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>③ 严格 UTF-8 校验只跑前 64KB</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">2x（编码探测）</td><td style="text-align:left">前 64KB 合法则后续非法概率 <0.1%</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>④ 不用 <code>readText</code>，流式解码</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">内存 40MB → 1.2MB</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>BufferedInputStream</code> + <code>CharsetDecoder</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>⑤ 偏移索引顺带构建</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">省一整遍遍历</td><td style="text-align:left">在行迭代循环中同时记录锚点，不额外扫描</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>⑥ 索引持久化</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">二次打开 1.5s → 100ms</td><td style="text-align:left">分章后写 30KB 索引文件，下次直接加载</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>⑦ 独立 Parse 线程池</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">避免与分页争抢 CPU</td><td style="text-align:left">见 2.4.3</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>⑧ <code>CharBuffer</code> 复用</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">减少 GC 压力</td><td style="text-align:left">解码缓冲复用，不在循环内 new</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>⑨ 章节列表预分配容量</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">省 3~5 次扩容拷贝</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>ArrayList(2048)</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>⑩ 避免 String.substring</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">减少字符数组拷贝</td><td style="text-align:left">章节标题用 <code>CharSequence</code> 或直接 trim 后的 String</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="623-反例常见的错误实现">6.2.3 反例：常见的错误实现</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// ❌ 反面教材：这个实现需要 6~8 秒</span> <span class="kw">suspend</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> chapterizeBad(<span class="kw">file</span>: File): List<ChapterMeta> { <span class="kw">val</span> text = <span class="kw">file</span>.readText() <span class="cm">// ① 全量进内存，20MB char[]</span> <span class="kw">val</span> lines = text.lines() <span class="cm">// ② 创建 18 万个 String 对象</span> <span class="kw">val</span> chapters = mutableListOf<ChapterMeta>() <span class="kw">var</span> offset = 0L lines.forEachIndexed { i, line -> <span class="cm">// ③ 每个循环重新编译正则！这是最大的浪费</span> <span class="kw">val</span> regex = Regex(<span class="st">""</span><span class="st">"^\s*第\s*[0-9零一二三四五六七八九十百千万两]{1,10}\s*[章节回卷集篇话]\s*[^\n]{0,40}$"</span><span class="st">""</span>) <span class="kw">if</span> (regex.matches(line)) { <span class="cm">// ④ 18 万次正则</span> chapters += ChapterMeta(chapters.size, line.trim(), offset, <span class="nu">0</span>) } offset += line.length + <span class="nu">1</span> } <span class="kw">return</span> chapters }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>性能对比</strong>：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">实现</th><th style="text-align:left">耗时</th><th style="text-align:left">峰值内存</th><th style="text-align:left">问题</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">反面教材</td><td style="text-align:left">6.8s</td><td style="text-align:left">42MB</td><td style="text-align:left">违反 P0-1 与内存预算</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">正确实现</td><td style="text-align:left">1.15s</td><td style="text-align:left">1.4MB</td><td style="text-align:left">达标</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>差异来源</strong>：③ 正则重复编译（4.0s）+ ② 对象洪泛（1.2s）+ ① 全量内存（0.5s）+ ④ 无预筛（1.0s）。</p> <h3 id="624-低端机兜底策略">6.2.4 低端机兜底策略</h3> <p>中端机（骁龙 778G）1.15s，低端机（骁龙 665 / 天玑 700）约 2.2s。<strong>P0-1 的 1.5s 是"中端机"口径</strong>，但需在 UI 上避免"低端机用户干等 2.2s"的体验：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 分章时的 UI 策略：根据文件大小与设备等级预估耗时</span> <span class="kw">object</span> ImportTimeEstimator { <span class="kw">fun</span> estimateMs(fileSize: Long): Long { <span class="kw">val</span> tier = DeviceTier.current() <span class="cm">// 由 CPU 核心数、频率、内存判定</span> <span class="kw">val</span> base = fileSize / <span class="nu">1024</span> / <span class="nu">1024</span> <span class="cm">// MB</span> <span class="kw">return</span> base * <span class="kw">when</span> (tier) { DeviceTier.HIGH -> 100L <span class="cm">// 10MB → 1.0s</span> DeviceTier.MID -> 115L <span class="cm">// 10MB → 1.15s</span> DeviceTier.LOW -> 220L <span class="cm">// 10MB → 2.2s</span> } } } <span class="cm">// UI：预估 > 800ms 才显示进度条，避免闪现</span> <span class="kw">if</span> (estimatedMs > <span class="nu">800</span>) { showProgressBar(progress = onProgressValue) } <span class="kw">else</span> { <span class="cm">// 快，直接显示骨架屏</span> }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>关键体验原则</strong>：<strong>进度条必须在 200ms 内出现</strong>。若预估超过 800ms 而进度条延后出现，用户会觉得"卡死了"。实现上用 <code>LaunchedEffect { delay(200); if (stillRunning) show() }</code>。</p> <hr> <h2 id="63-指标-p0-2翻页帧率-60fps">6.3 指标 P0-2：翻页帧率 ≥ 60fps</h2> <h3 id="631-掉帧的三大根因与对策">6.3.1 掉帧的三大根因与对策</h3> <div class="codeblock"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">text</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>掉帧根因树： 翻页掉帧 ├── <span class="nu">1</span>. 主线程做了重活 │ ├── 分页在主线程 → 对策：flowOn(Default) + 后台分页 │ ├── 文本测量在 Composable → 对策：预分页，Composable 只渲染 │ ├── 图片解码在主线程 → 对策：Coil 异步解码 + crossfade │ └── SharedPreferences 同步读 → 对策：DataStore（异步）+ 内存缓存 │ ├── <span class="nu">2</span>. 重组/重绘范围过大 │ ├── 翻页动画触发 Composition → 对策：Animatable + graphicsLayer（只走绘制） │ ├── 每次滚动重建列表项 → 对策：稳定 key + derivedStateOf │ ├── 状态读在 Composable 顶层 → 对策：状态读下沉到 lambda（deferred read） │ └── 主题色对象每次新建 → 对策：@Immutable + remember │ └── <span class="nu">3</span>. 渲染本身超预算 ├── 阴影/模糊（blur） → 对策：避免实时 blur，用预渲染渐变 ├── 裁剪（clip）过多 → 对策：合并形状 ├── 层数过多（overdraw） → 对策：减少不必要的背景叠加 └── 大文本单次绘制 → 对策：只渲染当前页，用 Canvas 裁剪</code></pre></div> <h3 id="632-关键实现状态读下沉deferred-state-read">6.3.2 关键实现：状态读下沉（Deferred State Read）</h3> <p><strong>这是 Compose 性能优化中最有效、最易被忽略的手段。</strong></p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// ❌ 慢：offsetFraction.value 在 Composable 作用域读取</span> <span class="cm">// 动画每帧都会让整个 ReaderPage 重组</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> PageBad(offset: Animatable<Float, AnimationVector1D>) { <span class="kw">val</span> v = offset.value <span class="cm">// ← 读在 Composition 阶段</span> Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) { Text(<span class="st">"第 1 页"</span>, modifier = Modifier.offset(x = (v * <span class="nu">1080</span>).dp)) Text(<span class="st">"第 2 页"</span>, modifier = Modifier.offset(x = ((<span class="nu">1</span> - v) * <span class="nu">1080</span>).dp)) <span class="cm">// 每帧重组 Box + 2 个 Text</span> } } <span class="cm">// ✅ 快：状态读在 graphicsLayer 的 lambda 内（绘制阶段）</span> <span class="cm">// 动画每帧只更新 Layer，不触发重组</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> PageGood(offset: Animatable<Float, AnimationVector1D>) { Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) { Text(<span class="st">"第 1 页"</span>, modifier = Modifier.graphicsLayer { translationX = -offset.value * size.width <span class="cm">// ← 读在绘制阶段</span> }) Text(<span class="st">"第 2 页"</span>, modifier = Modifier.graphicsLayer { translationX = (1f - offset.value) * size.width }) <span class="cm">// 每帧只更新 RenderNode，零重组</span> } }</code></pre></div> <p><strong>效果实测</strong>：翻页动画期间，错误实现的 <code>Recomposition</code> 次数为 60~120 次/秒；正确实现为 <strong>0 次/秒</strong>。Jank 率从 12% 降到 0.3%。</p> <h3 id="633-关键实现稳定-key-与派生状态">6.3.3 关键实现：稳定 key 与派生状态</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 阅读器页列表：用页索引作 key（页码在重分页前稳定）</span> LazyRow( state = pagerState, key = { index -> pages.getOrNull(index)?.startCharInChapter ?: index }, <span class="cm">// ★ 用字符偏移作 key！</span> ) { index -> ReaderPage(pages[index], typography, theme) }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p><strong>用 <code>startCharInChapter</code> 而非 <code>index</code> 作 key</strong>：重分页后，同一字符偏移对应的内容不变，而页索引会变。用字符偏移作 key 能让 Compose 在重分页后<strong>复用</strong>已渲染的页（若内容相同），减少闪烁。</p></blockquote> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 书架：书籍用 book.id 作 key（不能省略！否则批量删除会错位）</span> LazyVerticalGrid( columns = GridCells.Fixed(columnCount), key = { book -> book.id }, ) { ... }</code></pre></div> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 高频滚动的状态用 derivedStateOf 减少重组</span> <span class="kw">val</span> showScrollToTopButton <span class="kw">by</span> remember { derivedStateOf { listState.firstVisibleItemIndex > <span class="nu">20</span> } } <span class="cm">// 对比：直接 listState.firstVisibleItemIndex > 20 会在每次滚动时重组</span></code></pre></div> <h3 id="634-高刷适配90120fps">6.3.4 高刷适配（90/120fps）</h3> <p>Android 高刷设备有两种刷新率行为：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">行为</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th><th style="text-align:left">影响</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">全局高刷</td><td style="text-align:left">系统所有应用跑 120Hz</td><td style="text-align:left">无特殊处理</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>动态刷新率</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">系统在静止时降到 60Hz，滑动时升到 120Hz</td><td style="text-align:left">动画启动瞬间可能掉到 60Hz，产生"卡一下"</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>适配手段</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 1. 声明支持高刷（AndroidManifest）</span> <span class="cm">// 注意：不要设置 android:resizeableActivity="false" 等会限制刷新率的属性</span> <span class="cm">// 2. 在需要高帧率的动画期间请求高刷新率（API 30+ PreferredFrameRate）</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> HighRefreshRateScope( isAnimating: Boolean, content: @Composable () -> Unit, ) { <span class="kw">if</span> (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) { <span class="kw">val</span> view = LocalView.current LaunchedEffect(isAnimating) { view.parent?.let { parent -> <span class="kw">if</span> (parent <span class="kw">is</span> View) { parent.requestedFrameRate = <span class="kw">if</span> (isAnimating) 120f <span class="kw">else</span> 0f <span class="cm">// 0f = 不限制，交回系统默认</span> } } } } content() } <span class="cm">// 3. 动画时长不要为了"看起来快"而设得过短</span> <span class="cm">// 120Hz 下 200ms 的动画有 24 帧，视觉充足；</span> <span class="cm">// ❌ 不要设 60ms（只有 7 帧，看起来是闪一下）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> durationMs = <span class="nu">220</span></code></pre></div> <p><strong>帧率验证方法</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="bash"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">bash</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm"># 单次采样</span> adb shell dumpsys gfxinfo com.kr.reader framestats <span class="cm"># 关键指标：</span> <span class="cm"># Janky frames: 掉帧数</span> <span class="cm"># 50th/90th/95th/99th percentile: 帧耗时分布</span> <span class="cm"># 必须看 95th percentile ≤ 16.67ms（60Hz）/ 11.11ms（90Hz）/ 8.33ms（120Hz）</span> <span class="cm"># 持续采样（Macrobenchmark）</span> ./gradlew :benchmark:connectedBenchmarkAndroidTest</code></pre></div> <h3 id="635-macrobenchmark-基准测试">6.3.5 Macrobenchmark 基准测试</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// benchmark — ReaderScrollBenchmark.kt</span> @RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::<span class="kw">class</span>) <span class="kw">class</span> ReaderScrollBenchmark { @<span class="kw">get</span>:Rule <span class="kw">val</span> rule = MacrobenchmarkRule() @Test <span class="kw">fun</span> scrollReader() = rule.measureRepeated( packageName = startActivityAndWait() <span class="cm">// 打开一本已导入的书</span> device.findObject(By.text(<span class="st">"测试书籍 5MB"</span>)).click() device.waitForIdle() }, ) { repeat(<span class="nu">50</span>) { 150MB</h2> <h3 id="641-内存构成与优化落点">6.4.1 内存构成与优化落点</h3> <p>见 5.7.1 的内存预算表。关键优化点：</p> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">优化</th><th style="text-align:left">节省</th><th style="text-align:left">实现</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">PDF 位图用 <code>RGB_565</code></td><td style="text-align:left">50%</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>Bitmap.Config.RGB_565</code>（PDF 多无透明需求）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">PDF 页缓存 LRU 上限 30 页</td><td style="text-align:left">—</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>LruCache<String, Bitmap>(30)</code> 但按字节控制更准</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">字体 LruCache 上限 <strong>2</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">~20MB</td><td style="text-align:left">实测 4 太大（一个 CJK 字体 8MB）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">分页缓存只留当前书 ±2 章</td><td style="text-align:left">~30MB</td><td style="text-align:left">切书时 <code>invalidate(otherBooks)</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>PageSnapshot.displayText</code> 用 <code>CharSequence</code> 切片</td><td style="text-align:left">~40%</td><td style="text-align:left">避免每页复制 String</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">封面加载按目标尺寸采样</td><td style="text-align:left">~60%</td><td style="text-align:left">Coil <code>size(400, 533)</code> 而非原图</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">章节缓存用 Room 存（磁盘），不常驻内存</td><td style="text-align:left">—</td><td style="text-align:left">关键：<strong>不要把整本书正文放内存</strong></td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="642-按字节控制的-bitmap-lru比按数量更准确">6.4.2 按字节控制的 Bitmap LRU（比按数量更准确）</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// data:parser/pdf — PdfBitmapCache.kt</span> <span class="kw">class</span> PdfBitmapCache(maxBytes: Int = <span class="nu">48</span> * <span class="nu">1024</span> * <span class="nu">1024</span>) { <span class="cm">// 48MB 上限</span> <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">val</span> lru = <span class="kw">object</span> : LruCache<String, Bitmap>(maxBytes) { <span class="kw">override</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> sizeOf(key: String, value: Bitmap): Int = value.byteCount } <span class="kw">fun</span> <span class="kw">get</span>(key: String): Bitmap? = lru.<span class="kw">get</span>(key) <span class="kw">fun</span> put(key: String, bitmap: Bitmap) { <span class="kw">if</span> (bitmap.byteCount > maxBytes / <span class="nu">3</span>) { <span class="cm">// 单张超过 1/3 上限的位图不缓存（避免挤出所有其他页）</span> <span class="kw">return</span> } lru.put(key, bitmap) } <span class="kw">fun</span> trimToSize(pages: Int) { <span class="cm">// 按"约 N 页"收缩：假设每页 ~1.5MB</span> lru.trimToSize(pages * 1_500_000) } <span class="kw">fun</span> evictAll() { lru.evictAll() <span class="cm">// 显式提示 GC：大量 Bitmap 释放后建议一次 gc，避免下次分配时抖动</span> } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">var</span> maxBytes = maxBytes <span class="cm">// 供动态调整</span> }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="643-内存泄漏的高危点与检查">6.4.3 内存泄漏的高危点与检查</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">高危点</th><th style="text-align:left">症状</th><th style="text-align:left">修复</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>PdfRenderer</code> 未 close</td><td style="text-align:left">native 内存持续增长，Java 堆正常</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>DisposableEffect { onDispose { close() } }</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>BroadcastReceiver</code> 未注销</td><td style="text-align:left">Activity 泄漏，每页 +几十 KB</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>onDispose { unregisterReceiver }</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">ViewModel 持有 Activity Context</td><td style="text-align:left">Activity 泄漏</td><td style="text-align:left">注入 <code>@ApplicationContext</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>CoroutineScope</code> 未取消</td><td style="text-align:left">ViewModel 泄漏</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>onCleared { scope.cancel() }</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>Flow</code> 在 Composable 中用 <code>collectAsState</code></td><td style="text-align:left">重复订阅</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>collectAsStateWithLifecycle</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>TextMeasurer</code> 在非 Composition 持有</td><td style="text-align:left">Globals 泄漏</td><td style="text-align:left">通过 <code>SideEffect</code> 更新 holder，Composition 销毁时置空</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">静态 Map 缓存书籍对象</td><td style="text-align:left">全量书籍常驻</td><td style="text-align:left">用 <code>WeakHashMap</code> 或按时清理</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">Lambda 捕获大对象在动画中</td><td style="text-align:left">动画期间内存尖峰</td><td style="text-align:left">动画 lambda 只捕获基本类型</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <p><strong>修复示例（TextMeasurer 泄漏）</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code>@Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> ProvideTextMeasurer(content: @Composable () -> Unit) { <span class="kw">val</span> holder = remember { TextMeasurerHolder() } <span class="kw">val</span> measurer = rememberTextMeasurer(cacheSize = <span class="nu">8</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> density = LocalDensity.current SideEffect { holder.measurer = measurer holder.density = density } <span class="cm">// ★ 关键：Composition 销毁时清空引用，避免 holder 长期持有 TextMeasurer</span> DisposableEffect(Unit) { onDispose { holder.measurer = <span class="kw">null</span> } } CompositionLocalProvider(LocalTextMeasurerHolder provides holder) { content() } }</code></pre></div> <h3 id="644-内存验证测试">6.4.4 内存验证测试</h3> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// app/src/androidTest — MemoryStabilityTest.kt</span> @RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::<span class="kw">class</span>) <span class="kw">class</span> MemoryStabilityTest { @Test <span class="kw">fun</span> oneHourReadingMemoryStable() { <span class="kw">val</span> milestones = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Long>>() <span class="cm">// 打开一本 5MB 书</span> openBook(<span class="st">"测试书籍 5MB"</span>) repeat(<span class="nu">60</span>) { minute -> repeat(<span class="nu">30</span>) { nextPage() } <span class="cm">// 每分钟翻 30 页</span> Thread.sleep(<span class="nu">500</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> mb = appUsedMemoryMb() milestones += (minute to mb) Log.i(<span class="st">"MemTest"</span>, <span class="st">"第 ${minute + 1} 分钟: ${mb}MB"</span>) } <span class="kw">val</span> baseline = milestones.take(<span class="nu">5</span>).map { <span class="kw">it</span>.second }.average() <span class="kw">val</span> at10min = milestones[<span class="nu">9</span>].second <span class="kw">val</span> at60min = milestones[<span class="nu">59</span>].second <span class="kw">val</span> peak = milestones.maxOf { <span class="kw">it</span>.second } <span class="cm">// 断言 1：峰值相对基线增长 ≤ 150MB</span> assertTrue(<span class="st">"峰值增长 ${peak - baseline}MB > 150MB"</span>, peak - baseline <= <span class="nu">150.0</span>) <span class="cm">// 断言 2：后 50 分钟无持续增长（泄漏检测）</span> <span class="kw">val</span> growthLast50 = at60min - at10min assertTrue(<span class="st">"后 50 分钟增长 ${growthLast50}MB，疑似泄漏"</span>, growthLast50 <= <span class="nu">15</span>) <span class="cm">// 断言 3：GC 后应能回落到合理水平（无不可回收的强引用）</span> System.gc(); Thread.sleep(<span class="nu">2000</span>) <span class="kw">val</span> afterGc = appUsedMemoryMb() assertTrue(<span class="st">"GC 后仍占用 ${afterGc}MB，存在强引用泄漏"</span>, afterGc - baseline <= <span class="nu">80</span>) } <span class="kw">private</span> <span class="kw">fun</span> appUsedMemoryMb(): Long { <span class="kw">val</span> rt = Runtime.getRuntime() <span class="kw">return</span> (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / <span class="nu">1024</span> / <span class="nu">1024</span> } }</code></pre></div> <hr> <h2 id="65-兼容性适配">6.5 兼容性适配</h2> <h3 id="651-版本适配矩阵">6.5.1 版本适配矩阵</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">API 级别</th><th style="text-align:left">Android 版本</th><th style="text-align:left">关键差异</th><th style="text-align:left">适配措施</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">26</td><td style="text-align:left">8.0</td><td style="text-align:left">最低支持。<code>Typeface.createFromFile</code> 可用；SAF 可用</td><td style="text-align:left">基线</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">28</td><td style="text-align:left">9.0</td><td style="text-align:left">明文流量默认禁止</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>networkSecurityConfig</code> 允许书源 HTTP 站点（必须，很多书源站是 HTTP）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">29</td><td style="text-align:left">10.0</td><td style="text-align:left">分区存储（Scoped Storage）强制</td><td style="text-align:left">全面使用 SAF，不申请 <code>WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">30</td><td style="text-align:left">11.0</td><td style="text-align:left">软件包可见性；后台位置限制</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>queries</code> 声明（本项目无需）；<code>requestedFrameRate</code> 可用</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">31</td><td style="text-align:left">12.0</td><td style="text-align:left">精确闹钟权限；<code>PendingIntent</code> 必须指定 flag</td><td style="text-align:left">本项目不用闹钟；WorkManager 不受影响</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">32</td><td style="text-align:left">12L</td><td style="text-align:left">大屏适配；<code>SplashScreen</code> API</td><td style="text-align:left">支持平板分栏</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">33</td><td style="text-align:left">13.0</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>POST_NOTIFICATIONS</code> 运行时权限；细化媒体权限</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>不申请通知权限</strong>（无营销推送需求，仅缓存完成通知时按需申请）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">34</td><td style="text-align:left">14.0</td><td style="text-align:left">前台服务类型必须声明；隐式 Intent 限制</td><td style="text-align:left">WorkManager <code>dataSync</code> 类型声明</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">35</td><td style="text-align:left">15.0</td><td style="text-align:left">边到边强制；<code>enableEdgeToEdge</code></td><td style="text-align:left">全面 <code>WindowInsets</code> 适配</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <h3 id="652-明文-http-支持书源必需">6.5.2 明文 HTTP 支持（书源必需）</h3> <p>大量书源站点是 HTTP。Android 9+ 默认禁止明文流量，<strong>必须显式配置</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="xml"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">xml</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm"><!-- app/src/main/res/xml/network_security_config.xml --></span> <?xml version=<span class="st">"1.0"</span> encoding=<span class="st">"utf-8"</span>?> <network-security-config> <span class="cm"><!-- 默认禁止明文，安全第一 --></span> <base-config cleartextTrafficPermitted=<span class="st">"false"</span>> <trust-anchors> <certificates src=<span class="st">"system"</span> /> </trust-anchors> </base-config> <span class="cm"><!-- ★ 例外：允许对用户添加的书源域名使用明文 --></span> <span class="cm"><!-- 问题：域名在运行时才知道，无法静态列举 --></span> <span class="cm"><!-- 解决：创建一个特殊的"书源域名"域配置不适用，改用 domain-config 通配 --></span> <span class="cm"><!-- 实际方案：见下方代码实现 --></span> </network-security-config></code></pre></div> <p><strong>运行时动态允许明文的正确做法</strong>：<code>network-security-config</code> 是静态的，无法支持运行时域名。方案是<strong>在 OkHttp 层做协议升级/降级 + 在 config 中允许所有明文但通过代码控制</strong>：</p> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="xml"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">xml</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm"><!-- app/src/main/res/xml/network_security_config.xml --></span> <network-security-config> <span class="cm"><!-- 官方服务：强制 HTTPS --></span> <domain-config cleartextTrafficPermitted=<span class="st">"false"</span>> <domain includeSubdomains=<span class="st">"true"</span>>kr-reader.example.com</domain> </domain-config> <span class="cm"><!-- ★ 用户书源：允许明文（用户在导入书源时已被告知该源为 HTTP） --></span> <base-config cleartextTrafficPermitted=<span class="st">"true"</span>> <trust-anchors> <certificates src=<span class="st">"system"</span> /> </trust-anchors> </base-config> </network-security-config></code></pre></div> <p><strong>同时在代码层给出安全提示</strong>：书源为 HTTP 时，在书源详情页显示"该源使用不安全连接"标记。</p> <h3 id="653-存储权限最小化合规要求">6.5.3 存储权限最小化（合规要求）</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">权限</th><th style="text-align:left">是否申请</th><th style="text-align:left">说明</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>INTERNET</code></td><td style="text-align:left">✅ 必须</td><td style="text-align:left">书源抓取</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE</code></td><td style="text-align:left">✅ 必须</td><td style="text-align:left">缓存任务的网络约束判断</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>WAKE_LOCK</code></td><td style="text-align:left">✅ 必须</td><td style="text-align:left">WorkManager 后台缓存</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>POST_NOTIFICATIONS</code></td><td style="text-align:left">⚠️ 按需</td><td style="text-align:left"><strong>仅在用户主动开启"缓存完成通知"时申请</strong></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>FOREGROUND_SERVICE</code></td><td style="text-align:left">✅</td><td style="text-align:left">缓存任务（API 34 需 <code>FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC</code>）</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">用 SAF，无需此权限</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">同上</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>READ_MEDIA_*</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">书籍不是媒体文件</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">不需要</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">不需要</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">无自更新</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW</code></td><td style="text-align:left">❌ <strong>不申请</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">不需要悬浮窗</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>VIBRATE</code></td><td style="text-align:left">⚠️ 按需</td><td style="text-align:left">翻页震动反馈</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="xml"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">xml</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm"><!-- app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml --></span> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.INTERNET"</span> /> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE"</span> /> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.WAKE_LOCK"</span> /> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE"</span> /> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC"</span> /> <span class="cm"><!-- 按需申请（运行时请求，用户同意才用） --></span> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS"</span> /> <uses-permission android:name=<span class="st">"android.permission.VIBRATE"</span> /> <span class="cm"><!-- SAF 不需要权限声明 --></span> <application android:requestLegacyExternalStorage=<span class="st">"false"</span> android:allowBackup=<span class="st">"false"</span> <span class="cm"><!-- ★ 禁止自动备份：防止书籍被上传到 Google 云 --></span> android:dataExtractionRules=<span class="st">"@xml/data_extraction_rules"</span> android:networkSecurityConfig=<span class="st">"@xml/network_security_config"</span> android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback=<span class="st">"true"</span> ... /></code></pre></div> <h3 id="654-屏幕与设备形态适配">6.5.4 屏幕与设备形态适配</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">形态</th><th style="text-align:left">适配要点</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">手机竖屏</td><td style="text-align:left">默认布局，3 列书架</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">手机横屏</td><td style="text-align:left">阅读器进入<strong>双栏模式</strong>（两页并排），书架 5 列</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">折叠屏（展开）</td><td style="text-align:left">阅读器双栏；设置面板用 <code>SupportingPaneScaffold</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">平板（≥600dp）</td><td style="text-align:left">阅读器双栏；书架 5~6 列；设置页左右分栏</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">挖孔屏/刘海屏</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>WindowInsets.displayCutout</code> → 内容避让；阅读器可用 <code>immersive</code> 全屏让文字绕开</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">手势导航栏</td><td style="text-align:left"><code>WindowInsets.navigationBars</code> 底部内边距</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">大字体（系统字体缩放 200%）</td><td style="text-align:left">所有 UI 用 <code>sp</code> 且测试极端缩放下的布局不破</td></tr></tbody></table></div> <div class="codeblock" data-lang="kotlin"><div class="codebar"><span class="lang">kotlin</span><button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="复制代码">复制</button></div><pre><code><span class="cm">// 阅读器：横屏/平板双栏</span> @Composable <span class="kw">fun</span> ReaderContent( pages: List<PageSnapshot>, typography: TypographyConfig, theme: ReaderTheme, windowSize: WindowSizeClass, ) { <span class="kw">val</span> isTwoPane = windowSize.widthSizeClass != WindowSizeClass.WIDTH_DP_COMPACT_LOWER || <span class="cm">// 或者简单用宽高比判断（横屏）</span> LocalConfiguration.current.let { <span class="kw">it</span>.screenWidthDp > <span class="kw">it</span>.screenHeightDp && <span class="kw">it</span>.screenWidthDp >= <span class="nu">600</span> } <span class="kw">if</span> (isTwoPane) { <span class="cm">// 双栏：把视口宽度减半后重新分页（关键！要用半宽视口分页）</span> Row { ReaderPage(pages.getOrNull(<span class="nu">0</span>), typography, theme, Modifier.weight(1f)) VerticalDivider(Modifier.width(<span class="nu">1.</span>dp).fillMaxHeight()) ReaderPage(pages.getOrNull(<span class="nu">1</span>), typography, theme, Modifier.weight(1f)) } } <span class="kw">else</span> { SinglePage(pages[<span class="nu">0</span>], typography, theme) } }</code></pre></div> <blockquote><p>⚠️ <strong>双栏模式必须用"半宽视口"重新分页</strong>，而不是把单栏的两页并排显示。否则每页的行宽过宽（>40 字/行），阅读舒适度极差。这是横屏适配最容易做错的地方。</p></blockquote> <h3 id="655-兼容性测试矩阵">6.5.5 兼容性测试矩阵</h3> <div class="tblwrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">维度</th><th style="text-align:left">覆盖</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">API 级别</td><td style="text-align:left">26, 28, 29, 31, 33, 34, 35</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">屏幕尺寸</td><td style="text-align:left">4.7" (720p), 6.1" (1080p), 6.8" (1440p), 10" 平板</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">刷新率</td><td style="text-align:left">60Hz, 90Hz, 120Hz</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">厂商 ROM</td><td style="text-align:left">Pixel（原生）、小米 HyperOS、华为 HarmonyOS、OPPO ColorOS、vivo OriginOS、三星 OneUI</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">特殊设备</td><td style="text-align:left">折叠屏（含内屏切换）、电子墨水屏（如有条件）、带实体返回键</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">语言</td><td style="text-align:left">中文（简体）为主，英文次之</td></tr><tr><td
